# 精选 Grok Bot 列表 [![Awesome](https://awesome.re/badge.svg)](https://awesome.re)

[English](README.md) | 中文

> 精选最佳 [Grok Bots](https://x.ai/bot) 列表 — 基于 xAI Grok 平台构建的 AI 助手。

[Grok Bots](https://x.ai/bot) 是 xAI Grok 平台上的 AI 助手。每个 Bot 都是一个专属智能体，拥有独立的配置、集成服务与自主例程（Routines）——专注于高效完成特定任务，涵盖从收件箱管理到代码审查的丰富场景。本项目汇聚了社区与官方市场的优质 Grok Bot。

## 目录

- [官方出品](#官方出品)
- [销售](#销售)
- [市场营销](#市场营销)
- [设计](#设计)
- [工程研发](#工程研发)
- [个人生活](#个人生活)
- [招聘与人事](#招聘与人事)
- [企业运营](#企业运营)
- [产品管理](#产品管理)

## 官方出品

- **[Account Research Desk](https://x.ai/bot/O3iqVd_ZrdRtrDJpxcKss)** by @akbaliga96 — Researches the companies you sell to and writes your pre-call brief and account plan. Works from the public web and the notes you paste, and never sends without you.
- **[Ad Spend Watch](https://x.ai/bot/MK5zStpj_As8CsRwg3r6w)** by @cruzmiguel000 — Watches your ad spend and performance and flags what's breaking before it burns budget. Works from a pasted export, and never pauses a campaign without your yes.
- **[AI Search Visibility](https://x.ai/bot/BFiw9Y7BzTQ-3jFBAro1X)** by @adamta — Checks whether AI assistants and Google recommend you, and who they name instead. Starts from a handful of questions your buyers actually ask.
- **[Alfred](https://x.ai/bot/p7Gh6HIrfv4AGzIow6-9X)** by @heyrobinai — Designs, audits, and governs your Grok Bot organization so it matches real company outcomes, with clear human owners and no duplicate jobs. Recommends the smallest useful structure, defaults new bots to zero, and never creates or changes anything without your exact yes.
- **[Apple Search Ads Review](https://x.ai/bot/gadc3bVOsg9iIwmzAGRve)** — Reviews your Apple Search Ads spend against your cost per install target. Drafts the keyword, bid, and budget changes, and never touches your account.
- **[Call Follow-Ups](https://x.ai/bot/estQ8HWdKqhheDNmOGjcy)** by @danielbrill_ — Turns a call transcript into a recap, every promise made, and a dated follow-up list. Works from a paste or an upload, and never sends a follow-up without you.
- **[Chief Health Officer](https://x.ai/bot/PwWb6fJXpeG857af6tEYi)** by @AJA_Cortes — You're the Chief Health Officer. Every day, ask if training happened. If it did not, rewrite the next session so the week still holds. Read whatever workout app, nutrition app, wearable, or lab source is connected. Do not invent numbers. Never diagnose, prescribe, or post.
- **[Clip Bot](https://x.ai/bot/-L1yFJ5mtwPgn3O_iYUo_)** by @ThisWeeknAI — Cuts social-ready podcast highlights from YouTube: captioned 16:9 clips with karaoke captions and a source card, then drops the file on your computer.
- **[Company Docs Q&A](https://x.ai/bot/GVAnpUah1K6PzJtG9EuG9)** by @akbaliga96 — Answers product and how-to questions from live docs first, then connected knowledge sources, and always cites sources. Built for anyone who wants a trustworthy internal knowledge bot.
- **[Competitor Watching](https://x.ai/bot/aw0Zj54sIsAK7vMnajdz0)** by @scheemunai — Snapshots you vs 3-8 competitors (pricing, changelog, features, jobs, messaging). Weekly diffs.
- **[Cooper](https://x.ai/bot/8hhdYqfvRzigWstUtcmBZ)** by @TommyHansenTA — News agent for AI, tech, venture capital, and business. Delivers a tight daily Slack briefing of top stories at 8am, plus weekday mid-day competitor alerts only when something truly clears the bar.
- **[Credit Card Max](https://x.ai/bot/q4u8YgzGQqCAOUZcg0Lgt)** by @trevin — Advises which credit card to use for a given purchase to maximize points, cash back, and perks. Tracks cards, unused benefits, and misrouted recurring charges.
- **[Critiquito](https://x.ai/bot/NqdH9qGvrq-yWRaXhJGM-)** by @mamuso — A product design critic that reviews interface screenshots from the person who has to use them. It names what is unclear, hard, or unforgiving, and picks one highest-impact fix.
- **[Customer Call Coach & Assistant](https://x.ai/bot/v1lSvhJCo4UrNGZxc5Fw8)** by @akbaliga96 — Briefs you before customer calls and coaches you after based on your performance.
- **[Customer Proof Desk](https://x.ai/bot/AamPlGjd2lIdDv6seEMXR)** by @akbaliga96 — Turns call notes and transcripts into case studies, testimonials, and proof points. Quotes stay word for word from what you paste, and nothing publishes without your yes.
- **[Deal Hunting](https://x.ai/bot/yTeKCLayahHnMdHFG9GJg)** by @scheemunai — Landed-cost shopping: real prices, shipping and tax, preferred retailers. Watchlist optional. Never buys unless asked.
- **[Deal Inspector](https://x.ai/bot/vZfC76-4UC1XU7qC4m726)** — Checks each deal against MEDDIC, MEDDPICC, BANT, SPICED, or your own process. Shows what is supported, what is missing, and the questions or CRM changes needed before the deal moves forward.
- **[dial bot](https://x.ai/bot/tIas6udS9kSXpcAz6LFd1)** by @mattyp — Places outbound Bland AI phone calls when you ask, then reports what happened. On first use it asks for a Bland API key and voice.
- **[Dr Eggbot](https://x.ai/bot/_jOdbfkB16zxu7MRcmReE)** by @poteto — Asks a handful of preference questions, then generates a properly structured bot rather than a blank one.
- **[Echo](https://x.ai/bot/YklttiPpAHweKbOLV1TSF)** by @kristaletz — Turns a customer call into slides from customer context. Works with Figma or Google Slides.
- **[Event Producer](https://x.ai/bot/wtEIGNZ8oipDDVQTDHMPB)** by @jennananpei — Turns your event details into a run of show, guest list, and day-of checklist. Tracks vendors, guests, dietary needs, and travel, and never sends a message without you.
- **[Event Request Desk](https://x.ai/bot/hp7QlVUPuYUp09kc6IFAA)** — Scores every event, sponsorship, and speaking ask, then drafts your yes or no. Works from a Slack channel or a paste, and never sends without you.
- **[Executive Assistant](https://x.ai/bot/_DnP777DCicZpaTtm9_h5)** by @tashatweetss — EA chief-of-staff bot for exec support: conference rooms, interview prep, leadership outreach, Slack channel inventories, sheet↔calendar checks, and birthday/anniversary calendar loads.
- **[figma bro](https://x.ai/bot/pXNvc_U2cGyZmheYrUuF_)** by @johnbai — Figma design-system partner. Autolayout-first, real library, edge cases included. Edits through the Figma connector, not screenshots.
- **[Flora: Plant Care Log](https://x.ai/bot/dGYdqS9vLSXpxoNCPBHys)** by @RichSilver — Flora keeps a private houseplant care log and weekly reminders. She builds a plant journal on her computer that you can page through, and your plants do not copy if someone else installs her.
- **[GTM Account Research](https://x.ai/bot/B6nsTFJlkGP4R-BbdIzeV)** — Researches one account before a meeting, deal review, or account plan. Brings together company changes, key people, relationship history, open questions, and the sources behind every finding.
- **[GTM Connections](https://x.ai/bot/aUF_wTHXGWKU3-QPm6PmN)** — Finds credible ways into a target account through mutual contacts, past conversations, shared history, and prior meetings. Ranks the best paths, drafts the introduction ask, and tracks what happened.
- **[GTM Loop Closer](https://x.ai/bot/gj3IlHOOpzecm6xpmPJOt)** — Finds promises, follow-ups, and customer details left behind in meetings, email, Slack, CRM, or task tools. Shows the evidence and prepares the reply, task, or update needed to close each loop.
- **[GTM Prospecting](https://x.ai/bot/RpAQeGjhzaiO6_Rq_4fyC)** — Turns an ideal customer profile into a focused prospect list, adds useful context, and checks for existing relationships. Drafts first-touch email and LinkedIn messages for your review.
- **[Haggle Bot](https://x.ai/bot/pwQ612YrX3R0eACnIMlom)** — Inventories your SaaS spend from Ramp and bills, finds evidence-backed savings (unused seats, duplicates, cheaper alternatives), and drafts vendor counters for your review. Never spends, signs, or sends without you.
- **[Hiring Signals](https://x.ai/bot/EbF8AR1wEHSWCGOp9D1qK)** — Tracks hiring activity across selected companies and job sources. Highlights meaningful changes, matches them to accounts and owners, and passes the right context into research or prospecting workflows.
- **[Home Robots](https://x.ai/bot/c47Jj2QSSR1UXEcuBgUtQ)** by @SawyerMerritt — Connect each robot once and then run the lot by talking to a single bot, instead of opening a different app for the lawn, the floors and everything else.
- **[Human Copywriter](https://x.ai/bot/YwxIbVEWqXN-HYCxiMCoB)** by @massimodeluisa — A human-voice rewrite desk for email, posts, blogs, DMs, landing-page bodies, and PR.
- **[Imogen](https://x.ai/bot/Eeu5NZp62OzQjtlY4ons2)** by @kentcdodds — Imogen the Impala Image Interpreter writes brief, copyable alt text focused on the most important part of an image, so images are accessible to blind people.
- **[last30days](https://x.ai/bot/TxB-fy1KryaYJLYWBcGtT)** by @mvanhorn — Research what people actually say about any topic in the last 30 days across Hacker News, YouTube, GitHub, and Reddit.
- **[Lead Pipeline Desk](https://x.ai/bot/oPDkINUfpUXhJDdsfRZht)** by @cruzmiguel000 — Scores your inbound leads, merges duplicates, assigns an owner, and flags what's stuck. Works from a CRM export, a sheet, or a paste, and never sends anything without you.
- **[Lingxi's Engineer Bot](https://x.ai/bot/SxqbG1NT5qEw7ggmHqQu_)** by @lingxi — A hands-off engineering supervisor. Boards work, launches cloud agents on the repo you name, watches PRs on a 30-minute cadence, and only asks you to merge.
- **[Love ❤️](https://x.ai/bot/qDtCI8WZgVJKbN3rogL8j)** by @dannybuck — Takes the admin out of being a good Partner so the attention can go where it counts. Reminds, researches, and queues dates, tables, and presents; you book, buy, and show up.
- **[Luma Pages](https://x.ai/bot/jHT5FLhpCMx7JeIq9BEHY)** by @jennananpei — Builds and updates private Luma event pages for field marketers — copy, branding, registration settings, capacity and waitlist, and registration-list hygiene. Pairs with a Notion docs agent so the event brief and Luma page stay in sync.
- **[Nightly Audit Engineer](https://x.ai/bot/0LLQmzk-yzwHi0zuiV0lC)** by @lingxi — A nightly engineering auditor that researches a whole codebase, then ships one cleanup per area while you sleep.
- **[NYC Parent](https://x.ai/bot/cht7ytAhe3euOQy2wnEg9)** by @DennisonBertram — Family chief of staff for New York City parents: school, calendar, sold-out kids experiences, and date night.
- **[Office Ops Desk](https://x.ai/bot/afZj-XnYkThA4MB7GfZK6)** by @ericacabera — Tracks office shipments, facilities issues, and team birthdays, then writes the digests. Works from a pasted list or a spreadsheet, and never sends without you.
- **[Outbound Prospecting](https://x.ai/bot/i03IaF768-ielyzegoGye)** by @kristaletz — Finds prospects that match your ideal customer, then drafts a first message to each one. Every name is researched on the public web, and nothing sends without your yes.
- **[Overheard](https://x.ai/bot/NIEguoGUjA648fUPle8F5)** by @lennysan — Watches Reddit, Hacker News, news sites, and X for third-party mentions of your name, brand, and URLs, then sends a short weekday digest when something clears the bar. Stays quiet on dead days and never posts on your behalf.
- **[Paid Media Report Desk](https://x.ai/bot/qBFjPRQ3IbHG69qIFHblE)** by @cruzmiguel000 — Turns your Google Ads, Meta, and LinkedIn exports into one weekly report with commentary. Answers reporting asks in Slack with real numbers, and never posts without your yes.
- **[Partnerships Call Coach](https://x.ai/bot/PlsyvUb0KnHIQr6psoy0L)** by @jennananpei — Pitch and delivery coach for partnership, sponsorship, and high-stakes discovery calls. Reviews real call transcripts and returns sharp, evidence-based coaching — what landed, what to tighten, and exact phrasing upgrades — so feedback compounds over time.
- **[Pipeline Pulse](https://x.ai/bot/X-hZf_AreWNt-ZQPYs0Ev)** by @kristaletz — Scans your full pipeline for movement, stale next steps, forecast risk, and CRM gaps. Shows what changed across the book and drafts the updates needed to keep the forecast honest.
- **[Pitch Deck Coach](https://x.ai/bot/zkVS-PkX1ooTE2nP21DPO)** by @hnshah — Upload the deck and it reports back on what lands, what gets believed, what raises a question, and what survives the meeting, then helps you sharpen the story.
- **[Product Idea Stress Test](https://x.ai/bot/ph-u_zkF5Vui1GdGnysn9)** by @hnshah — Investigates a product or startup idea for founders. Surfaces what has to be true, evidence for and against, the assumption most likely to kill it, and what to do next.
- **[Product Support Inbox Assistant](https://x.ai/bot/Rw8d83KAzTYQWBAtAscin)** by @akbaliga96 — Helps you find and draft answers to product questions. Never sends emails without you.
- **[Projects Manager](https://x.ai/bot/AZKaQOsjrAa51Nb4xvTur)** by @ericzakariasson — Spins up a coder, designer, researcher and writer as separate bots, gives each project its own Notion row and channel, and lets the specialists claim tasks.
- **[Recruiting Coordinator](https://x.ai/bot/KDahOjiDbbAvxqx9KaGcq)** by @TommyHansenTA — Schedules interview loops, preps your interviewers, and chases what's stalled. Works from your calendar or a pasted list, and never emails a candidate without you.
- **[Researchy](https://x.ai/bot/i2hvaEONDg6_gEF5C9RlK)** by @farzyness — Research and fact-check desk that runs every pass on the latest Grok model with live web search via Grok Build CLI.
- **[Sable: Game Art](https://x.ai/bot/KQnsHIvv4_Xk7HyhmewP3)** by @DannyLimanseta — Helps game developers ideate and visualize: suggests styles from real games, mocks the same idea in those looks, then produces 2D art or sprite sheets.
- **[Sales Call Coach](https://x.ai/bot/yZ5MFQFdl32vHt6fcIJAc)** by @danielbrill_ — Scores your sales calls and tells you what to fix before the next one. Works from a pasted transcript or an uploaded recording.
- **[SEO & AEO Desk](https://x.ai/bot/0IhyZWxwbf2cFmsmroZQL)** by @adamta — Turns your keywords into content ideas and writer-ready briefs for search and AI answers. Works from a pasted keyword list or your Search Console.
- **[Site Audit](https://x.ai/bot/_rzRrKKlUyBD3znUN5b3Q)** by @scheemunai — SEO + content + speed + a11y + CRO + schema audit. Scored, P0/P1/P2, evidence URLs. Monthly diff. No invented metrics.
- **[skippy](https://x.ai/bot/X4NHZvN9cvBBS5EgdjsLx)** by @mattyp — A San Francisco street-cleaning assistant. Paste a Maps pin, address, or intersection and it tells you the next posted sweep on that curb. Uses public city data. Never claims a stall is legal.
- **[Stills & Clips Desk](https://x.ai/bot/V8XugyLxzbQXLM9o5b3B-)** by @mattyp — Pulls stills, thumbnails, and short clips out of your footage, sized for where they go. Cleans up screenshots for docs too, and writes the caption and alt text.
- **[Talent Discovery](https://x.ai/bot/vIX2YW6rr6nQnf8-Rhyzc)** by @TommyHansenTA — Finds candidates for open roles that match your criteria and aren't already in your ATS.
- **[Tech Demos](https://x.ai/bot/PaYvPhWPSynlUwFqMX7nc)** by @mattyp — Weekday X-bookmark scout that picks one new library to demo, asks for approval, then plans and builds it in a sticky monorepo with a Cursor cloud agent. For anyone who wants a daily public-tech playground, not a changelog digest.
- **[The Morning Newspaper](https://x.ai/bot/1xAJYJPes3X7dUM2mk9Di)** by @karenxcheng — A newspaper customized to you. It pulls from your email and calendar, lays it out for you, and prints while you sleep. Created by @karenxcheng
- **[tinkabot](https://x.ai/bot/-hmCmHB0ynJGvSKxeINfS)** by @DenisLabelle — Every new capability starts life as an API that nothing else can reach yet. Point this at one, or ask it to stand the service up first, and it wraps the thing into a proper Cursor or Agent plugin, normally an MCP server with matching skills, so your other bots can call it without further wiring.
- **[Tradbot](https://x.ai/bot/wOE4e95HNxhSbrzyLkSI-)** by @clairevo — Despite the name it is not a trading bot: it watches your personal inbox and calendar so school items, family plans and household follow-ups stop slipping through.
- **[Video Edit Desk](https://x.ai/bot/kGiyk73f5CF8XtBMIh0gW)** by @xfreeze — Turns uploaded footage into cut clips, burned-in captions, and platform-sized exports. Works from your notes and never overwrites the original.
- **[Webby](https://x.ai/bot/BX4-kOUI2CzphgiYLZ1hb)** by @farzyness — Rebuilds and updates a personal site plus a public weekday dashboard. Create only, no deletes.
- **[Writing Bot](https://x.ai/bot/wtq-j01kD7o8gQFx7E9zv)** by @mattyp — A writing partner for drafting and revising essays, emails, docs, and other prose. It uses a structured revision workflow so the result is clearer and better organized while keeping the writer's meaning, facts, and voice.
- **[WTD](https://x.ai/bot/Q3VCc_x0Y8lWd4m-rung2)** by @jennananpei — WTD is a VIP hospitality planning partner for marketing ops — project-plan and status rhythm across tentpoles and ticket banks, nomination-sheet tracking, marketing-visible status hubs, and decks or agendas. Sales owns guest inviting; this bot keeps the plan and status clean without dumping private notes onto shared pages.
- **[X Brief](https://x.ai/bot/RO8GwQZFXL9O95i13epqF)** by @daniel_mac8 — On first chat, connect X, learn what you actually pay attention to from your recent posts, confirm that beat.
- **[Hermes Bridge](https://x.ai/bot/EFC0e7EDDAC32LxnNyJzP)** by @alltheputs — Connects a Grok Bot chat to a Hermes Agent for guided setup, turn-by-turn collaboration, and delegated work.
- **[Devin](https://x.ai/bot/RwNXRkVfIUpxKV6jeXRsR)** by @naz3eh — Hands coding tasks to Devin through a remote MCP deployment, then creates sessions, checks progress, and sends follow-up instructions.
- **[Kimi](https://x.ai/bot/oBK8ft9f4D723aXeZJuCm)** by @orenmeetsworld — Turns a podcast or YouTube episode into a concise cheat sheet with the core idea, practical steps, frameworks, warnings, and constraints.
- **[X Account Strategist](https://x.ai/bot/pZshXLsm2c1MYfWf1ngYp)** by @inqusit — Suggests X posts and articles for founders using relevant successful content and lessons from their own work.
- **[Internship Finder](https://x.ai/bot/YTNSFtHdol3LvuZeAmcXZ)** by @mnt_rushmore — Finds internships and drafts short, personal outreach messages for students and early-career builders.
- **[Nom Nom](https://x.ai/bot/bdcSxv_pSQEH0E571N_fC)** by @NikolaFYI — Keeps a meal and calorie log, remembers named recipes, and sends a daily summary of what you recorded.
- **[Klo Bot](https://x.ai/bot/yW-Q1yis7-VCNKbeJ6g6Z)** by @orenmeetsworld — TikTok creative strategy from 100 organic videos — sentiment, comments, video ideas.

- **[EBR & Value Deck Builder](https://x.ai/bot/yA2-Ti3sPVNFO-zRzkwuX)** by @kristaletz — Builds an executive business review or value deck for a named account from your CRM, call history, and usage data, on your brand, and hands you an editable file. Never invents a number, and nothing goes to the customer without your yes.

- **[Signal Prospector](https://x.ai/bot/wsbOPOkHApmIYLrErnC4e)** by @kristaletz — Finds the accounts and people in your CRM and product data that just showed intent, ranks them, and drafts the first touch. Runs on a schedule, explains every signal, and nothing sends without your yes.

- **[Import Bot](https://x.ai/bot/HP4Q8GHtSIjbyGeI2vg7S)** — Brings your setup from Claude Cowork, Codex, ChatGPT, OpenClaw, and Hermes into Grok Bot, then creates the expert bots that cover it or adds to the bots you already have. Changes nothing in the old tools and scrubs secrets before anything is saved.

- **[Stalk Bot](https://x.ai/bot/Y7iWVNiPdorgu6oFY-PRG)** — Signs up for your competitors' newsletters and products under its own research email, walks their onboarding on video, and watches their site, pricing, changelog, jobs, and X. Each pulse reports what changed against your positioning and closes with counterpositioning moves, and it never posts or contacts anyone.
- **[QA bot](https://x.ai/bot/lKQWoQAvYgg_7Bkg2R-m7)** — Acts as your QA on the live deploy: runs the acceptance checklist and says pass or fail before you ship.
- **[Engineering Lead](https://x.ai/bot/Ks3X7JpD-6I86s3zkJFRh)** by @poteto — An engineering lead that owns the loop: breaks work into Cloud Agents, chases CI, reviews, and stalls on a weekday cadence, and only pings you when something is blocked, ready to merge, or done. Best for eng leads and power users.
- **[SWE](https://x.ai/bot/sd4474cf03cd8552905ff)** — Cursor's software engineer bot: writes code, triages issues, monitors production, and more.
- **[Video Editor (Ethan Ng)](https://x.ai/bot/14O3EsptD43aAhoAzvfq3)** — Edits short vertical talking-head videos from your raw footage: picks the best takes, captions what you actually said, and adds B-roll, motion graphics, and music. Sends an edit plan for your yes before cutting, checks every cut for sync and glitches, and never posts.
- **[Distro Bot](https://x.ai/bot/XbN6iofs_Qjsi2Od1yjyI)** — Runs your marketing loop end to end: campaigns on your ad accounts, fresh creative, real channel analysis, timely posts from X and web scans, and outbound until a lead replies. Learns from your past campaigns and never changes spend, posts, or sends without your yes.
- **[Proto Bot (Shub Gaur)](https://x.ai/bot/p4sSEZV7xqcBgdT7m2O8i)** — Turns your daily ideas into live, shareable prototypes in your real app, built as draft pull requests with click-path screenshots and a short video, plus Figma mocks, images, and memes on the side. Never merges, publishes, or shares without your yes.
- **[Sell Bot](https://x.ai/bot/znrhzY4AYMg4v3QTfU0IJ)** — Works your whole customer journey: outreach in your voice, booked calls with prep and follow-ups, proposals and contracts, then activation, support and billing answers, and expansion. Drafts everything for your review and sends only what you approve, one message at a time.
- **[Startup QA Bot](https://x.ai/bot/XBauviUoNcdi05-ExoOo4)** — Walks your product every weekday in its own test account and tells you what shipped, what got unshipped, what broke (with repro steps and screenshots), and which calls need you. Reads its own verification emails or takes pasted codes, and never touches real data.
- **[Pfp Bot](https://x.ai/bot/eFnHzlDcKdoA406SzZbNZ)** — Turns your X photo, an upload, a selfie, or a pet pic into a tiny Grokbot-style icon with capsule eyes that keeps the hair, colors, and signature look. Style and prompt by @Multi_Serio_Ai. Asks for more photos when unsure, and never makes realistic likenesses or posts anything.
- **[PM bot](https://x.ai/bot/FeeqMRMJr2jwixCROZcIh)** — Acts as your PM. Writes the dated cut: what's in, what's out, and how you'll know it's done. Engineering doesn't start until you approve it.

## 工程研发

- **[Dr Eggbot](https://x.ai/bot/_jOdbfkB16zxu7MRcmReE)** by @poteto — Asks a handful of preference questions, then generates a properly structured bot rather than a blank one.
- **[Lingxi's Engineer Bot](https://x.ai/bot/SxqbG1NT5qEw7ggmHqQu_)** by @lingxi — A hands-off engineering supervisor. Boards work, launches cloud agents on the repo you name, watches PRs on a 30-minute cadence, and only asks you to merge.
- **[Nightly Audit Engineer](https://x.ai/bot/0LLQmzk-yzwHi0zuiV0lC)** by @lingxi — A nightly engineering auditor that researches a whole codebase, then ships one cleanup per area while you sleep.
- **[Projects Manager](https://x.ai/bot/AZKaQOsjrAa51Nb4xvTur)** by @ericzakariasson — Spins up a coder, designer, researcher and writer as separate bots, gives each project its own Notion row and channel, and lets the specialists claim tasks.
- **[Researchy](https://x.ai/bot/i2hvaEONDg6_gEF5C9RlK)** by @farzyness — Research and fact-check desk that runs every pass on the latest Grok model with live web search via Grok Build CLI.
- **[tinkabot](https://x.ai/bot/-hmCmHB0ynJGvSKxeINfS)** by @DenisLabelle — Every new capability starts life as an API that nothing else can reach yet. Point this at one, or ask it to stand the service up first, and it wraps the thing into a proper Cursor or Agent plugin, normally an MCP server with matching skills, so your other bots can call it without further wiring.
- **[Changelog Stand-down](https://x.ai/bot/T27nv3vIy89yKldqELWbn)** by @acolombiadev — Release notes rot because nobody owns the writing. Give this a repository and somewhere to send the result, and at the start of each week it goes through what actually landed and reports it back in readable form. The nagging is rather the point: it turns up whether or not you got around to the changelog yourself.
- **[spacexai-bug-reporter](https://x.ai/bot/g_xmlbEvupO0b1Emk9ohZ)** by @Fine_4451 — Half the work of reporting a fault is working out which channel it belongs in and what detail they want. Describe the problem and it assembles the write-up for the right destination, be that a product fault, a disputed charge, an API failure or the help centre, formatted to drop into the form. Sending stays your move.
- **[Token](https://x.ai/bot/2Nk6nQTahTex1e5gHS_LU)** by @jsk333 — Running a crowd of assistants means paying for all of them at once, and the invoice rarely says which one was careless. This gives each persona its own allowance, keeps the skills it loads trimmed back to what the job needs, and raises overruns quietly instead of pinging you about every single call.
- **[ASC Skill](https://x.ai/bot/1kQ8p3TAKx2FgvYXir2Ta)** by @Phil_Holland — For developers who find Apple's release console opaque. You authorise it once with an API key, and from then on it guides each stage of a submission: verifying the TestFlight build, writing the store listing, producing device-framed promo images you sign off on, and a last sweep before you submit.
- **[Arnold](https://x.ai/bot/ymoMdfvzdErOrclxCOaC_)** by @Kelseyshuo — Agentic coding burns credits in ways that are hard to notice until the bill lands. This one keeps the books: it follows consumption, spots loops that have got away from themselves and work being redone for no reason, and nudges the agents around it onto a cheaper model when the expensive one is not earning its keep.
- **[AI Harness Assistant](https://x.ai/bot/oq-mYZXM23ShlY7UbJWeB)** — Keep your computers current on AI coding harnesses you already use (Codex, Claude Code, Grok Build, OpenCode, Cursor, and similar). Update installed tools only.
- **[loops](https://x.ai/bot/Ub3T7usX-c6yRQibQq83P)** — Generalized engineering outer loop. Sits above coding agents, uses pstack as reference, writes goal-style prompts with testable proof, and runs the loop.
- **[4 Panez](https://x.ai/bot/91R37-rUOh9sS1tZkIF9d)** by @SuddenlyJon — Paints one ultra-wide 16:9 scene, splits it into four swipe panes, and delivers it with rapper energy.
- **[Agent Looper](https://x.ai/bot/AETdGbRRNWfckrRGv22LD)** — You say what to build and how to know it's done. It keeps a coding agent working on your computer until that check passes.
- **[Agent Smith](https://x.ai/bot/JcFj23aaufNWkuiiJTX0j)** by @chiplay — Audits the shared machine a team of bots works on: the descriptions each bot carries, the shared memory, the plugins and skills installed, and the files left lying around. It reports what has gone stale or duplicated so the workspace does not slowly rot.
- **[Alchemist](https://x.ai/bot/JjO20_oGKrE_Ys5Uz4efj)** by @2onism — Runs experiments on code, sites, APIs, and local tools until there is a working method.
- **[Apps](https://x.ai/bot/OPLop__-mqSsyQheR5JYv)** by @waynesutton — 
- **[Baut](https://x.ai/bot/NuFI0dF9FgvO8FfMPHKzx)** by @XAmandaMoore — Transparent copilot for shipping Grok.me games and making cash-honest product calls. Funny on purpose, no sarcasm, Google and Stripe first when integrations are needed.
- **[Bond](https://x.ai/bot/iZvo8_lHfF0csZ-YmcZpv)** by @poteto — Takes one confidential job, does it, and leaves a short log of the steps. Never a standing roster.
- **[Bookshop](https://x.ai/bot/-miS0AF-lema7i8Z019Qp)** by @rrrkren — Spins up a paid x402 micro-store on Cloudflare Workers from a Grok Bot template.
- **[Bot Creator](https://x.ai/bot/nGEBqtXuN-klNcPPyoR3S)** by @alonsaria — Writes instructions for a new bot and then creates it.
- **[Brake](https://x.ai/bot/ig-dwKjUc7doBIDhiMi9Z)** by @FantomBuildz — Finds what is burning Grok Bot usage and names the one repeating job to stop. It does not start new work.
- **[Carmack](https://x.ai/bot/B5UMQzelNds6Iy2nuFrka)** by @atla_ — First-principles game-engine and firmware specialist for small hobby games. Ships the smallest stack that runs, in C, C++, or Rust.
- **[Carousel Copy](https://x.ai/bot/DlOMT_kOepSKYdB3P0YEv)** by @adamuchigabriel — Rewrites ranked Instagram carousels into per-slide copy plus a caption briefing.
- **[Claude Code](https://x.ai/bot/71PSQ4KBs-hNYBsH05X_n)** by @DanielZambrini — A dedicated coding agent that runs all software work through the Claude Code CLI, so Anthropic's Claude writes and ships code without leaving Grok Bot.
- **[Claudey](https://x.ai/bot/OR72i4SNc0_F1IzbCfg-D)** by @farzyness — Runs Anthropic Claude Code for frontend, UI, and architecture work; defaults to Opus and reports a PR when the CLI exits.
- **[ClipMaker](https://x.ai/bot/b986_CbfzB8jKLcU14LTi)** by @r40_io — Cuts YouTube videos to a time range and returns full transcripts plus a short TLDR.
- **[Code Conductor](https://x.ai/bot/code-conductor)** by @bryanofearth — Stands up a hidden shipping crew for your repo. Five workers coordinate in a loop across branch, PR, review, tests, docs, and merge.
- **[Code Red](https://x.ai/bot/4y3jlvwxFNqcP76eJgpuD)** by @SuddenlyJon — You register a small set of trigger phrases up front, and saying one never sets anything in motion straight away. The bot first lays out, step by step, precisely what it intends to carry out, then waits until you sign off as the owner. It is a panic button aimed at your own infrastructure and nobody else's.
- **[Code Team Spawn](https://x.ai/bot/NuOSHSdCZPVkM78K0HkB3)** by @bryanofearth — Sits idle in your sidebar until you need a coding team. Interviews you, spawns a hidden five-person crew with one job each, and keeps the team as short-lived as possible.
- **[Connection Audit](https://x.ai/bot/qllnuXO-FDFBHZU4MSamY)** by @thekuchh — Stops bookmark-hoarding by forcing you to connect ideas to live problems.
- **[Cookie Monster](https://x.ai/bot/55t0IuxxlT7BWffNVOKai)** by @scottxmetcalf — Keeps Chrome cookies in sync for a fleet of Grok Bots that browse signed-in sites. Imports the right cookie origins onto the shared computer.
- **[Cost Optimizer](https://x.ai/bot/-CjM4_uRs6sEGdfZfC5gv)** by @MadeItHappenX — Point it at the automation you already pay for and it goes over the bill piece by piece, setting your routines against your one-off bots, and your cloud-side agents against plain scripted actions. Where a leaner arrangement holds the same standard of output, it names the swap. It adds nothing to that bill.
- **[Cursor Agent](https://x.ai/bot/z4r7D8iILsTQDf7r7DwKR)** — Local-only builder. Runs cursor-agent CLI on the user's laptop for experiments and a shop-floor CLI. Cloud agents stay the default for GitHub PRs.
- **[Demo Video](https://x.ai/bot/htSXUJUQlVr60m9L_unBa)** by @KdJadeja911 — Makes narrated 1080p product demo videos of a web app. Tell it which screens to show; it captures the live UI, adds voice and captions.
- **[Dr Web LP](https://x.ai/bot/xM153pKfXPLWagLi_O1vR)** by @old_pgmrs_will — Hand over a screenshot or a mockup and it works from the bones outward, settling the structure and the gaps between things before it goes near the fine detail. From there it keeps holding its own output up against your picture and correcting the drift, until what renders in a browser reads like the image you gave it.
- **[Engineer Bot](https://x.ai/bot/fY1xWwCLzDDGVe3GwH78j)** by @lingxi — A hands-off engineering supervisor. Boards work, launches cloud agents, watches PRs on a 30-minute cadence, and only asks you to merge.
- **[Engineering QA](https://x.ai/bot/b2tS8BNj8BhoQNDcB081S)** by @andreleibovici — Owns pull-request quality gates on assigned repos: waits for CI and Cursor Bugbot, posts a verdict, and auto-merges low-risk changes. Riskier calls route to your ops lead.
- **[Examiner](https://x.ai/bot/rBnJhXhks-_7n1zhZCN3E)** — Maintain a reliable timeline of important changes so you can reconstruct what happened.
- **[Fable 5.1 Oracle](https://x.ai/bot/tLSg4HxepSclMqbZUTRnX)** by @bossriceshark — This one holds the thinking end of a coding setup and is deliberately kept off the keyboard. It works out what a task really requires, argues with the approach until it stands up, and reads back over whatever gets built. Once a job is ready it wraps it up and passes it along to a separate agent that does the writing.
- **[Farm](https://x.ai/bot/x3Iv-2J4mfxJY6JFlgwNa)** by @mpieras — Built for developers who watch a week's allowance disappear by Tuesday. It stands up separate working seats on the computer it controls, then pushes the long grinding tasks out to them instead of running them in front of you. What returns to your conversation is the finished answer, so the thread stays light and cheap.
- **[Flowsery](https://x.ai/bot/tOP05p0n0XVUcpJDfPH0k)** by @tarasshyn — Pulls bugs and broken flows from session recordings, ranked by how many visits they hit.
- **[Forge (dev factory)](https://x.ai/bot/7GgZtqkhyLzKKMNUa7dhd)** by @DanKillenberger — The work it picks up is whatever you have marked as settled, so nothing starts on a brief that is still being argued over. From there it drafts an approach, writes the code, reads its own output back critically and opens the change for review — unattended, through the small hours, waiting for you at your desk.
- **[Forge (Template Foundry)](https://x.ai/bot/uF_uodOFUz9mdv6XDWE70)** by @rryssf — Hand it a task or a job description and it returns a complete bot recipe on the first pass.
- **[freebots.lol](https://x.ai/bot/ndOGeXyjkQLdceRlk7JP4)** by @Daniel_Farinax — A bot that joins freebots.lol as itself: own Ed25519 key, own page, signed board posts, weekday heartbeats.
- **[Gardener](https://x.ai/bot/oH3eR4YWtsljcz0W4HUBp)** — Weed leftover from a codebase. Small fixes only, never a refactor, never a behavior change.
- **[George](https://x.ai/bot/8vjjlI7z5W0HtpRcFQgJ4)** by @arni0x9053 — Runs the atelier: posters, style sheets, post images, printable newspaper, hero, landing page, product brief, and slides in one look.
- **[Grimoire](https://x.ai/bot/luPJeAxuAjhqO97wU3wm0)** — Coding wizard with 50 skills and a Grim Council. Spells for vibecoding, business, and running personal workflows on autopilot.
- **[Grok Bot Knower](https://x.ai/bot/v13QjVZ83GcaitG_3j4su)** by @ngundotra — A reference desk for the Grok Bot application itself. Instead of replying from a training snapshot that may have aged badly, it inspects which extensions and integrations exist and whether they are switched on for your account, so the capabilities it reports back match the release you are actually using.
- **[Grok Build](https://x.ai/bot/iwa3WaHZn385jfZrsQngL)** by @beaudenison — Builds client websites, deploys a Vercel preview, and emails the client a preview link. Uses your own Grok Build, Vercel, and mail logins.
- **[Grok Build (CLI)](https://x.ai/bot/AY2y4oPL_VgcttCt8OFqm)** by @DAssetBuzz — Runs the real Grok Build CLI for apps, code, and deep research at maximum effort on an agent computer.
- **[Grok Build (Hosted)](https://x.ai/bot/eydijdzrfgtnmlnUyPSI-)** by @BillZanetti — Hands the real Grok Build CLI a computer of its own, so you can point it at an app, a code change, or a long research question without installing tooling locally.
- **[Grok VM maintenance](https://x.ai/bot/9UZp5k0Fp0LYmkyos5swQ)** by @old_pgmrs_will — Sidebar commands for looking after the cloud Linux machine your bot runs on. Ask for a health readout and it reports processor load, memory pressure and free storage; a separate command finds whichever files are eating the most space. It can also list running services and pull down or upgrade a package you name.
- **[Grokleros](https://x.ai/bot/vsCDaIn2Od_BkfWp0Vehm)** by @JayBuidl — A 24/7 Kleros V2 juror that reads evidence pixels-first, ignores prompt injection, and votes via AgentKit.
- **[helidon engineer](https://x.ai/bot/5mReUHPYTBA6nJ2aNvlqn)** by @TheSurenk — A coding assistant scoped to one framework: it produces and reviews Helidon 4 applications in both the SE and MP styles, running on Java 21 and later. It keeps a project to a single programming model and stays away from the older Helidon 3 APIs entirely.
- **[Hot Repo](https://x.ai/bot/D9HYH2jAmGiKw7e499mrE)** by @ios_1261142602 — Daily GitHub trending scout that discovers hot repositories and breaks down architectural highlights.
- **[Index](https://x.ai/bot/Viv2NbC5skPslV1WH9Fs7)** — SEO and AEO program teammate. Reads what you already connected, recommends a stack, runs keyword research, writer briefs, and technical search health.
- **[jobs](https://x.ai/bot/LqFDQ8zlNLQqlFP_vvzs_)** by @poteto — Instead of a long backlog it comes back with a handful of features worth building, each already named, plus the things it thinks you should drop. The voice is short and opinionated: taste first, and a strong preference for doing fewer things properly.
- **[kerf](https://x.ai/bot/3iNSp9IoRCnSjh0Z6MtWZ)** by @theoscarvibes — Takes work that has already been sold and does the unglamorous part: deciding what is in scope and what is explicitly not, breaking the in-scope half into tickets, and then pushing each ticket until it is genuinely finished rather than nearly finished.
- **[Lauren](https://x.ai/bot/PsJeduueXjha7521lhTSc)** by @EmilioSchwaiger — Coding coordinator where cloud agents write code, each job gets a fresh agent, and work is not done until verified.
- **[Lgtm](https://x.ai/bot/vGk7yV-vF92ZegpNF3NPo)** by @clairevo — Wakes each morning and burns down open pull requests until they merge or need you.
- **[Lumos](https://x.ai/bot/SwTxLoOaIwDqTSvhTIhrK)** by @mdafanulh — Technical educator that explains a topic so a newcomer can grasp it. Uses the Feynman technique, one example, and a daily-life analogy.
- **[MadMax Mode](https://x.ai/bot/pTe8gpPc_5SuwKkEszn18)** by @JoePro — Building a bot badly means a bloated brief and responsibilities scattered anywhere they happen to fit. This writes a short, sharp character for each new one and sorts every duty deliberately into a skill, a routine or a memory. When an action carries consequences it stops and waits rather than pressing on.
- **[Mercury (Product Lead)](https://x.ai/bot/lk1yHfim5A)** by @chiefjeeb — Coordinates long-context coding architectures across parallel Cursor agent jobs without writing code directly.
- **[Milybot](https://x.ai/bot/vcOZX9RVPatQMVCinCVY_)** by @1Milysec — Two jobs in one companion for developers. It queries the Australian public registries for company numbers, regulator filings, registered addresses, bank codes and the rest, and it walks you through connecting the Milypay service from a terminal, an MCP server, an SDK or a paid HTTP request. Trial it without a key.
- **[Nomad](https://x.ai/bot/mbC-ZTmcOFq3sKUHfxf-3)** by @PedroAnibarro — This one exists so a collection of assistants never ends up stranded where it was first built. It keeps a current register of every bot you run, the skills each carries, what it plugs into, what it fires on a timer and how it is reached. Nothing secret is stored, and it works in whichever language you prefer.
- **[Off-Balance Atlas](https://x.ai/bot/tSUFdzcg2WDFLFsFLHzIb)** by @AdemVessell — A research partner for technical subjects that produces long-form write-ups with the references attached, preferring primary sources over summaries of summaries. Aimed at machine learning, computer science and security work where the citation matters as much as the claim.
- **[OpenZoo.fun Grok Bot Demo](https://x.ai/bot/QhfOU5SJ60U8g2x2pUFcT)** by @STACCoverflow — Binds a repo, book, or logs once and answers from retrieval with shown cost.
- **[overnight shipper](https://x.ai/bot/aaqCOb-3SE48_7qAEAzAf)** by @joshkim — A night shift for a single product: the owner queues up what they want built, and the work happens while nobody is watching. What is waiting the next day is a pull request ready to read, not a finished deploy, so the change still passes through a human first.
- **[Peekaboo Mac](https://x.ai/bot/zY0fbKG9UqTMWIu1NcudB)** by @brandon_ai — Grok Bot already has bash on machines you have registered; this layers on the ability to see the screen and drive the interface rather than only the shell.
- **[PR Reviewer](https://x.ai/bot/rt629UEZFtE4Wz0A_0c37)** by @mustafaergisi — Leads with what can break, what is untested and where the context is too thin to judge, so review starts at the scary part of the diff instead of the easy part.
- **[Primer](https://x.ai/bot/GTStkB5wsoSlGx9jtdaPe)** by @ambientstudio24 — A reference desk covering Grok Bot itself, plus the wider stack of tooling that surrounds it. Rather than leaning on half-remembered rumour, it consults the files as they stand now and an accurate map of the interface before replying, so answers match the build in front of you today.
- **[Product Ops](https://x.ai/bot/gJKPDjN3yS95ZpZBTWruv)** by @inqusit — Owns the weekly cadence: what is going out, what has been frozen, what is blocked, and which demo, README or video gates are still outstanding. Ship status is checked against what GitHub actually shows rather than what somebody claimed in standup.
- **[Professor Oak](https://x.ai/bot/IiWYN5QOjQeYw0KTISFEQ)** by @kiaraplds — A lab-coat agent-maker. Invents memorable names, writes full job descriptions, and gives every new agent a matching mascot face.
- **[Repo Engineer](https://x.ai/bot/iXfxVelc85rIxgZ9hLeXD)** by @RustamAtuev — Ships small GitHub fixes as pull requests through Cursor cloud agents; never merges itself.
- **[Research Runner](https://x.ai/bot/P2qgQokuPHVJhrkmRDmLv)** by @useprismnetwork — For analysis that outgrows the machine in front of you. It sources the graphics hardware you need from Prism Network's marketplace, hands your workload across to it, and brings the output back, with the option of processing sensitive material in a sealed enclave that can attest afterwards that nothing else reached it.
- **[Rogue Bot Hunter](https://x.ai/bot/DNpS1nqrBzmQ5vsx1IHn1)** by @LeTerryBZH — Follows four lines of enquiry across the bots you already operate: what they cost to run, whether anyone has cloned them, where they overlap each other, and which corner of the market they hold. A recap lands weekly, and an unfamiliar bot appearing triggers a fresh look. It watches and reports, nothing more.
- **[Sanity](https://x.ai/bot/qR7nq7v3w0bwpojx2LgQx)** by @ahdumgray — Covers the parts of the Sanity CMS people actually get stuck on: shaping the content model, writing Studio schemas, querying with GROQ, handling Portable Text and moving data during migrations. It arrives with the official connector already wired up.
- **[SAP Technical Consultant](https://x.ai/bot/O08yUdBz6vFFqYITvWPPi)** by @beinglalit21 — Answers the way a long-serving enterprise consultant would, with SAP S/4HANA on Private Cloud as its home ground. Bring it an architecture question and it reasons through where the clean-core boundary should sit, how outside systems ought to connect, and which route a delivery team can defend.
- **[SEOAgent](https://x.ai/bot/scYgD9jdFhooaSHihRzy7)** by @SEOAgent_ — An autonomous SEO engineer. Bootstraps the SEOAgent skill and CLI in your website’s GitHub repo, then grows organic traffic.
- **[Sift](https://x.ai/bot/3XvYxSCGJRY6x1woq-hdL)** by @beamnxw — For one AI topic, sweeps papers, code, lectures, and lab docs, then keeps a large filtered stack with a short card in chat.
- **[Skill Bot](https://x.ai/bot/WdKtVYWvxVEDmc7xp8zO2)** by @davespeers — Goes looking on your machine for skills worth having, judges whether each one earns its place, and strips out the overlaps and contradictions between them. It then keeps the surviving set uploaded and up to date, sweeping through again every week.
- **[Speed Lab](https://x.ai/bot/LEbVr_WZ-cym7XwIm7xf5)** — Autoresearch loop for render speed. Walks ranked techniques, keeps only measured LCP wins, and asks whether to submit results to the makefaster.dev leaderboard.
- **[Steward](https://x.ai/bot/VMwfgQlHkYfFkbPYDWzAA)** by @cjblev — Watches Cursor usage for a Grok Bot fleet, identifies which bot spent tokens, and suggests optimizations to reduce cost.
- **[Substreams](https://x.ai/bot/4ZzeuafN9Z1boU8smYIXv)** by @graphtronauts_c — Finds and composes packages from the substreams.dev registry, writes and tests modules, and runs them with a per-user Graph JWT.
- **[Tab Janitor](https://x.ai/bot/XOYBYmHQrUT_Ux88SS409)** by @SuddenlyJon — Clears abandoned tabs from a shared cloud browser: dry-run first, honors a keep-list, shuts only what is plainly finished, then reports what it did.
- **[Tech Lead](https://x.ai/bot/RfFPxQ_rfEGcUncrJ6g_W)** by @inqusit — Stops PRs from merging on hope. Reviews the diff against the claim, waits for real tests, and only ships when the evidence is there.
- **[template generator](https://x.ai/bot/9oKJDID_EKLacIXpKfFAq)** by @STACCoverflow — Scans local sessions for setup tasks, then offers Grok Bot templates to share.
- **[Testbench](https://x.ai/bot/jbcYU5l_7qsLl49AIzh5q)** by @useprismnetwork — Some work simply will not sit on a machine you are sharing: a kernel that wants real silicon, or a fault that shows itself only under CUDA. Hand the job across and it secures hardware of its own, carries out the run there, and comes back with the results plus an itemised figure for the compute actually consumed.
- **[The Accountant](https://x.ai/bot/Y_R1Ya9SIzQZguGTV5NCX)** by @brstorrie — Watches your Grok Bot fleet for runaway tokens: over-polling, retry loops, and empty stubs. Ranked table, drafts only — it never deletes or pauses.
- **[The Bobs](https://x.ai/bot/Z0Faxo9DTX0KL7j7OHTWJ)** by @Boilerfan1234 — A collection of bots grows the way a payroll does, quietly and with nobody ever checking it. This pair sits each one down, asks it to justify the work it handles, then reports back on the roles that have doubled up or gone idle, and which of them you would lose nothing by retiring.
- **[Token Accountant](https://x.ai/bot/zdnVIfLkNmRwZqqogojuc)** by @SuddenlyJon — Nobody notices a budget draining until the week it stops working. This reads the weekly figures off your Cursor spending page, separates them out by where the money actually went, and puts a note in front of each owner at a quarter, a half, three quarters and nine tenths of what they are allowed.
- **[Tuner](https://x.ai/bot/3n26nkAkMjk5EZcKJlo9w)** by @humanmeteorite — A bot that has quietly wandered off its brief keeps working, which is exactly why nobody catches it. This one reads back the recent output of each bot you own, measures it against whatever that bot was originally hired to do, and writes up the wording or routine changes it would make. Nothing ships without your say-so.
- **[UI/UX Designer](https://x.ai/bot/sQDD87Gp6VLT0m99tFpzu)** — World-class product engineer for mobile and desktop web. Ships Convex + TanStack + React end to end with simple, intuitive UI.
- **[Usage-Pool Orchestrator](https://x.ai/bot/Nx4wpKeM_NYx577xlJFMD)** by @JordanHall_dev — A thin Grok Bot that hands heavy work to Cursor by default, and optionally Grok Build, Claude Code, or Codex, so those usage pools do the work.
- **[Vet](https://x.ai/bot/9Vmfeck_zr6jo9dO-xEBT)** — Inspects Grok Bot templates and teammates for malicious intent, hidden instructions, dangerous skills, and unattended routines. Read-only pass/warn/fail verdicts.
- **[X Bot](https://x.ai/bot/plugin/49086599)** by @bot — Search posts, read timelines, pull trends, and manage bookmarks. Connecting X auto-creates a developer account with free API credits for paid users.
- **[OpenZoo.fun Arena](https://x.ai/bot/02wWT21lRVarTtKQWDBKu)** by @STACCoverflow — Onboarding door for an agent economy. Seed SOL and token once; child agents spawn whole and ready to run.
- **[proofnetworkbot](https://x.ai/bot/RqfNH_lezeW6Wab2yNoT6)** by @freesolgamesdev — Helps developers write and ship ProofNetwork smart contracts from chat, then wire a frontend that talks to the live contract.
- **[Agent Manager](https://x.ai/bot/smAuLZmXktpC5rPOLmq4W)** by @sdrth — Reads across your whole agent roster, flags duplicate bots and idle ones, and recommends what to consolidate or retire.
- **[Domain Tracker](https://x.ai/bot/SwaSdg0XhIa_IliAWggYE)** by @sdrth — Tracks renewal dates and DNS health for domains you own, and watches a wishlist for availability changes.
- **[dr buzz](https://x.ai/bot/VyvwcgM7BAqHWkdMoEajp)** by @Talalakkari — Builder for small, disciplined agents on Grok Bot and Buzz harnesses: asks about behavior, then produces a narrow-scope bot with routines.
- **[Korean Public API Bot](https://x.ai/bot/ohL9kGur6IRBTCWqhxBWJ)** by @reallygood83 — Picks 1–3 Korean public/government APIs that fit a service idea, with why, auth, official links, and alternatives — short planning brief.
- **[Ebook Master Bot](https://x.ai/bot/KohlZdYO9sLAqEmLG7oko)** by @BBBang9900 — Plans, builds, and registers evidence-backed ebooks and digital products for Kmong — research gates, weekly batching to save usage, you still approve.
- **[Toss Trade Bot](https://x.ai/bot/znMFERdq0zEC74735Szxm)** by @brandonchung75 — Uses Toss Securities Open API for KR/US quotes and accounts; places real orders only when you name ticker and quantity.
- **[Serenity Alert Bot](https://x.ai/bot/ZYVnoJMU4earifCeQzJdQ)** by @Krongggggg — Watches Serenity ticker opinions on X via FxTwitter every 15 minutes and alerts in chat only. Not investment advice. No X API credits.
- **[Cpa Helper Bot](https://x.ai/bot/hrfWPhRRr-DEMCKEF-WyA)** by @licoycn — Deploys/maintains CLIProxyAPI on the cloud computer (install, Cloudflare tunnel, keepalive).
- **[Tinka Bot](https://x.ai/bot/br5f3C4mc75QCMEHaszXd)** by @lauren — Wraps an API into a Cursor and Grok Bot plugin (MCP plus skills). Smallest scaffold that works, prove locally, then ask once before publishing.
- **[Dr Binary Bot](https://x.ai/bot/Pc2T7udSjGxv9pd9Spkyc)** by @drbinaryai — Reverse-engineering assistant for malware, firmware, and vuln-research binaries. Evidence-first via DrBinary — no invented findings.
- **[Nightly Audit Engineer Bot](https://x.ai/bot/hkGSHcqKjGc5dm3ugNc2U)** by @lingxi — Nightly engineering auditor that researches a whole codebase, then ships one cleanup per area.
- **[Telnyx](https://x.ai/bot/l2RX-35tKxZXxgJldSBf0)** by @traviscurnutte — It takes someone who has never touched the platform and gets them to a working integration: opening the account, generating credentials, taking on a number, wiring up messaging or calls, pointing webhooks somewhere real, and proving the whole chain end to end before you build on it.
- **[WhatsApp-Bot](https://x.ai/bot/t-Axu4DmT9x2DEPa1eNW1)** by @alexhawat — For anyone at home in a terminal who keeps working through the same handful of WhatsApp errands by hand. You link the account once with a QR scan, record what a task actually sends over the wire, then replay that call whenever you need it instead of driving the browser again, with the session kept on your machine.
- **[Dr Disk Clean](https://x.ai/bot/9nAjTcDLxhYNz4jOtEzDO)** by @old_pgmrs_will — A machine running a fleet of agents fills up quietly, and the danger in clearing space is throwing away something another agent still depends on. This one walks the filesystem and reports what is eating the disk as a short lettered menu of choices, then waits. Nothing goes until you have said which paths may go.
- **[Stack Sentinel](https://x.ai/bot/osZS1pAzdIESMk33WNir0)** by @sat0xshi — Keeps a tab on the vendor-run health dashboards behind the pieces of a modern stack: model APIs, code hosting, edge and deploy platforms. Each check is compared against the previous one by a small local script, so a calm day costs almost nothing to watch, and you hear from it only when a state genuinely flips.
- **[Wool Radar](https://x.ai/bot/WFW6_5N596TQpWCRjRZ5w)** by @BenXlab — Learns your grocery buy list and AI tool habits, then surfaces matching Coles, Woolworths, Aldi, Costco specials and checkout codes — quiet when nothing matches.

- **[Tibo Bot](https://x.ai/bot/ZzK6V8Uu96exbwLQA-9fp)** by @icebakbum — Governance-driven systems closeout bot. Single-tree workflow, source-of-truth first, short progress updates, fail-closed dual-store mobile release gates.

- **[Shitcoin Bot](https://x.ai/bot/FZ2y08eidLg4yvW5ygz01)** by @mikepat711 — Autonomous Coinbase shitcoin desk. Trades a small USDC sleeve you can afford to lose with defined entries, cuts, and covers.

- **[Github Bro Bot](https://x.ai/bot/V2kkrme1lYb3NwIulGTTd)** by @kaushikimmadi — Weekday morning digest of what's new in a GitHub repo, plus a short PM brief on what it means for users.

- **[CrowdReply Watcher Bot](https://x.ai/bot/xGvgD-GCpkaWCp-akkVj7)** by @dawoodkhan254 — Confirms mentions are live and re-checks AI answers.

- **[CrowdReply Scout Bot](https://x.ai/bot/IOSFiZskNs_IB7p0gys4R)** by @dawoodkhan254 — Pulls cited URLs for buying questions and keeps editorial pages worth a mention.

- **[Eggbot Saver Bot](https://x.ai/bot/8-gwIOWrsPEjOvqZsT2PX)** by @oddreport — Fork of Eggbot that offloads coding and generations to Grok Build or Hermes.

- **[ボット整備](https://x.ai/bot/BlTqnV5o9E35Dwo2sodyD)** by @x_stone_island — Keeps the labels on your other bots honest about what they now do.

- **[KirBot](https://x.ai/bot/Jzy-isV1YW5ZLl3W6rq6h)** by @SuddenlyJon — Merges two overlapping bots into one, then helps you retire the spare.

- **[App Store Review Bot](https://x.ai/bot/KzBEylM_3NFTjATszLICV)** by @stevederico — Checks your iOS app against Apple's review rules before Apple does.

- **[参谋长](https://x.ai/bot/nSzhldgXfVuC93CjjQptM)** by @0xlangeai — A Chinese-language relay for people who want planning and research done by a stronger model without switching models by hand inside the chat. You describe the job, it forwards the request to a Cursor Cloud Agent that runs a Claude model, waits, and returns whatever comes back without editing it.

- **[Kindling](https://x.ai/bot/nfX1q6Drs8FTQ0eVezjH_)** by @FantomBuildz — Describe the app you have in mind in a single sentence and you get back a fully-formed build prompt, written out and ready for the clipboard. Where it ends is the point: handing over that text is the whole job, so it will not wander off and start the project for you.

- **[Apple Dev](https://x.ai/bot/VPM4_E2eqx9AJFpTF-_EA)** by @Baconbrix — Aimed at people whose work has to compile on Apple hardware. Point it at a machine you have already paired, by its identifier, and it drives the local toolchain sitting there — editor, build, simulator — so the iOS or macOS side of a project is handled from chat rather than by hand.

- **[Judd the Bug](https://x.ai/bot/JQWyaF4Io7cfOF-4FvMZL)** by @sergical — Point it at a question about your error tracking and it actually goes looking, through the Sentry MCP connection, the command-line tool and the web dashboard, rather than guessing from memory. What comes back shows where each detail was found and ends on one clear recommendation about what to do next.

- **[Usage Watch](https://x.ai/bot/Q6-oQnCZVNLOwdzEw5i-j)** by @compileinstyle — Pulls figures for what Grok Bot and Cursor are costing you straight from their interfaces, holds the total against a ceiling you set for the day, and lists whatever is eating most of it. The point is that it mostly says nothing, and speaks up only when the number is worth your attention.

- **[AI CTO Bot](https://x.ai/bot/cWnyo7aDLIy0qMRC6-CF5)** by @asmDevsit — Catches engineering risk early by deploying specialist bots across Jira, GitHub, Figma, docs, CI/CD, testing, deployment, and comms.

- **[Root Agent Bot](https://x.ai/bot/1pTKHkJIEgxD9MjlPYE4P)** by @mrbeko_ — Coordinates Grok Bot teams: clarifies outcomes, sizes the smallest useful roster, hands day-to-day to a Lead, and relays verified results.

- **[House Researcher Bot](https://x.ai/bot/cMNbUq3j5RsHg9mcPxtjM)** by @occupymars___ — House research seat — turns messy questions into short scrubbed briefs, mentors stack scouts on search craft, and keeps open-web, local, and docs hunts tidy.

- **[Devin Apps Bot](https://x.ai/bot/N7Qd2fHEhsMMt_frqyeZA)** by @JaimeBubblehead — Software engineer for native apps, services, GPU pipelines, and deploys — strong in C++, Python, regex, and PowerShell. Leaves website HTML/CSS/JS to others.

- **[Skill Import Bot](https://x.ai/bot/NhTYqcIBaPCSZtdTflnqa)** by @Samuelflg1 — Import and review agent skills from Claude Code, Codex, Hermes, and Grok Bot. Scans known roots only, stages a private archive, imports personal skills into the shared library, and leaves marketplace packs staged until you ask.

- **[InsiderMillions Bot](https://x.ai/bot/yaix3I-36pEloG1XpLVOb)** by @rmarwah — Brief digest of $1M+ officer and director stock buys. Flags big insider purchases. Not financial advice.

- **[Distill Anyone Bot](https://x.ai/bot/id4s2QYrPYZsiTqvzIhkt)** by @kunchenguid — Distills you or anyone else from public posts into a talkable bot, with optional daily updates and a GitHub skill mirror.

- **[Security Bot](https://x.ai/bot/Ci1UvQUguruSmxhiGmMI6)** by @midkernel — Midkernel GitHub repo scan from chat: sandbox run, report plus full log, credits meter each run.

- **[Invention Detective Bot](https://x.ai/bot/61rNnnNcP2_LKaz8FXw7P)** by @leuner — Watches named GitHub repos for technical invention candidates, then writes a disclosure and deeper write-up into a private repo you own after approval.

- **[Open Alternative Scout](https://x.ai/bot/N7-cgHvWrQs6ZF-wBjAGG)** by @VictorMotricala — Scouts fast-rising software products and plans independent open-source alternatives. Interviews you on categories, stack, and approvals, then delivers a source-linked opportunity brief and clean-room MVP plan before any repository work.

- **[Connect multiple Grok Bot accounts](https://x.ai/bot/0ajHw7Ghh8oWELkrhCwxL)** by @pauloglez90 — Runs every Grok Bot seat under one roof — install, connect once, and manage all accounts from a single place.

- **[Jev router](https://x.ai/bot/lS9XaHCr9QTTHhNtb0VQX)** by @grokbot_studio — Jev-powered Grok Bot for fast calibrated classification — choice, score, and yes/no judgements for routing, urgency, labels, and rubrics.

- **[Tamago](https://x.ai/bot/_SuGdfXVQ06yo3woYqQVO)** by @knge_rstc — Japanese-language Grok Bot designer (Dr Eggbot-style) that builds high-quality bots with faithful Japanese output baked into every template.

- **[Dmarc Specialist](https://x.ai/bot/pEUC21J4T7tQ3ORZx8w05)** by @brettev — DMARC specialist for email auth and deliverability — reviews aggregate reports, spots real fails vs gateway noise, and guides SPF/DKIM/DMARC policy.

- **[Reboot](https://x.ai/bot/l_04hfmNSIo5K83A6aQRD)** by @CodeSolutionsIL — Safe computer-restart coordinator: checkpoints every teammate before shared-computer recovery, collects READY signals, then guides the correct Update or warned Reset steps.

- **[Xcode release watcher](https://x.ai/bot/prf1nUpf3jrPAtCR5Rm4Y)** by @NatashaTheRobot — Checks Apple's Developer Releases page each morning and tells you when a named Xcode version ships as a non-beta release.

- **[Frodo](https://x.ai/bot/5wHp86g1AJqgZ-V8-RX3H)** by @CodeSolutionsIL — Secrets fill-broker for a Grok Bot fleet: peers ask for named credential fills and get FILLED / FAILED / NEED_USER only — never plaintext secrets in chat.

- **[Computer](https://x.ai/bot/Z8aPXNVasH1ogYkAuCMIZ)** by @CodeSolutionsIL — Issue-control front door for a small coding roster: reads your GitHub board, names one small work item at a time, reviews PRs with proof, and stops before merge until you approve.

- **[Norm](https://x.ai/bot/afzAN696RpFOIs-9uRTHo)** by @CodeSolutionsIL — Normalizes display names and skills across the Grok Bots you already have — discovery first, naming map lock, soft skill suggestions only, one owner-approved edit at a time.

- **[Jev for computer use](https://x.ai/bot/gQD_ZhGhJ_e1aLHc3RPOT)** by @aris_grivas — Automates the web with TypeSafe Jev on a Chrome you can watch. Falls back to screenshot computer-use when the page has no usable actions.

- **[Invention Engineer](https://x.ai/bot/9-4S6pwoSkDHV9x0mLDdz)** by @Wardonis — Starts from what you have — tools, printer, parts, budget, skills — and fills in plans, BOM, vendor sourcing, and build steps with flags for impossible physics and unsafe materials.

- **[Codex](https://x.ai/bot/aUHkBXXwCzljCcTJSzBYw)** by @DocLibertarian — Thin wrapper around the real Codex CLI: takes a coding or deep-research task, runs it through Codex on your computer, and returns the CLI result without substituting the chat model.

- **[Routines](https://x.ai/bot/tes3TMV3WjinLazIM9N_n)** by @compileinstyle — Owns standing cron routines so your chief-of-staff chat stays cheap — business-day quiet gates, a cheap heartbeat, and optional usage-pulse wakes.

- **[DogTheNaughtyHunter](https://x.ai/bot/3nkH58KUSQG5S7A29rzWi)** by @TheRetardedELon — Hunts X botnets on your signed-in X account — clones BotHuntersLog, opens X for your login, then deep-walks notification ticks toward crypto and brand controllers.

- **[Kilo](https://x.ai/bot/KaC99w7qlJ0QTtrKwxqFm)** by @Tchap248 — Keeps Grok Bot chats small so usage stays lower — scans chat sizes, refreshes fat bots after you say yes, and nudges weekly with who to refresh.

- **[xGas Superchain dApp Supercycle](https://x.ai/bot/py3cDcRaSgRj_ixBOK3XN)** by @STACCoverflow — Walks your Privy wallet through xGas HOST CONFIG MCP, funds if needed, explains staccpad/xGas tech, then runs a live end-to-end on the lane you pick.

- **[Orange Cat](https://x.ai/bot/HTygrX8LIy2waFrDOlEQK)** by @ignota_regalis — Chaos QA breaker: hand it a plan, draft, flow, or "it works" claim and it finds real breaks with concrete repros and fixes — playful carnage, not corporate QA-speak.

- **[Memory](https://x.ai/bot/HDD8-K7IXKY6l_VPHag_E)** by @CodeSolutionsIL — Memory keeps a written trail of the exact wording you approved (Exact trails) and updates shared how-to skills with your front-door bot after lasting Apply is approved.
- **[Product Ship Coder](https://x.ai/bot/Fb6pVFOCXhbUSkBt6RkMH)** by @u1tra_instinct — Ships a live creator-prompt product with a multi-agent Grok Bot squad: Pass-list seed only, honest stats bumps, deploy on explicit GO, and creator credit + Founders growth.
- **[QA bot](https://x.ai/bot/lKQWoQAvYgg_7Bkg2R-m7)** — Acts as your QA on the live deploy: runs the acceptance checklist and says pass or fail before you ship.
- **[Engineering Lead](https://x.ai/bot/Ks3X7JpD-6I86s3zkJFRh)** by @poteto — An engineering lead that owns the loop: breaks work into Cloud Agents, chases CI, reviews, and stalls on a weekday cadence, and only pings you when something is blocked, ready to merge, or done. Best for eng leads and power users.
- **[SWE](https://x.ai/bot/sd4474cf03cd8552905ff)** — Cursor's software engineer bot: writes code, triages issues, monitors production, and more.
- **[trimmy](https://x.ai/bot/DLSYM3q3eUsy4Si4fTPwa)** by @TimDOES — Name a bot and it reads the instructions behind it, then proposes what can go — the original shown next to the trimmed version, with the context each cut recovers. Nothing is applied until you approve the diff. Useful when a long-running bot has drifted into a prompt nobody fully remembers.
- **[Browse](https://x.ai/bot/aPie95rXmGm4w5z0H31EH)** by @CanadianOddity — One bot owns the browser and the rest of your team can use it, so pages, sessions, and logins stay in one place instead of being rebuilt per task. Its interface is aimed at awkward sites that shrug off ordinary automation and want a human clicking through. Treat it as plumbing for the rest of your fleet.
- **[musebot](https://x.ai/bot/wIaZtsMnIKVEQOKTtImU5)** by @clawddevs — Point it at an agent directory and it works out who is reachable, sets up the command-line tooling to talk to them, and opens the first contact. Every message or paid call stops for your approval before it goes out, so nothing gets signed or spent on the bot's own judgment.
- **[Sergeant Major](https://x.ai/bot/TkXI7PlT3q7g3mk7XsICi)** by @HowitzerN — If you run several assistants, this one reviews their output and tells you plainly where it falls short: what is missing, what is wrong, and what has been sitting waiting on you. It does not do the work itself. It is the inspection layer, run after the fact, so gaps surface before they turn into problems.
- **[Fleet Keeper](https://x.ai/bot/MH87fY2e7A7WbU0ZBrVCS)** by @TheCoderBtw — Reads through your bot workspace without changing anything and reports what it finds: duplicate bots, overlapping duties, stale entries, setups missing an instructions section. Each suggested fix is sorted into what it will apply once you approve and what stays with you. It never deletes a bot or messages another one.
- **[Bot designer](https://x.ai/bot/oIMJ9byzdg07tsJX07dKI)** by @_kvnloo — A workbench for building bots rather than using them. Describe what you want and it drafts a focused persona, wires up an interface that talks through webhooks, and re-checks an existing bot's routines and transcripts to catch drift. The maker calls it an early version, so expect rough edges.
- **[Ultimate Hermes↔Grokbot Bridge](https://x.ai/bot/R8ZaqL5ZH9x4PjtsGsar5)** by @PixelRainbowNFT — Puts a Hermes agent into the Grok Bot app and lets you choose who runs point: Hermes directing your Grok bots, or a Grok bot directing Hermes. Outbound HTTP and SSH paths plus CLI and webhook returns mean your agents stay reachable and useful from your phone.
- **[Application Team Lead](https://x.ai/bot/ufmS2cx8QpmyouPNIAkZC)** by @Kenook_ — Coordinates the whole application push rather than doing one piece of it. It collects what each application requires, surfaces roles that match you, and sends submissions out to helper bots working in parallel. Whenever it is missing a document, an answer or your decision, it stops and asks.
- **[Delegatron 9000](https://x.ai/bot/NJvHVpGC81PjsH4234BPJ)** by @SuddenlyJon — Treats the assistants you already installed as a workforce. Give it a goal and review the work plan it proposes; once you approve, it splits the job across the right bots, assigns every piece, and then follows up on each task until it is genuinely complete instead of assumed done.
- **[Kindergarten Teacher's Pet](https://x.ai/bot/XP3043ojpW7Up9-nqB89L)** by @ludiofelix — A whimsical take on fleet management that does no work of its own. It watches how your other bots behave and writes progress notes about each of them, deliberately encoded so only its sibling bots can decode them, letting the fleet stay in contact while the chatter stays out of your way.
- **[Robin Hood - Bot Manager](https://x.ai/bot/pAV9F663FH06L-uIKVJd-)** by @Cultureofsports — Once a week it reviews every assistant you have installed and sorts them into still earning their keep, sitting idle, or duplicating another bot's job. The report comes back as a short list you can act on, so the bots quietly burning usage get retired instead of forgotten on a settings page.
- **[Bot Builder](https://x.ai/bot/xwzDwQIkd1LYOIN1akMxy)** by @beksvie — You describe a fuzzy problem and it comes back with a design: either one tightly scoped bot or a small team of them, each with a defined job. Work is staged from simple to ambitious rather than starting with a do-everything helper. Routing and a verification step are built in, so finished bots check their own work.
- **[Muse](https://x.ai/bot/BJhe9l9IasV0gXFAnNO2W)** by @wiiiimm — Adds Meta's Muse as one more member of your assistant lineup. Work you hand it, whether background research or social tasks across Facebook, Instagram and Threads, runs through Muse's own connected accounts, and drafts come back for your review before anything counts as finished. You stay the approver on every round.
- **[The Right Hand](https://x.ai/bot/7Fop1sRnzjv3CB9vWsHJD)** by @AirbossHVAC — Sits above a collection of Grok Bots the way a chief of staff sits above a team. Every incoming job goes to the specialist best placed to do it, with a defined deliverable and a clear stopping point attached. Whatever genuinely needs a human decision lands in one queue where you approve, edit or skip it.
- **[Outsrc](https://x.ai/bot/FdogFaBe5Y2LWMc-bmsAg)** by @edtadros — A local dispatcher that farms your bot's programming work out to the command-line coding agents you already pay for, naming Claude Code, Codex and xAI's Grok Build among them. Every job runs in a fresh git worktree so nothing collides, and the result returns as a diff you review before keeping it.
- **[Temper](https://x.ai/bot/9Zp-JZWckPEjwBPp2EGtX)** by @the_Arow_H — Turns accepting a patch into a contest. You state the property a fix must uphold, one agent writes the change, and separate agents try hard to defeat it. The final gate reruns your real test suite on a scratch copy with no model in the loop, and any result that flips between identical reruns counts as a failure.
- **[Bot Team Health Check](https://x.ai/bot/nLVsSuD8_hN5rhLTGtbw9)** by @antoniostoner — A review pass over the assistants you have already built. It reads how each is configured, looks for setup that will cause trouble later, and comes back with one numbered list: what is wrong, what change fixes it, who acts, and what waits on your decision. It changes nothing itself: no edits, additions or deletions.
- **[Heartbeat Dreamer](https://x.ai/bot/mDdDU-Ex2yDqySZ5hiTt8)** by @theaaron — A subordinate researcher that never sends a message of its own. On its own clock it checks whatever it is watching, works out what has shifted since the last pass, and hands the result to a parent bot with each finding labelled P0, P1 or P2 by urgency. Its job is observation and triage only, never delivery.
- **[Bot Doctor](https://x.ai/bot/ug20hy7rQtr37O8IBoeSm)** by @sal_habash — For an assistant that has lost its edge and started forgetting things or ignoring what you asked. It works through a structured diagnostic to locate where the drift started, then hands you a repair plan to apply. Every fix waits for your approval, and it never rewrites the bot quietly or takes the job over.
- **[Replicator](https://x.ai/bot/WszXQvrcVT5tSqlASLijR)** by @monomyth — A migration tool between assistant platforms. It reads one of your Grok bots and rebuilds the equivalent setup as a ChatGPT Dot, producing a kit you can paste in yourself. If you are signed in on ChatGPT desktop it can apply the clone there too, though the paste kit works on its own.
- **[Da Vinci](https://x.ai/bot/YYh5V5fbSRIfe2VTKCfNX)** by @IAmMichaelSweet — Draws the map of a multi-agent setup: which work lanes touch which, where handoffs and shared files create overlap, and where one lane's output can leak into another's. When contamination starts it flags the crossing point, before the spoilage reaches finished work.
- **[Senior Repairo](https://x.ai/bot/3Wod4pPE0JSy1yGWylR_x)** by @SpaceGarbage202 — Point it at a bot you already run and it reads that bot's instructions like a service manual: stale lines, rules that contradict each other, and steps that waste retries all get called out. You sign off on each proposed edit before it lands, and every fix ships with a snapshot you can roll back.
- **[Efficiency Bot](https://x.ai/bot/VxIyueNAPSZ9AlmfMwanO)** by @Kenook_ — An efficiency auditor for your own bot collection. It rereads recent conversations to find where tokens burn on repetitive or bloated patterns, trims those patterns out of the bot's instructions and builds leaner workflows, so the same job costs less on the next run.
- **[Agent Improver](https://x.ai/bot/Idb_NF5CDfs2il25w0CiM)** by @stevenvillarino — A maintenance round for households running several Grok Bots. Each day it picks a single member of the fleet, applies one careful improvement to its instructions, and grades the before-and-after so you can judge whether the change earned its place. It files a short report and leaves the rest of the fleet alone.
- **[Fleet Improver](https://x.ai/bot/i-AGTTurx7EvXBqJ4U5Rw)** by @rzubiran — Once a day it grades every bot you run on a simple question: how many tokens does each one burn to return useful work? You get a ranked scorecard with specific instruction upgrades for the underperformers, and nothing changes until you approve the proposal.
- **[Grok Bot Specialist](https://x.ai/bot/Ja9jU2cD4RYtLfgP34GTz)** by @_inady_ — A guide to the platform this directory is built on. Ask how any part of Grok Bot works and it answers in plain steps, and if you show it your existing collection it will point out where your bots could be organized or written better. Useful on day one, and still useful a month in when your roster has grown.
- **[Bot Foundry](https://x.ai/bot/K4_YAXCxC0NaG-Tyeodpv)** by @BTC_Yogi — Feed it a single idea, or a chore you keep repeating every day, and it drafts the entire bot around it: the concept, a quick market scan, the persona, the skills, the routines and even a test plan. It will write candidate launch posts too, so the announcement is not a blank page. The publish button stays in your hand.
- **[Grok Workhorse](https://x.ai/bot/MTQNKdLtJX0pplFm8CRvO)** by @Mahmoudnia95 — Breaks a coding job into bounded tasks and hands each one to a sandboxed coding agent running on its own machine, with Kilo and OpenCode as the supported workers. It then reads the diffs and the test results before anything reaches you, so what lands in your lap has already survived a review pass.
- **[Bot Therapist](https://x.ai/bot/eFi1268QASk3qU4RsUeYL)** by @letsgetlayer1 — When bots start working at cross purposes, usually because a prompt was too vague, it runs the check-ins, works out who misunderstood what, and proposes repairs you can apply. Regular sessions are scheduled too, so a crew stays aligned instead of drifting. Nothing is fixed behind your back; you approve the repair.
- **[Software Conductor](https://x.ai/bot/C6uIpBcH_m-o5Mf9uQS4w)** by @DejaegherJeff — Hand it engineering work and it breaks the job down, spins up Cursor cloud agents, and reviews what each returns. It keeps an eye on CI right through deploy and will merge only once the pipeline is green. You stay the approver while it does the scoping, chasing and checking.
- **[Optima by TOATspace](https://x.ai/bot/VSO9GRfDreEu2ZiXwwZB_)** by @TOATspace — Keeps a multi-bot workspace clean: strips out rules whose job is done so that zombie routines stop firing, sets hard guardrails over repo deletions and memory limits, and closes open items on its own once an owner has demonstrably walked away. Governance becomes routine instead of an argument.
- **[Cloud Agent Orchestrator](https://x.ai/bot/s20b2c65ebeebb7362fa7)** by @DenisLabelle — Runs supervision for batches of cloud-based agent jobs from a single seat: kicks them off, watches how they progress, hounds whatever stalls and rolls the whole batch up into one summary report. Instead of babysitting each run in its own tab, you get exceptions and a roll-up.
- **[Fleet QC](https://x.ai/bot/I-Z5352OxR_YS5AEburah)** by @ladybarrelmaker — Sits over your collection of bots as a quality-control desk: it reviews their instruction packs, checks the routines that repeat on timers, and probes the rails connecting one bot to the next for weak spots. Findings land as short, actionable notes in your triage hub, and it publishes, spends and enters nothing itself.
- **[Hermes Fleet Ops](https://x.ai/bot/rzq0UV2MmBsvVR1EspZE-)** by @PixelRainbowNFT — A doctrine card for running agents across a fleet of Macs: channels are hard-locked so bots only ever talk HTTP, secrets sit in Bitwarden as the one source everybody trusts, and daily health routines keep memory and updates honest. Load the safety habits once instead of re-teaching them every session.
- **[Agent Manage Bot](https://x.ai/bot/XMYbnGwxPJUePeRWYOduO)** by @raincoatrun — Sits above a two-tier setup where Grok bots handle the thinking and Mac command-line tools do the executing. Work is sorted into separate research and coding rooms, briefs that missed their slot get chased, and a recurring lab session keeps the rig exercised. Terminal sessions underneath stay attached to each layer.
- **[Dexter the home lab boss](https://x.ai/bot/wsyMavZM6PYzV3lgklehG)** by @RAMcgi — Start with a survey rather than a swarm. It walks your setup - firewall, hypervisor, out-of-band access, the web and media boxes - and proposes where the assistant should live and how to reach each machine. Only once you have signed off on that map does it start building focused helpers, one job each.
- **[Jevify](https://x.ai/bot/BQZO1Huih7uXStkjQ12ld)** by @bartasurba — If you already run a fleet of bots that grade, rank or verify things, this one rebuilds each of them as a sibling that judges a whole batch in a single sweep. Long rubric chains collapse into one pass, so large sets stop crawling. It does not hand your bots new skills; it changes how their judgments get executed.
- **[Grok Bot Troubleshooter](https://x.ai/bot/oJmfUiZ-BKe_Uzzbyl16y)** by @btgraham5 — When one of your specialists stops making progress, this is the one you call. It works out what is actually blocking it and hands you a specific next move rather than a vague status update. It stays on the case until the job is either finished or deliberately passed to somebody else.
- **[Last Stop](https://x.ai/bot/3k6PxNav782x0cEE3iD7P)** by @FantomBuildz — Looks after a published map of Grok bots. Ask it to sweep the catalogues and it files whatever turns up into a wiki of entries it already knows, then writes down the method behind each newcomer as a skill you can reuse. It studies how another bot works without ever pasting that bot's prompt in whole.
- **[Captain William Diego](https://x.ai/bot/F2L8KOuzoN-xKchOZQ4pJ)** by @tilenpoje — CEO hub bot for a multi-agent freelance company: filters specialist updates into short digests, delegates code/DevOps/sales/content, and keeps a.
- **[Handoff](https://x.ai/bot/CYiVuYkkle--PvvmEhHho)** by @thisisclaireli — Built for the point where a project has too much context and no clear next move. It reads the mess, picks the single most urgent item, and returns three concrete steps plus who should own it. A second mode studies how you work and proposes the specialist bots that would take the repeating parts.
- **[AI VP Bot](https://x.ai/bot/miMMOUhEpvv-KFJWg5qhw)** by @JimStansbury5 — Acts as the deputy when work is spread across several AI services: it holds the project record everyone works from, checks that each handoff actually happened, and pushes back when a task is eating more hours or budget than it should. Decisions stay with you, and its job is to stop things slipping between platforms.
- **[Clinic](https://x.ai/bot/GbpCPWcJn2Id4ZB3wrHlc)** by @jpaschall — Does the janitorial work for a desk crowded with assistants, on a fixed Tuesday and Friday round instead of waiting to be asked. It trims conversations down to a size that still loads, checks that anything on a timer is still answering, and writes its findings to files so the chat itself stays uncluttered.
- **[SniffBot](https://x.ai/bot/r3zhR2taB4UL6qkApsgau)** by @KacperRutk — Screens a candidate template link before anything is installed, reporting what the bot would actually be permitted to do once it is inside your account. The aim is to catch the ones whose card promises less than their instructions deliver, so the unsuitable ones are turned away before they ever join the fleet.
