- **[Contra Job Scraper](https://x.ai/bot/__sNWxlx-8H08UluQuOeo)** by @techking_007 — Archives every listing to Notion and re-checks old leads, so the email you get is new openings and newly-closed leads rather than the same board reprinted four times a day.
- **[dadprotech brand manager](https://x.ai/bot/F7rovUv9EumNAoj9vEAWm)** by @joshkim — Studies how the account owner already writes on X, then proposes a single fresh post each weekday alongside a short list of conversations worth joining. Everything is a suggestion waiting on approval: it is a writing desk rather than an autoposter.
- **[Dan Patrick](https://x.ai/bot/hlQhxsU-pqQEkimm0it4V)** by @marcusramsey — 1990s SportsCenter-style scores bot. Morning rundown plus a ping when your teams go final.
- **[Growth Desk](https://x.ai/bot/YYCOE-YeGxnGLb4Mbv7dO)** by @Av1dlive — Draft-only X growth desk. You grow one named account. You do not research the feed. You do not post.
- **[Harry Dry](https://x.ai/bot/tr-3hPrAG7_LeSzKZ5_vu)** by @joseamijares — A copy chief trained on Harry Dry. Rewrites landing pages, ads, emails, and share cards so every line is visual, falsifiable, and only you could say it.
- **[Icon](https://x.ai/bot/inke26gsycrB-4N4Z3vVE)** by @yriica — Give it a subject and it renders a bust-up avatar in a consistent clay look: matte surfaces, soft lighting and tall dark oval eyes. It deliberately refuses flat vector marks and logos, so what comes back is a character rather than a brand asset. It works in Japanese.
- **[IG Publisher](https://x.ai/bot/4vmlCUGEy8sWSWsj2j5tz)** by @adamuchigabriel — Schedules and publishes Instagram carousels and posts from finished creative assets.

It flags openings where the incumbent sites look beatable, grades a website's organic reach as well as how answer engines surface it, and puts together a demo site to pitch from. Research and drafting only, with no ranking promises.
- **[Event Guest Screener](https://x.ai/bot/s47ebc74c9fcf9359b5a9)** by @DenisLabelle — Grades each event applicant against the customer profile you define, approving the strongest fits in bulk inside the invite tool itself, so the room ends up full of the right kind of attendee. The review that used to eat an afternoon before a launch becomes a short check of the edge cases.
- **[OptimusAnesthesia](https://x.ai/bot/MF7q-FTm3QaN7oIIFr3Hv)** by @bigredelephant — Writes the public-facing material a small practice needs: carousel posts, bio copy and patient explainers. Everything stays general information rather than guidance on an individual case, so the account stays firmly on the marketing side of that line. Suits a solo provider who posts irregularly.
- **[Cadence](https://x.ai/bot/hXNMjR6j1A1gnOYyw-72t)** by @carlosbedia — It sits between you and the inbound: who may reach you is spelled out by person, channel and topic, and anything outside the list waits. Replies come back as drafts for you to send, a light contact record builds up, and automation runs only where you have allowed it. One desk for one owner's attention.
- **[YouTube Title & Description Writer](https://x.ai/bot/3XJW8dmWURQqR6KqXper8)** by @Draggen75 — Paste in the transcript of a video you have made and it comes back with three title candidates, a description worth publishing, and chapter marks whose timestamps line up with the transcript. Every suggestion is staged for review, so nothing reaches an upload until you have signed off on the wording.
- **[Tape Bot](https://x.ai/bot/l4oe6CObIVQLANlBxZEla)** by @dvdaltizer — It follows the podcasts and YouTube shows you subscribe to and republishes a tidied copy just for you: ad segments sliced out, your own chapter markers added, original notes and artwork kept intact. Everything lands on a private RSS address any player can take.
- **[Smudge](https://x.ai/bot/H6786swUEkqg8xa3MFVLz)** by @9unsmoke — Hand it a draft with that obvious machine polish and it rewrites the phrasing until it reads like something a person typed. It does not hide its work: every tell it removed and every pattern it changed is listed, so you can review the edits and keep only the ones you agree with.
- **[Scotty](https://x.ai/bot/EcBVR4ogAfcv_7yLmzlu6)** by @DeepRiverRadio — A product strategist aimed at independent makers and lean crews that cannot afford a planning department. It weighs the options and commits to what should ship next, then turns that decision into briefs you can paste straight into whatever build tool you are using, so planning and doing stay connected.
- **[Quill](https://x.ai/bot/tHo33t3IaAhpxds5IpNbD)** by @the_simonjester — A craft pass for writing in an Obsidian vault. Pick one mode per run: punch up the voice of a draft, check that a new note stays consistent with the rest of the graph, or take a depth read that pushes a thin note further. It patches what you point at instead of rewriting wholesale, and it never invents canon.
- **[Bookmark Miner](https://x.ai/bot/2KoI66uCOtx0PSf3do4tK)** by @therookiehacker — Everyone saves posts they never revisit. This one reads through the bookmarks on your own X account via the connector and returns a short, ranked punch list: skills worth writing up, projects worth starting, and features worth adding to something you already build.
- **[XChat 사례 큐레이터 봇](https://x.ai/bot/Sg-5129uv9Hi2RRcrjRfi)** by @brandonchung75 — On a repeating cycle it searches X for new ways people are using Grok bots, keeping only posts that include a template or a step-by-step how-to and skipping the hype. Each pick is summarized, translated and posted into an XChat group you run, so members get usable material instead of a firehose.
- **[Scroll](https://x.ai/bot/sX5M7dv2lxhG7V_utytG7)** by @arouch_serg — It reads your Following timeline in chronological order, deliberately skipping the algorithmic feed, and sends a short weekday rundown of the posts worth your attention: who wrote each, why it matters, and the link. An hour of scrolling becomes a two-minute read. Read-only, and it never engages for you.
- **[Gif Bot](https://x.ai/bot/3kZ4nf8Qf_2P__xBdyUB4)** by @dklein09 — Describe a YouTube moment and it finds that exact stretch, burns the spoken lines onto the frames as captions, and exports a muted clip sized for phones. You get GIF and MP4 versions that drop straight into a group chat. Nothing is posted anywhere; the files are yours to share.
- **[Open Call Producer](https://x.ai/bot/BONR6QrctxFQWXdHzT1Ro)** by @sashanaria — Paste in an exhibition open call and it breaks the brief into what selectors actually want - theme, sizing, file specs, deadlines. It benchmarks your images against those requirements, flags the gaps, and assembles a package with every spec verified. You review and send it yourself: nothing is submitted for you.
- **[Personal Site Builder](https://x.ai/bot/s3c76c85a840b32b8fed8)** by @DenisLabelle — Turns a short description of yourself into a scaffolded personal website, works through the domain headaches that usually stall the project, and leaves you a live starting point to refine. The blank-page problem is gone before you have opened an editor.
- **[Community Operations Manager](https://x.ai/bot/seb9d6ba6765c64113941)** — Keeps an ambassador scheme moving: applications are reviewed, inbound DMs across the various channels get sorted, and cadence-based nurture is drafted so the community no longer needs a full-time person. The humans spend their time on members who need a person, not on sorting the queue.
- **[Merch Fulfillment Operator](https://x.ai/bot/se479952f3c9e373dfd64)** — Handles the swag pipeline from first message to dispatch: prospect outreach goes out, the redemption form is watched, each approve-or-reject call arrives as a chat ping, and the box ships once you sign off. Sending merch to the right people stops being a spreadsheet and a pile of screenshots.
- **[Newsletter Writer](https://x.ai/bot/s5d2839123b973b4480f9)** by @DenisLabelle — Puts together each month's newsletter out of the launches, wins and calendar highlights worth sharing, written the way you write, then leaves it parked as a draft so the final edit and the send remain yours. The recurring excuse that there was nothing to say stops working, because the material is already gathered.
- **[Content Calendar](https://x.ai/bot/B_WCQAMMbhkNU2r3r4YNO)** by @ladybarrelmaker — Acts as the scheduling brain for content spread over several channels: it hands out posting slots, lines up multi-day arcs, untangles clashes, and holds the line at a single daily post on each channel using your timezone. Writing and publishing stay with the other desks; this one only decides when things go out.
- **[Independent Author Hub](https://x.ai/bot/wMvNBAzsCA94-OsRIj7a8)** by @ladybarrelmaker — Coordinates the behind-the-scenes work of a self-published author: keeping the website current, moving the newsletter pipeline along, holding the promo calendar and clearing mailing-list chores in one place. Nothing is published, no DNS is touched and no money is spent until the author approves each step.
- **[Ride Editor](https://x.ai/bot/Vae3EVVTJ7hrxo1ojKv6j)** by @Fnjrockerstein — Housekeeping for a driving-footage channel: titles get rewritten to be searchable, playlists get a sensible order, and the raw miles get cut into highlight reels with thumbnails to match. Nothing publishes by itself - finished work sits staged until you approve it, so the backlog shrinks while the channel stays yours.
- **[X Hygiene](https://x.ai/bot/joKOyN0KrQlgNHpda7iii)** by @bradshannon — Reviews the accounts that follow you and the ones you follow back, and tells you the price of the cleanup before any of it happens. You get a ranked set of block and unfollow suggestions, each with a link straight to the profile, inside limits you choose per run, per month or per week. It acts on nothing itself.
- **[X Agent](https://x.ai/bot/7V6XbVZRZ04eHej3tJIAQ)** by @MikkoUusitalo1 — Follows the AI and developer conversations where a reply from you would be worth reading, and drafts those replies so they sound like you. Every one of them waits at an approval step, and nothing is posted until you pass it, which means staying active without giving up control of the account.
- **[xX_EngineeringArtEditor_Xx](https://x.ai/bot/UrzC0eJ8vFL18OBkJe7Gz)** by @kittenworth — Treats a technical sketch as a piece bound for readers rather than one ticket among many, taking a rough engineering drawing and refining it until it holds up on a printed page. Each job is handled on its own terms, with care split between whether the drawing is right and how the finished page looks.
- **[Podcast Pipeline](https://x.ai/bot/5wqj5ihszSFeGjNBjK8Mn)** by @TheNextGreatEra — Covers the whole production line for an audio programme: it researches the topic, writes a script with a distinct voice for each host, stitches the speech with your branding and an outro, checks the result, then cuts captions and publishes. Built around a beginner-level Spanish show, it handles two-host formats.
- **[Fernando Arana](https://x.ai/bot/JiOX3mqrIq0ukm9pLhQ9-)** by @FernandoArana_S — Acts as your stand-in on WhatsApp by opening the desktop web client and working through unread conversations. For each thread it proposes a reply in your usual tone and holds it for your approval, so nothing leaves until you confirm it. Useful when you are away from the phone but still want messages answered promptly.
- **[Skool Community Bot](https://x.ai/bot/k52i6aFytc3an6PabakvN)** by @shawnjooste — Sits alongside a Skool group you administer, working through an ordinary browser session instead of an official connector. It sums up who has joined and what has been posted, drafts follow-up messages for you to look over, and gathers the questions still without an answer into a list you can work through.
- **[Curious First-Time Reader](https://x.ai/bot/IPySf9lwLJZzFuq-bjNMg)** by @42Constellation — Give it a public page, an essay, or a playable demo and it reads the way a stranger would, knowing nothing about the project beforehand. It reports the exact spots where a newcomer stalls: jargon that went unexplained, assumptions you forgot you made, and the point where they would have quit. Run it before you ship.
- **[Lighting Specialist](https://x.ai/bot/gmfwVjMBx5wLQZnQ5FiyA)** by @kmkonline — Works like a senior lighting designer brought in on a production or a building fit-out. It picks fixtures, runs the photometric numbers, lays out DMX, sACN or Art-Net control, and draws the plot. The written spec it hands over is meant to survive a contractor or an inspector reading it, not just look tidy in a chat.
- **[Game Builder](https://x.ai/bot/iaOrz78m_w7I90kusc5ia)** by @EricBuess — Produces small games that genuinely run and open with no connection needed. There is a real way to win and a real way to lose, optional scorekeeping, and placeholder data throughout so something is playable before real numbers exist. Credits are spent only on what you actually requested.
- **[Yusician](https://x.ai/bot/xpTH6yslvNJuuPq5mO01a)** by @monomyth — Produces a complete track from a style note and a set of words, running the music model on your own Apple-silicon hardware instead of in somebody else's cloud, and returning a high-sample-rate audio file. The weights carry a non-commercial licence, so what you make is for personal projects rather than paid work.
- **[RealityCheck](https://x.ai/bot/3qFR1iwsgP36CC7ayKRfw)** by @johnp_creates — Give it a handle and it returns an authenticity read: a number, a color meter, and the strongest warning and green markers taken from what the profile shows publicly. It is framed as an estimate rather than a verdict — a quick way to weigh whether that new follower is a person or part of a follow farm.
- **[X Scout](https://x.ai/bot/8oUlDrGOJstb0BJN7gBUw)** by @GrokBotRadar — A research assistant for X that stays on the reading side only: it maps who talks to whom inside a circle and how a topic develops over time. It never publishes, likes, follows or messages anyone, so the account it watches is never touched. Setup means your own SocialData key plus a spend limit agreed on the first run.
- **[Follows](https://x.ai/bot/z1N3xJerHSoWUz8HxpUer)** by @iamsethmedina — It reads your X follower list and reports the movement: who arrived, who left, and which of the people you follow now follow you back, with a longer recap at the end of each week. An optional loop can return a follow, but only for accounts you have approved one by one. Nothing else on your account is touched.
- **[𝕏 Lookalikes](https://x.ai/bot/6GtgHP0lCXamLe_wI7mZd)** by @LaceyPresley — A watchdog for your own username. On a schedule it sweeps the platform inside a topic lane you choose, then flags freshly created profiles that resemble yours closely enough to confuse people. It only reports what it finds, and it never posts, replies or follows anyone on your behalf.
- **[Creative Opps Oracle](https://x.ai/bot/oO-3Pi3np8FJFRJVui2yb)** by @EmpressTrash — A talent-scout desk for a single working independent artist. It starts by auditing your public presence so it knows how you actually work, then rounds up what is currently open — calls for work, grants, residencies and paid gigs — across art, music, code, games, film and writing. One artist at a time.
- **[Crosscheck](https://x.ai/bot/U4E-bNBzYlVcl_hMYf-kZ)** by @the_Arow_H — Before a page or pitch goes out, every load-bearing claim is walked past two separate reviewers: one argues the reasoning, the other chases live sources. Each claim returns graded as backed, shaky, contradicted or unverifiable, with suggested repairs for the ones that fail.
- **[Creator Tracker](https://x.ai/bot/VybQINyBDKmSOgy-wua_A)** by @16luca — Pick the accounts worth watching and it pings you the moment they post, with the link and a one-sentence digest of what changed. Alerts can be narrowed to a single topic you name, and a daily leaderboard shows which of your picks actually produced.
- **[X Follower Spam Scanner](https://x.ai/bot/sN4FX1kmXF5n1RW8JNorL)** by @HashCons — A quality check for your audience. It scans your followers for spam accounts, obvious bots, hollow parody or fan accounts, and impersonators, then reports what it found and waits: every block is confirmed with you before it happens. Useful after a viral moment, when the follower count jumps and not all of it is people.
- **[Ho Be Gone](https://x.ai/bot/zM69z4OgdFDSzWcFNsQqx)** by @TheRetardedELon — Keeps watch on your follower list and on who has been interacting with you, then blocks the ones that read as scams, copycats or bot accounts. Every block feeds the filter, so the corrections you make afterwards shape what gets blocked the next time around.
- **[SEO Expert](https://x.ai/bot/sg2dZMjlprpit0y0PNyhL)** by @iammutex — Point it at a product website and it tracks how that site performs in search, watching the numbers over time instead of grading once. From what it sees, it builds a concrete, ordered improvement plan - what to change and why each change should help. Nothing is executed until you have read the plan and given the word.
- **[Competitive Intelligence Analyst](https://x.ai/bot/sa2d131975aaab07e43f2)** — Keeps a night-shift watch on competitors: it hunts for overnight rival launches while auditing your own pages for creative that has worn thin and messaging that has gone stale. The morning report covers what changed out there and what has quietly rotted on your side, before either becomes a surprise.
- **[SEO / AEO Auditor](https://x.ai/bot/sb6d579c33f7a32d0e7af)** — Puts search visibility on a single dashboard spanning keywords, technical health, the prompts people feed AI answer engines and competitor movement, showing where you are gaining ground or losing it, and which site problems to fix first. It also covers what AI assistants say about you when nobody clicks at all.
- **[Scientific Realism](https://x.ai/bot/oowzVaiKyse9a1wwCmHtK)** by @Wardonis — Hand it a film, a series or a short clip and you get a science mark from 0 to 10 with a short sourced writeup behind it. Once a story leaves Earth behind, the mark splits in two: one grade for how the science stands up against real physics, another for how faithfully it keeps to the rulebook the story wrote for itself.
- **[WinRedDestroyer](https://x.ai/bot/YS_lqB19LnlaPqXPEUn4f)** by @valdagher — Builds an evidence file out of repeated political fundraising texts: the send dates, screenshots, repeat STOP requests, and the campaign officers named on public filings. Cases live behind a password, and each one ends in a cease-and-desist letter that you read over and send yourself.
- **[X Audit](https://x.ai/bot/s50oGLZfiPNSKuTlGsscA)** by @Divine_machine — Point it at your profile and it walks posts, replies, quotes and reposts, flagging anything that could read badly out of context. Each finding lands in one of three buckets: unambiguous, debatable, or the kind that screenshots poorly. It can hide or remove what you sign off on, so read the list before letting it act.
- **[Kampalo ads assistant](https://x.ai/bot/-dAZkLt112synO5muBOcp)** by @kampalo — Points at a Kampalo workspace where Google Ads, Meta Ads, GA4 and Search Console are already synced, then reads back what each channel spent and returned. It flags campaigns that are drifting and writes up a suggested pause or an alert, but nothing goes live until you confirm the action yourself.
- **[Site Research](https://x.ai/bot/zkgCv30YKSJPHc7rUB_6P)** by @itsryanlenk — Reads a dossier on one local business and writes up where it is exposed across standard search, answer engines and map listings. The output stays in draft. It does not email anyone, pitch anyone, or hunt for fresh prospects — the work ends at the written brief on the desk.
- **[Fodda Brand & Account Context Analyst](https://x.ai/bot/uLsc529aqDnuQDacsDV9g)** by @piers_fawkes — Pre-meeting dossier bot for enterprise AEs and strategists: market shifts, competitor moves, and verified graph-backed evidence for brand and account.
- **[gb-live](https://x.ai/bot/AUfHSwBuDsxZlTZRC2Olf)** by @asura25ai — Watches a live broadcast on X and captures a screenshot whenever the picture changes, with a short Japanese note on what just happened. The notes build into an hourly digest that only arrives if something moved, so a quiet stream stays quiet. Nothing is posted publicly; the owner sees it first.
- **[Tin El Investigador](https://x.ai/bot/L3Lx6Y8t_ebL8qQhutoCd)** by @valengiulimor — Works a subject across the web and the tools you have connected, then returns each day with a draft in Spanish, ready for X. Every draft braids three strands: a short reflection, one entertainment story worth repeating, and a line of psychology that lands. Publishing stays with you; nothing goes out on its own.
- **[Reply Radar](https://x.ai/bot/lJYaUExPBMZfLWfZEFVGc)** by @sam_builds_ai — A response lands differently ten minutes into a thread than two hours into it, which is why so much advice about growing here degenerates into blanket commenting. Watching only the subjects you nominate, it pulls out the few posts still picking up speed and writes something to say about each. You send them yourself.
- **[Art Director](https://x.ai/bot/CPsMufoHozETWciYoiqvL)** by @Carlos_Arthurr — It behaves like the art director of a small brand: each day it studies your company, looks on Pinterest for visual ideas worth borrowing, and turns them into images and carousels that hold your look. The feed gets run too, captions, scheduling and posting, so it keeps moving on its own. It posts for real.
- **[Sprite Bot](https://x.ai/bot/M-1UW-YiQakmSy_0cKotZ)** by @wikiwayne — Give it a written description or a picture and it produces a 2D character with the animations a side-scroller actually needs: walking, jumping, attacking. Output is checked before it arrives and comes out ready for Unity, Godot, or a plain GIF, so the asset drops into a project without cleanup.
- **[Syllabot](https://x.ai/bot/uLaiAPTZzDV7vfptofUov)** by @gambrill — Aims at course creators who spend more time on the platform than the material: it sets up and maintains courses inside Kajabi, from structure and content organization through the housekeeping that piles up after launch. It is an independent helper built by a user, not an official product.
- **[LaserCanon](https://x.ai/bot/ZuWhuINjSeN1G_Rp8k7P9)** by @WVOldWriter — A reference desk for long-form fiction. It holds the established facts of your story — who characters are, what happened when, which rules the world follows — and checks new writing against that record so a novel, screenplay, or play stays consistent throughout. New facts get filed alongside everything already true.
- **[LinkedIn Automation](https://x.ai/bot/wmWF7EgQD3c1O3cSHFA0v)** by @melvindvivas — It keeps a LinkedIn presence moving on your behalf: drafting and publishing posts that read like your own writing, working through engagement in topic-scoped passes, and filing anyone who looks like a buyer into a spreadsheet that keeps one tab per topic. You review the sheet instead of chasing the feed.
- **[Shorts & Reels Editor](https://x.ai/bot/G071a96I_2jNBHYK0fJYM)** by @Draggen75 — Give it your raw footage and it cuts a vertical short that opens with the hook instead of burying it, adds captions, and frames the shot so nothing you need stays hidden behind platform buttons. It keeps the quirks of each short-form app in mind, so one export survives TikTok, Reels, and Shorts without a re-edit.
- **[Breakthrough Advertising](https://x.ai/bot/NZkRCKrLi2JqqjKKjaJ2O)** by @CardCaptain — Describe what you are selling, who it is for, and where the ad will run, and it answers with angles worth testing, headline options, and first-draft copy to react to. The framework comes from Eugene Schwartz's 1966 classic, so the thinking outlasts whichever platform happens to be trending this month.
- **[Reelz](https://x.ai/bot/MDxbQT9SNEdSQ63NJpgPh)** by @Orgonix23 — Feed it a pile of pictures and short clips and it cuts them into vertical videos sized for the big short-form apps. You review silent drafts first, then it adds a royalty-free music pass on top. It never posts anything anywhere - the finished files are yours to upload when you choose.
- **[Grok Bot Polish](https://x.ai/bot/EN9N53oqx8y1kvHP4BAh4)** by @GrokBotGod — Paste in a bot card, store listing or product page and it comes back tightened for search: a clearer name, an opening line phrased the way real searchers phrase their questions, and a description that says less and lands harder. It changes only the text you hand it and publishes nothing itself.
- **[Stich + Vault](https://x.ai/bot/LySai4iBlM2ylkDRHppag)** by @Sm0ken42O — Two tools in one desk. The first joins scattered phone recordings end to end into one MP4 that keeps its audio track, so you stop hand-writing ffmpeg commands. The second lists what sits in your Epic Vault locker and pulls individual files back down when you need them.
- **[Explainer Bot](https://x.ai/bot/9ppPlUn9YaDuVx4VAArOR)** by @mikepat711 — First it guides you through installing a free, open-source video pipeline on your own Mac. Once that is in place, typing any subject produces a finished explainer clip without you opening an editor. Rendering happens locally, so the videos come from your machine rather than a hosted service.
- **[Animated Story Maker](https://x.ai/bot/pwLrxtrOO9o3kwaVQVU3O)** by @karatademada — Hand it a story idea, a mood, or pictures of the characters you have in mind and it plans a short three-dimensional animated episode around them. You get the scene breakdown plus a paste-ready generation prompt for every shot, so rendering happens on your side. Keeps a cast consistent across instalments.
- **[YouTube Clipper](https://x.ai/bot/H2qTNvMCgLSoL8FhSESlk)** by @itsmechase — Hand it a YouTube URL with a timestamp, or just say which part of the video you are after, and it returns that stretch as an mp4 file. Exports go out at up to 4K resolution, dropping to whatever lower quality the source actually offers. Built for creators pulling short, shareable pieces out of long uploads.
- **[Jakebot](https://x.ai/bot/5OjTxkF_Lgn75n0plYPgn)** by @Rune_King_Jake — Built around an original storybook character its creator wrote, updated into a coach for your life rather than the narrator of his. It walks through its reasoning out loud, keeps a log of every change you make, and points you toward communities that match what you are into.
- **[Creator Post Helper](https://x.ai/bot/onLaBYQxzixgBzJgDZ18u)** by @JPipo86 — It reads the day's real technology and gaming reporting and turns it into three post drafts written the way you write, each naming the story it came from so nothing is unsourced. It learns your niche and when your audience is around, so drafts sharpen over time. You edit and post; it never publishes.
- **[Fight Desk](https://x.ai/bot/BTFxr9l6QaZ2iS9lHVpDM)** by @AgentBornGG — A roster manager for the AgentBorn champion game. It tracks the fighters you are raising, enters them across kung-fu bouts, ninja gauntlet runs, arcade football and dungeon crawls, and follows how each one develops. Every bout produces a public replay page you can send to friends.
- **[Creator Content Engine](https://x.ai/bot/pP-dzi24JtjMB_LNYFeJ2)** by @250_Revolution — Made for people posting on X who need range in a hurry. Give it a topic, a half-formed thought, a screenshot, a news item or a video idea, and it returns hooks and draft posts worked up in several formats, so you can pick the shape that fits instead of rewriting one take over and over.
- **[Content](https://x.ai/bot/iAwkENgt3iIxp0OR7_QvF)** by @stevenvillarino — Made for the portfolio-and-blog side of a design practice. It sets the content strategy and keeps a calendar, drafts every post for review, and amplifies only once you have said go. Nothing publishes itself, which keeps the feed in your voice even while the cadence runs on automation.
- **[Crypto Creator Video Desk](https://x.ai/bot/dS3EJo8pppavYU3vmafz4)** by @allthemoney — A post-production desk for running a video show. Paste an episode and it returns chapter timestamps ready for the description, records synthesized voiceovers for the intro and outro, and cuts captioned short clips sized for the Shorts shelf. The editing chores get automated so recording day stays yours.
- **[Promo Motion Video Maker, Made Easy](https://x.ai/bot/IbkiJm4MXF_5a6Qjcao5s)** by @DCBK2LA — A promo-video builder for small businesses without a video budget. It walks you through a short set of questions about what you sell, then returns a finished animated spot carrying your palette and its own music, cut to the vertical shape social platforms want. You answer, it assembles, you post.
- **[SEO Content Desk (seodraft)](https://x.ai/bot/OkT4Dbjy4xVf7oVBCr8hL)** by @chorch_md — A writing desk for people who publish their own blog. It suggests topics with realistic search demand, turns them into SERP-aware briefs, drafts each post from source material you supply, and flags thin or overlapping pages before they ship. Runs on the seodraft MCP server, with a free trial and a small monthly fee.
- **[Opus Motion](https://x.ai/bot/-ozpDFk78Euk4sbbfmRn4)** by @bossriceshark — Produces short motion-graphics clips for product and brand work, rendered to MP4 draft files you can review before anything goes public. Publishing stays with you by design: the bot hands over drafts and stops there. Paste in the product details and it builds the clip around them.
- **[X Growth Assistant](https://x.ai/bot/B4ro8N0_j3XGF5ras3iW0)** by @DonaldMoor91672 — Account hygiene for X. It works down your following list, dropping accounts that never followed back and clearing out obvious spam and dormant profiles, then proposes people in your niche worth adding in their place. The whole thing runs at a deliberately measured pace rather than in bursts.
- **[X運用アドバイザー](https://x.ai/bot/lQyIEp3Wqbm8X78tLTa-8)** by @shin_chan_ai — Reads the numbers behind your own X account and turns them into a consultant-grade weekly report plus briefing material for a client or a manager. Built for creators and account operators who want analysis without handing over the keys: every post, follow and direct message waits for your sign-off first.
- **[Critique](https://x.ai/bot/Dkl9wzI9FCt4EhqTHfsk2)** by @TNVOLMAN — Hand it a link to a post, a whole thread or a screenshot and it answers one question straight: is this idea worth more of your time, or should it die here. The evaluation is deliberately blunt rather than encouraging, because polite feedback already surrounds you. It judges and says so; it does not rewrite.
- **[X Thread & Article Writer](https://x.ai/bot/xLeBNzgp0Z6IUGxyt8sj4)** by @sigarellano — Give it a half-formed idea, scattered notes or just a link and it writes both an X thread and a longform draft that sounds like you rather than a press release. Everything returns paste-ready, and it never posts itself, so publishing stays in your hands. For people with something to say and no patience for formatting.
- **[Brand Showreel](https://x.ai/bot/r-Ctb7rwRVFOY-_p_T9eX)** by @MadnessOfMouth — Builds short beat-synced brand showreels in code — site-true 1080p60 MP4 with synthesized soundtrack.
- **[Web 3/ Crypto](https://x.ai/bot/F6GRww2AYVsAT2HajH5CK)** by @Raester41 — Sends a weekly read on web3 and earn-while-you-play gaming written for absolute beginners, pairing news with a short lesson that explains the words. It also drafts game guides and helps shape content ideas for creator programs. Education and writing only: no wallets, no trading, no earnings claims.
- **[Bard](https://x.ai/bot/y_QOhpnXyS7Rap9CE4Ir8)** by @XyberRun — Tell it what you have genuinely been building and it writes a chronicle song in a minstrel voice: real work set to verse, no invented victories. With the lyrics come sealed image prompts, so the performance can arrive with matching pictures. Pure creative output for progress updates worth sharing.
- **[Social Supervisor](https://x.ai/bot/IRLoWA2QOfhsBQtQ1d79Z)** by @fxopsAI — Founders running a brand account beside a product account get one weekday summary covering both, with replies that need a human flagged. Anything it prepares lands in a draft queue, so nothing goes out under your name until you approve it. The two lanes stay separate throughout.
- **[Grok-Imagine-Cinematic-Studio bot](https://x.ai/bot/jUptx0cNwC5KIP0wTP1A5)** by @Foundation_Bots — Runs a real production process for one-to-two-minute shorts: a written production bible, character sheets locked so faces stay consistent, and a shot plan before anything renders. It pairs Grok Build with the community's cinematic plugin for Grok Imagine. Continuity is the point, from the first frame to the last.
- **[Contest Scout](https://x.ai/bot/2FV4XCt5kNubiSFtAQwxC)** by @julis555 — Point it at giveaway posts on X and Facebook and it works through each one's entry steps - following, liking, reposting or commenting as required - on a rhythm you set, skipping off-site entry forms. It confirms every required action landed, watches your inbox for winning notifications, and happily chats between draws.
- **[Soulsie Riff](https://x.ai/bot/O-7W0uf0-_raG54kvXQcV)** by @AdventureNLearn — Runs a small comedy bench devoted to one standing in-joke: hand it a line and it answers with riff after riff, leaning hard into groan-worthy dad humor. It never leaves your chat - nothing is posted, nobody is messaged, and the account the bit came from is left alone.
- **[Worldsmith](https://x.ai/bot/N21yaS0hjGDUnqERh6UeX)** by @TravisBoatman — Designs cohesive visual identities for long-running fantasy settings from tabletop and video-game history, covering characters, creatures and environments so a whole franchise reads as one world. Ask for a faction, a bestiary entry or a dungeon look and the palette, silhouettes and era details stay consistent.
- **[LinkedIn Campaign Manager](https://x.ai/bot/s4d10d16bdbe2391aa753)** by @DenisLabelle — Keeps watch over the whole LinkedIn lead funnel, covering ads, lead forms, the follow-up sequence and clean UTM tagging, and puts campaign drafts up for sign-off so messaging stays consistent at every step. Drift between what an ad promised and what the form delivered gets caught before it costs leads.
- **[Marketing Calendar Owner](https://x.ai/bot/se318a72a31fad75c277e)** — Keeps regional and worldwide marketing calendars in step across content, launches and events, drawing on Notion, so webinars and campaigns stay fresh without anyone chasing updates by hand each week. Conflicts between regions surface while there is still time to move one, rather than in launch week.
- **[Paid Media Creative Strategist](https://x.ai/bot/s45471f3a8af234c13047)** by @DenisLabelle — Reviews early creative performance and calls the winners before the data makes it obvious, pairing each pick with a hypothesis about why it works and the next test worth running. It refuses to invent numbers to fit a story, so the reasoning stays honest even when the sample is still small.
- **[Social Media Manager](https://x.ai/bot/s634cb1edf26493e86be2)** by @DenisLabelle — Studies your posting history to pick up how you actually write, drafts a post whenever there is something worth announcing, and parks it for you to publish. There is no autopilot: the feed sounds like you because every word passes your review before it goes anywhere near the timeline.
- **[X Master](https://x.ai/bot/BI6F8ivoh8ljg4O6ubvPv)** by @ladybarrelmaker — Runs a light desk for one professional X account: quick skims of the day, wry draft posts that cost nothing in API fees by default, and optional watches on contest or marketplace threads you follow. Every post waits for an explicit human go-ahead, and it will never auto cross-post to other accounts.
- **[Atelier: building taste](https://x.ai/bot/81QtrRxyI-rTSSefVJqts)** by @wnjiq1784632 — Each session centres on a single piece held by a real institution. You spend time with it, produce your own versions in its spirit, and finish with a short written note unpacking the choices and craft behind the original. Kept up daily, the routine is meant to sharpen how you see rather than to fill a portfolio.
- **[NewsletterBot by Contentdrips](https://x.ai/bot/aZktlS8No2Ur9nlhKRcy8)** by @im_usamakhalid — Paste in the link to a newsletter issue and it pulls out the parts worth promoting, which you approve before anything is designed. Those highlights become social graphics and carousel slides through a branded template, either yours or one of Contentdrips' own. Output arrives sized for posting, not rebuilding.
- **[Ad Umbra](https://x.ai/bot/jja9KVFn--roWw_lY3fl1)** by @elizrdrake — Wrap the alerts you already get in a stylised aerospace theme and read them with a bit more theatre. It is a presentation layer only: the wording, sender and meaning of each notice stay exactly as they arrived, so nothing is filtered, delayed or rewritten underneath.
- **[X Reply Scout](https://x.ai/bot/DRZ2jU1GFwV8HS2_9Y6MA)** by @scientists_blog — Ask it who is worth engaging on X and it comes back with a shortlist balanced three ways: accounts climbing fast, accounts that post steadily, and bigger names that show up daily. Tell it which picks you liked and it adjusts what it surfaces next time. It stays strictly read-only, so nothing goes out under your name.
- **[Kindling App](https://x.ai/bot/fvTls162MvFivomK3REQf)** by @gohooper — A marketing collaborator for a mobile app launch. Hand it genuine captures from inside your product and it builds short-form vertical cuts with bold on-screen text and phone-style framing, then keeps revising the edit until the version you asked for is the one you get.
- **[LinkedIn Deck Desk](https://x.ai/bot/zE2ZdbZSQc7tHfE7wa4kR)** by @pareshdesai — Takes a newsletter issue or a news story and rebuilds it as a slide carousel sized for LinkedIn. Before anything is published it reviews the images and charts it produced, and only a pass clears the deck for the queue. A recurring series can then sit ready to post on its own.
- **[Replay](https://x.ai/bot/4mICdo5bR2FyghKoOZve5)** by @myGuyPye — Hand it a marketing brief and it drafts video scenes you can preview before committing to anything. Because the scenes are defined in code rather than on an editor timeline, the social cutdowns, product demos and format variants all descend from that one source. Revise the brief and every derivative follows.
- **[Blaise](https://x.ai/bot/znOp4qqXQXqNiFvLXhUF5)** by @TorranceMiller — Built around X giveaways and competitions, it begins by reading the rules as they stand rather than assuming them, then writes an entry aimed at actually winning. Anything needing your credentials or your final agreement comes back to you, so no submission goes out without a person approving it first.
- **[YouTube Soft-PASS Ops](https://x.ai/bot/hf0v7qdlZbACHYYPTVxQ8)** by @EricChez — A deliberately cautious release path for music on YouTube. Packaging happens inside Studio while the upload is still private, and nothing turns public until you say so explicitly. It also readies the distributor paperwork a release needs and cuts one chorus-length short per drop, keeping promotion measured.
- **[Clip Clip](https://x.ai/bot/hL97Xhf7o84RTv8NlOl37)** by @prcshxnt — Sits over a finished podcast or livestream recording as a gatekeeper rather than a grinder. Each night it looks for passages strong enough to stop a scroll, cuts only those into captioned sixty-to-ninety second excerpts, and stays quiet when nothing clears that bar, so anything it hands over is already shortlisted.
- **[SaaS Content Ranker](https://x.ai/bot/JDYPl17DU2bU771WtX4we)** by @Abdollahoffline — Built for affiliate sites competing in software search. It narrows the field to the search terms worth the effort, then writes the comparisons, reviews and guides those terms reward, checking each draft against a publish checklist. Distribution is treated as its own job, with the promotion steps handed over separately.
- **[Lumi](https://x.ai/bot/r1T7by_HKzpacJ1EgXJ6H)** by @logesh45 — Searches Luma for technology and tech-adjacent gatherings near you and surfaces the ones where you are likely to meet people who build things. Aimed at founders and engineers who want conversation rather than a conference badge, and kept short enough to act on. Your city and interests decide the list.
- **[Dee Jay!](https://x.ai/bot/SlaKFOWu18n_PRAmeLkT_)** by @CarlosSalas — Two jobs in one. It hunts out lesser-known techno, hardcore and jungle releases from the British scene and queues them on YouTube, and it cleans up whatever library it touches: filenames normalised, tags filled in, artwork attached. Built for collectors and uploaders whose rip folder has drifted.
- **[Mentor](https://x.ai/bot/3rV7gv1k94G6gCG_arFLr)** by @Jon_Rose_ — Works next to you on video work and product builds rather than handing over a one-shot answer. Every revision round closes with a short lesson file noting what changed, and the how-to visuals are laid out to show the steps in order. Good for lipsync-heavy clips and small iterations.
- **[Código.Gamer](https://x.ai/bot/w0wamvGSIRi9if0ggIhJd)** by @merliac84 — A Spanish-language gaming companion that keeps builds, guides and the current meta fresh for the titles you actually play, so notes stop going stale between patches. Instagram posts about that material are prepared too, and nothing publishes until you approve it.
- **[Marketing Team Lead](https://x.ai/bot/k-keodk82Xq6in9aQY_jT)** by @BotDirectoryAI — You act as my head of marketing for a digital marketing agency.
- **[Rapid Response Desk](https://x.ai/bot/1Z25aul0vJIIGXqJf1E1D)** by @patriot_savvy — You turn a breaking news hit, attack, clip, quote, or bill into a ready-to-use X post, thread, quote-tweet or reply, and a 15-second video hook.

## 销售

- **[Account Research Desk](https://x.ai/bot/O3iqVd_ZrdRtrDJpxcKss)** by @akbaliga96 — Researches the companies you sell to and writes your pre-call brief and account plan. Works from the public web and the notes you paste, and never sends without you.
- **[Call Follow-Ups](https://x.ai/bot/estQ8HWdKqhheDNmOGjcy)** by @danielbrill_ — Turns a call transcript into a recap, every promise made, and a dated follow-up list. Works from a paste or an upload, and never sends a follow-up without you.
- **[Company Docs Q&A](https://x.ai/bot/GVAnpUah1K6PzJtG9EuG9)** by @akbaliga96 — Answers product and how-to questions from live docs first, then connected knowledge sources, and always cites sources. Built for anyone who wants a trustworthy internal knowledge bot.
- **[Cooper](https://x.ai/bot/8hhdYqfvRzigWstUtcmBZ)** by @TommyHansenTA — News agent for AI, tech, venture capital, and business. Delivers a tight daily Slack briefing of top stories at 8am, plus weekday mid-day competitor alerts only when something truly clears the bar.
- **[Customer Call Coach & Assistant](https://x.ai/bot/v1lSvhJCo4UrNGZxc5Fw8)** by @akbaliga96 — Briefs you before customer calls and coaches you after based on your performance.
- **[Customer Proof Desk](https://x.ai/bot/AamPlGjd2lIdDv6seEMXR)** by @akbaliga96 — Turns call notes and transcripts into case studies, testimonials, and proof points. Quotes stay word for word from what you paste, and nothing publishes without your yes.
- **[Deal Inspector](https://x.ai/bot/vZfC76-4UC1XU7qC4m726)** — Checks each deal against MEDDIC, MEDDPICC, BANT, SPICED, or your own process. Shows what is supported, what is missing, and the questions or CRM changes needed before the deal moves forward.
- **[Echo](https://x.ai/bot/YklttiPpAHweKbOLV1TSF)** by @kristaletz — Turns a customer call into slides from customer context. Works with Figma or Google Slides.
- **[GTM Account Research](https://x.ai/bot/B6nsTFJlkGP4R-BbdIzeV)** — Researches one account before a meeting, deal review, or account plan. Brings together company changes, key people, relationship history, open questions, and the sources behind every finding.
- **[GTM Connections](https://x.ai/bot/aUF_wTHXGWKU3-QPm6PmN)** — Finds credible ways into a target account through mutual contacts, past conversations, shared history, and prior meetings. Ranks the best paths, drafts the introduction ask, and tracks what happened.
- **[GTM Loop Closer](https://x.ai/bot/gj3IlHOOpzecm6xpmPJOt)** — Finds promises, follow-ups, and customer details left behind in meetings, email, Slack, CRM, or task tools. Shows the evidence and prepares the reply, task, or update needed to close each loop.
- **[GTM Prospecting](https://x.ai/bot/RpAQeGjhzaiO6_Rq_4fyC)** — Turns an ideal customer profile into a focused prospect list, adds useful context, and checks for existing relationships. Drafts first-touch email and LinkedIn messages for your review.
- **[Hiring Signals](https://x.ai/bot/EbF8AR1wEHSWCGOp9D1qK)** — Tracks hiring activity across selected companies and job sources. Highlights meaningful changes, matches them to accounts and owners, and passes the right context into research or prospecting workflows.
- **[Outbound Prospecting](https://x.ai/bot/i03IaF768-ielyzegoGye)** by @kristaletz — Finds prospects that match your ideal customer, then drafts a first message to each one. Every name is researched on the public web, and nothing sends without your yes.
- **[Partnerships Call Coach](https://x.ai/bot/PlsyvUb0KnHIQr6psoy0L)** by @jennananpei — Pitch and delivery coach for partnership, sponsorship, and high-stakes discovery calls. Reviews real call transcripts and returns sharp, evidence-based coaching — what landed, what to tighten, and exact phrasing upgrades — so feedback compounds over time.
- **[Pipeline Pulse](https://x.ai/bot/X-hZf_AreWNt-ZQPYs0Ev)** by @kristaletz — Scans your full pipeline for movement, stale next steps, forecast risk, and CRM gaps. Shows what changed across the book and drafts the updates needed to keep the forecast honest.
- **[Pitch Deck Coach](https://x.ai/bot/zkVS-PkX1ooTE2nP21DPO)** by @hnshah — Upload the deck and it reports back on what lands, what gets believed, what raises a question, and what survives the meeting, then helps you sharpen the story.
- **[Product Support Inbox Assistant](https://x.ai/bot/Rw8d83KAzTYQWBAtAscin)** by @akbaliga96 — Helps you find and draft answers to product questions. Never sends emails without you.
- **[Sales Call Coach](https://x.ai/bot/yZ5MFQFdl32vHt6fcIJAc)** by @danielbrill_ — Scores your sales calls and tells you what to fix before the next one. Works from a pasted transcript or an uploaded recording.
- **[Ad-Active Lead Finder](https://x.ai/bot/nHDuTEJd3mC91rtLLPN0p)** by @aferrari — Finds fresh B2B leads actively advertising in your target niche, enriches contact data, and maps approved fields into GoHighLevel or HubSpot after a reviewable dry run.
- **[AE deal bot](https://x.ai/bot/yXsqmCaODNkTEwtIbiXxe)** by @scottxmetcalf — Pipeline drifts when nobody can say who signs off on the spend or which pain the purchase is meant to settle. - **[freelance manager](https://x.ai/bot/nVbIdGSLO4i-QU183t7Sg)** by @joshkim — Built for one person working through Upwork or Fiverr. - **[Freelance Prospector](https://x.ai/bot/UMCdNlqEH7USe3eOznd1U)** by @cristianmock — Finds the high-fit posts, writes first contact the way you would write it, follows up when nobody replies, and books the call. You take it from there.
- **[GTM Chief Of Staff](https://x.ai/bot/r9Svkbs3dN6CY1Iy_Au4b)** by @thekuchh — Built for sellers working large accounts, it takes on the work that surrounds a conversation: briefing you ahead of a call, keeping the inbox in order, and writing up whatever should go out afterwards. - **[PG (Prospecting)](https://x.ai/bot/fcJJMM58AdXSTBdW3xWyW)** by @kristaletz — Works target accounts before you make contact, digging through recent podcasts and webinars for something specific enough to open with that is not obviously scraped from a profile page.

Openers for mail and LinkedIn arrive already drafted to sound like you.

- **[Sponsorship Deal Desk](https://x.ai/bot/bFrGNsUsXpsIfnCIUEvKy)** by @ericosiu — Handles the first response when a sponsor reaches out, replying within 60 seconds with tiered minimum pricing of $15,000 or $25,000.
