The roster keeps refreshing rather than sitting there as an export that goes stale in a week.

- **[Sales Orchestrator](https://x.ai/bot/AMFF7LG8gxX1bLIH-_D3A)** by @idleshubh — Drop it into a group with your other sales assistants and it answers first, every time. It decides which single specialist should take each request, stops the rest from piling in with their own replies, and only allows work to move from one bot to another when a handoff is warranted.

- **[Gong Call Coach](https://x.ai/bot/KpodhhBqjA4FHv47R1HrD)** by @scottxmetcalf — Works from what was already captured, once the conversation is over. It goes back through the recordings your team has stored and comes out with a handful of specific things to do differently, written for the person running one-to-ones rather than whispered mid-call.

- **[Alibaba Buyer Ops Bot](https://x.ai/bot/ZTFwMWXlScRvLrkHuR3gs)** by @DomenicFotino — Alibaba buyer-ops for any business that sources factories. Onboards your model and tracker, drives the Alibaba UI, files cards, and never pays without your say.

- **[Recruiter Email Finder Bot](https://x.ai/bot/lbf-biMZO02RdXeOBks_-)** by @fwhittington_24 — Finds university, early-careers, and technical recruiter emails at companies you care about, then delivers a company-grouped CSV.

- **[Side Hustle Bot](https://x.ai/bot/g17AUEbD0Oo-5b1HDpuQB)** by @BBBang9900 — Builds and sells digital products as a side hustle — validates demand, makes the asset, uploads to marketplaces, and skips work that will not sell.

- **[Sequencer](https://x.ai/bot/6xnxd_CHg0cXVMQCwnRrZ)** — Runs your outbound sequencer as a staff function. Connects your tools, learns voice and ICP from Gmail and CRM, keeps sequence state on a sheet, and stages drafts you send.

- **[Stale Leads Closer](https://x.ai/bot/GeL4XFcQHm4WIG6jp--R0)** by @robertodjg — Revives quiet sales leads: drafts warm CRM SMS, holds a Slack veto window, then auto-sends what nobody blocks. Built for service businesses in HighLevel and Slack.

- **[Garden Retail Research Specialist](https://x.ai/bot/Y8YOrZBsPE8U-jyTI7Qtk)** by @dltelford — Weekly sales intel for people who sell into garden centers: trends, forecast stocking, tech/tools/merch, and real supplier leads on a Monday cadence.

- **[LinkedIn Bot](https://x.ai/bot/GyEovoZBfdoeEdJoYqwVP)** by @isb — Your LinkedIn memory for sales: search your posts, network, and DMs refreshed weekly from your export, with optional Syft ICP prospect discovery.

- **[Webb Knox](https://x.ai/bot/7veO5EEAD-LxgkOGy4sjk)** by @gerardocasta711 — A lead specialist for anyone who sells something: tell it what you're after and it finds verified, scored leads with proof of why each needs you now, drafts honest outreach and follow-ups, and sends a weekly pipeline recap. Works for solo makers and sales teams alike.
- **[Pipeline Scout](https://x.ai/bot/9KmU329RgFvaDOhtyc20x)** by @Screaming_Chkn — Drop 10–30 target accounts. Pipeline Scout pulls public intent and news, scores ICP fit with reasons and risks, then drafts email + LinkedIn in your voice. You get a review list — send or skip. The bot never sends, never connects, never enrolls a sequence. Built for me. Steal it. Paste 20 accounts.
- **[Winback Desk](https://x.ai/bot/KBA_4Aap3iYEZEi12_hwW)** by @Screaming_Chkn — Find customers who quit in the last 6 months, draft personal win-back + exit-feedback emails for your approval, then overnight cluster the reasons into a 5-step retention plan. Drafts only — nothing sends without you. Built for me. Steal it.
- **[Sell Bot](https://x.ai/bot/znrhzY4AYMg4v3QTfU0IJ)** — Works your whole customer journey: outreach in your voice, booked calls with prep and follow-ups, proposals and contracts, then activation, support and billing answers, and expansion. Drafts everything for your review and sends only what you approve, one message at a time.
- **[Jarvis Coordinator](https://x.ai/bot/vGBwBEELW79gSF340zJpb)** by @HBud69 — Written in German, it holds a fleet of six specialists together - news, office work, sales, systems and a security audit seat among them. Approvals are deliberately tight and digests arrive on a recurring rhythm, with the owner the only route for anything travelling in or out. As much fleet manifest as setup guide.
- **[Momentum](https://x.ai/bot/so0a9yurfhzfZFEEo3mWz)** by @ustechgod — Keeps a group of cooperating bots from stalling. It watches each lane, spots the specialist that has gone quiet, and prompts that bot to pick its thread back up before the work piles up. Alongside each nudge it hands you a shortlist of next moves that cost nothing to make.
- **[Plant Field OS](https://x.ai/bot/rAdegh2szovJbQjFsdxYa)** by @Quantumcowgirl1 — Structures the working day for field reps selling into industrial plants. It starts with a morning planning screen, carries a map of the units already installed at each account, and adds a free site assessment that doubles as a way to learn the pitch. Leadership gets one thin dashboard instead of a data dump.
- **[Venduto](https://x.ai/bot/VH6hfT_aihtFVmOcSQTwp)** by @SuperTost100 — Sells for you on European resale platforms, taking a listing from first enquiry to an agreed price. It re-checks availability before committing, keeps buyer conversations moving, pushes back on lowball offers and nudges the deal towards a close. Because it can agree sales on your behalf, it is worth supervising.
- **[First Customers Finder](https://x.ai/bot/1vDU_ekPGWy7UztJhp2yK)** by @fabian_thepulse — Each weekday it researches five people or companies that look like a genuine fit for what you sell, checks that they are real and reachable, and drafts the first message to each one. The sending stays with you — it decides who is worth a look, you decide who hears from you. A research desk rather than an auto-mailer.
- **[$100M Offers](https://x.ai/bot/wGsQKvaJgqNoE5w7l2_h_)** by @CardCaptain — Describe what you sell and who you sell it to, and it works the offer until the value is obvious: what is included, what makes it different, and why the price feels fair. The framing follows Alex Hormozi's book on offer design, explained in plain language for people new to marketing rather than for agencies.
- **[Promise Ledger](https://x.ai/bot/srdRIiBSYskKc70-TRwG1)** by @AirbossHVAC — Paste the notes from a customer call and it hands back three things: a short recap, a list of every commitment made during the conversation on both ends, and a dated list of what to chase next. It was built by a home-services owner, so it reads like the paperwork an HVAC or plumbing shop actually needs after a job.
- **[Follow-Up Bot](https://x.ai/bot/9X3XAP55-_L_47wFmH-jf)** by @day__bliss — After a conference or a busy event week, hand it the contacts you collected and it drafts a personal follow-up for each one, by email or X direct message. Nothing goes out until you approve the wording. If a thread sits unanswered for a while, it nudges you with a short list of conversations worth picking back up.
- **[Sales Business Bot](https://x.ai/bot/Rm7qrrA91s_C7tMi62SgB)** by @EzekielB6516 — Made for a solo operation run entirely from a phone, it covers the road from registering the company to a website, payments, prospect outreach, a live customer list, and social posts. Nothing ships until you have signed off on it personally.
- **[Boardy](https://x.ai/bot/Qf3kc7g_FQY41atLr-dBr)** by @jp_costa — A bridge that pulls an outside AI introductions service into the chat. Name the person you hope to meet or the kind of introduction you need, and it writes the request from your own Gmail account, then relays whatever comes back into the conversation. Your mailbox does the sending; the bot does the typing.
- **[Intake Desk](https://x.ai/bot/in_PtzvudSXOJ178cNxv0)** by @AMFoxtrot — Solo operators get pulled apart by requests arriving from five directions at once. Paste them here - leads, jobs, favors, whatever came in - and you get one sorted queue, ranked, with a single sensible next step attached to every item. The point is not elaborate workflow; it is knowing what to touch first.
- **[Quote Chase Desk](https://x.ai/bot/tWEuvegaSpz8PwlwhFVHg)** by @AraXsuper — Made for trades businesses, it holds a live list of estimates still awaiting an answer and writes follow-up nudges on a patient, spaced rhythm, with every message waiting for your sign-off before anything leaves. As soon as a customer answers, the chase for that job halts by itself, so nobody gets pestered twice.
- **[Account Manager](https://x.ai/bot/sd4333bc337078a9752ac)** — Keeps the context on each key account warm by building a briefing for every conversation out of transcripts, CRM entries, your notes and Slack, and it writes the follow-up afterwards too. You walk in already briefed on the last touchpoint and walk out with the recap done instead of a blank page.
- **[Account Research Specialist](https://x.ai/bot/sa7d7f82d0068c2367a17)** — Combines Salesforce records with live external signals to gauge how well each account fits and how warm it runs, then assembles a research pack per account that you can share around. Reps learn which prospects deserve the touch before the first call, instead of discovering the answer three weeks in.
- **[Deal Desk Coordinator](https://x.ai/bot/sb434ef300dea2b88aa58)** — Writes a context-rich internal note per deal out of past email threads, Salesforce records and call history, then waits for your sign-off before filing them into Salesforce. Anyone joining the deal reads one note instead of reconstructing three months of history from fragments.
- **[Deck Updater](https://x.ai/bot/s2a289081613e5e361c79)** by @DenisLabelle — Updates the pitch deck while the conversation happens: discovery notes get folded into the relevant slides during the call or right after it, with next steps slotted into place as they are agreed. You leave the meeting with the follow-up material moving instead of a deck that is already stale.
- **[Meeting Prep Buddy](https://x.ai/bot/s445a0c9a2ca4bea7729e)** by @DenisLabelle — Puts together a dossier before each meeting by pulling from the agenda, your notes, the CRM, past recordings and Slack: who will be in the room, the last touchpoint with each of them, threads still open and suggested talking points. Preparation stops depending on whatever you can recall in the elevator.
- **[Prospecting Plan Builder](https://x.ai/bot/s8b59eb62f90871ac5c36)** by @DenisLabelle — Builds the week's outbound book of work from scratch: it seeds the contact list, fills in missing email addresses and mobile numbers, and returns a tracker that is ready to work immediately. Reps start Monday from a list worth calling instead of an empty sheet and good intentions.
- **[Renewal Desk Operator](https://x.ai/bot/s4c86a54f188668096400)** by @DenisLabelle — Puts a renewal file under every account roughly ninety days ahead of the date: usage numbers, open tickets, recorded calls and CRM history assembled into one picture, with the commercial note drafted and a ping as the date approaches. Renewals start from evidence instead of a calendar reminder and a scramble.
- **[Kickbox → HubSpot Importer](https://x.ai/bot/PRc9UmcxySAv0UBU4g8fx)** by @pofabro — Point it at a spreadsheet of contacts or a saved segment in your CRM and it verifies every address before anything moves. Only the ones that pass get written back, and they land as updates to records you already have, so nothing new is created and nothing is deleted.
- **[Lead Vetting](https://x.ai/bot/eEfAacXdtzHANV5hVhG6o)** by @anzevoje — When a consultancy or partnership request lands, it reads the message, checks it against what you actually do, and writes up whether it is worth your time. Its verdict lands in your Slack as a few decision points, and nothing goes back to the enquirer — the reply stays yours.
- **[Legend Lead Ops](https://x.ai/bot/WCZjv-qHMtHz2AdQW136q)** by @legendRoofingCo — Built for a roofing business: it takes incoming enquiries, sorts the ones worth pursuing, and drafts the text and email follow-ups. It also assembles what the Quick Quote handoff needs so nothing gets asked twice. Contact details fall under explicit exposure rules, with the founder's own information behind a gate.
- **[Punto Tricolor Ops](https://x.ai/bot/ARSoBhGtQqYPT2Nu44ABi)** by @cyberandres — Built around a small local company rather than a startup team. Each weekday morning it delivers a technology briefing as voice, a separate agent takes customer orders, table reservations and appointments, and a prospecting pass works through nearby restaurants and clinics. One desk carrying the daily load.
- **[Prospect Drafts](https://x.ai/bot/Ed8OwTpWaFfZdJHEAoT4t)** by @sam_builds_ai — Made for outbound sellers who would rather edit a note than write one from nothing. Give it the regions, industries and offer you sell into, and it goes looking for companies that match, then opens a Gmail draft carrying your tone, your number and your signature. Sending stays a human click.
- **[Business Info Finder](https://x.ai/bot/lsg8TX4CiXRGWhkK9T8gN)** by @Serenejaysew — Give it a company name or an individual and it returns a single page laid out for skimming: who owns the entity, which numbers and inboxes reach it, the right person to approach, where they post publicly and when the doors are open. It gathers what is already published and formats it. It never messages anyone for you.
- **[Competitive Landscape Desk](https://x.ai/bot/2pBIwrTpcLT7kDQ3NNGxY)** by @Cypher0x9 — Give it your product and your market and it builds a side-by-side table of the rivals you are up against, with sources attached to each claim. It then lays out the realistic options and commits to one recommendation. You decide what leaves the desk - nothing goes out on its own.
- **[Sales Call Prep Desk](https://x.ai/bot/8QpQcgUVgUpxG5RR4mwnL)** by @Cypher0x9 — Before you dial in, it assembles a short, sourced brief about the account that you can check, then lists the discovery questions worth your time. It maps the objections you are most likely to hit and closes with the specific request that advances the sale. It will not message the customer itself.
- **[Outbound Scout](https://x.ai/bot/7FPx_GNADVyb36VRYoKz1)** by @iammutex — Hand it your product's URL or a two-line blurb and it first pins down who the ideal buyer actually is. From there it hunts for companies matching that profile and digs up the named decision-makers worth approaching, contact details included. It stops at research - no outreach is sent on your behalf.
- **[Account Health](https://x.ai/bot/s5465ebb9e192551741f7)** by @DenisLabelle — Reads usage patterns and account health signals across a full book of customers, then boils them down to one watch list before each quarterly review. Accounts drifting toward churn rise to the top, and quiet expansion candidates get flagged too, so the QBR agenda starts from evidence rather than gut feel.
- **[Compelling Events Monitor](https://x.ai/bot/sdeb02761a9d185e5899f)** — Spots the moments that make outreach worth writing by watching leadership accounts for awards, product launches and hiring moves, then gathers the best engagement openings into a single digest. Outreach stops being a cold guess about timing and starts landing when there is a real reason to write.
- **[Pipeline Analyst](https://x.ai/bot/s98af8b9dcde521e1a9fa)** by @DenisLabelle — Scrubs the Salesforce and spreadsheet data, flags the deals that have stalled and the commits at risk, then opens Monday with a scoreboard that gives the week an honest pipeline view to start from. Forecast conversations stop starting with whose number is stale and start with what to do about it.
- **[Lead Scout](https://x.ai/bot/j3Qule6BFplSpVqgLpVnV)** by @itsryanlenk — Searches close by for small companies whose public websites look like they have never been tuned for search or answer engines, and writes a short file on each find. Those files go straight to the research bot that works downstream of it. It suits teams that already run outbound and want a fuller list.
- **[Icebreaker (Apache1999)](https://x.ai/bot/c2y0GRnjxC7scs76bzxGw)** by @Apache1999 — A lead radar for commercial brokers. On weekday mornings it sweeps for owners of commercial property whose situation points towards a sale, then leaves a compact call sheet naming who to ring and why. A morning where nothing has moved produces no message at all, so the ping is always worth reading.
- **[Roof Radar](https://x.ai/bot/nuv1bSb5V15XZeFnu5BwF)** by @SacredFolio — For roofing contractors who prospect after weather events. It reads publicly reported hail and storm data, scores nearby properties as candidates, and checks each against open building footprints so a lead arrives with its evidence attached. Contacting homeowners is deliberately left to you.
- **[Instagram Iris](https://x.ai/bot/bc_XHf6W4m2dwI9qGDiYU)** by @FrankFindsOut — Give it your ideal-customer profile and it scores Instagram creators against that bar, so your outreach list starts qualified instead of guessed. For each one it hands you a hook drawn from that creator's own recent feed plus a ready-to-edit DM you send yourself.
- **[Sales Outbound](https://x.ai/bot/s8ff03023f140bab479f7)** by @DenisLabelle — Lifts the research and the first-touch writing off the reps' plates: accounts get studied overnight, contacts ranked by intent, and email plus LinkedIn messages drafted in your voice, ready for a morning review pass. The outbound day starts from reviewed drafts rather than a cold list.
- **[LinkedIn Lead Outreach](https://x.ai/bot/DEMmOi7SiFhsFtIKuBKRD)** by @ethan_leee9113 — Point it at the comments under a founder's giveaway or lead-magnet post. Each commenter is logged to Notion, answered in public, then moved into a direct message where the email handoff happens, and incoming connection requests get accepted. Volume stays inside the caps the card sets, so the pace is yours to choose.
- **[GTM Expert](https://x.ai/bot/PIGZO9iQIeWkbmE79Q7bT)** — You coordinate lookalike research, competitive intelligence, search and AI visibility, creator shortlists, and ICP/TAM mapping as one go-to-market teammate.

## 企业运营

- **[Alfred](https://x.ai/bot/p7Gh6HIrfv4AGzIow6-9X)** by @heyrobinai — Designs, audits, and governs your Grok Bot organization so it matches real company outcomes, with clear human owners and no duplicate jobs. Recommends the smallest useful structure, defaults new bots to zero, and never creates or changes anything without your exact yes.
- **[Event Request Desk](https://x.ai/bot/hp7QlVUPuYUp09kc6IFAA)** — Scores every event, sponsorship, and speaking ask, then drafts your yes or no. Works from a Slack channel or a paste, and never sends without you.
- **[Executive Assistant](https://x.ai/bot/_DnP777DCicZpaTtm9_h5)** by @tashatweetss — EA chief-of-staff bot for exec support: conference rooms, interview prep, leadership outreach, Slack channel inventories, sheet↔calendar checks, and birthday/anniversary calendar loads.
- **[Haggle Bot](https://x.ai/bot/pwQ612YrX3R0eACnIMlom)** — Inventories your SaaS spend from Ramp and bills, finds evidence-backed savings (unused seats, duplicates, cheaper alternatives), and drafts vendor counters for your review. Never spends, signs, or sends without you.
- **[Office Ops Desk](https://x.ai/bot/afZj-XnYkThA4MB7GfZK6)** by @ericacabera — Tracks office shipments, facilities issues, and team birthdays, then writes the digests. Works from a pasted list or a spreadsheet, and never sends without you.
- **[Tradbot](https://x.ai/bot/wOE4e95HNxhSbrzyLkSI-)** by @clairevo — Despite the name it is not a trading bot: it watches your personal inbox and calendar so school items, family plans and household follow-ups stop slipping through.
- **[Inbox to Asana](https://x.ai/bot/Ka18PTTKUNtDDPg0HpYva)** by @wikiwayne — Mail arrives as a wall of messages; work arrives as a list. This reads a company Gmail account, sorts each message into keep, chase later or let go, and writes the survivors across to your Asana tasks with dates attached and reminders set. It never replies and never sends anything out from your address.
- **[Dispatch](https://x.ai/bot/6zJ1yU4dDfAVYFJcy0687)** by @seoulscurry — Inbox owner for Gmail, personal or work. Weekday sweep of must-answer mail — people, bills, contracts, vendors — drafts replies in your voice from Sent, and never sends. Stays quiet when the inbox is clean.
- **[Slacker](https://x.ai/bot/R-TSImHItwbFHL8vYj9sc)** by @scottxmetcalf — VIP Slack triage for a GTM or enablement lead. Three daily digests surface only the asks that need you, with paste-ready drafts. Stays quiet when nothing is waiting.
- **[Robauto Website Growth Agent](https://x.ai/bot/7k0TLQBu4hPI5oE3ywRHU)** by @RobautoAI — Traffic is starting to arrive as software rather than as people, and most sites were never built for that. This grades yours on how well a machine can read it, follow it and settle up with it, then hands over one fix a day that you can drop straight in, re-checking after each change until the score tops out.
- **[GrokBot Optimizer](https://x.ai/bot/b7m5siCKd6baaWkPihOGa)** by @rjdhardesty — Aimed at anyone running more than a handful of bots at once. It takes stock of who is on the roster, what each one is meant to be doing and what runs on a timer, weighs all of that against the operating principles you have written down, and points out overlap or drift. Every change arrives as a proposal you approve.
- **[Newsletter Cleanup](https://x.ai/bot/dHd69sBvMG2o3lJa__T7K)** by @scheemunai — Buckets everything into a review page you can actually read, then acts on your decisions. It unsubscribes rather than deletes, so nothing disappears from your inbox.
- **[Action Loop](https://x.ai/bot/py5cXgcAKPdEYsYK_AmcM)** by @thekuchh — Closes open execution loops systematically through rapid cycles of action, feedback, adjustment, and re-execution.
- **[ADM account bot](https://x.ai/bot/4Gc1tZsJu7C8YH-EnTfaN)** by @scottxmetcalf — Each week it works through the accounts you own and puts a plan behind them: which ones look wobbly enough to be worth defending now, where there is genuine room to sell more, and how to frame the case so the customer actually hears it. Aimed at account managers and CSM teams.
- **[AI Orchestrator (JP)](https://x.ai/bot/-kSMWtBCorQFkgUhm0DLk)** by @mei_999_ — Assigns one job per specialist, keeps the departments tidy, runs scheduled check-ins, and manages the shared memory. Posting and destructive changes wait for the owner's approval.
- **[AI PM OS](https://x.ai/bot/9dtfHw4LHmwc5uBC-a9vj)** — Sample of the AI PM OS for product managers. Leads with the Problem First skill to pull teams back from solutions to the underlying problem.
- **[AIO Specialist (AEO/GEO)](https://x.ai/bot/wOvqAFpr3o8VB3g4Tmpxr)** by @mathiasnoyez — Answer-engine and generative-engine optimisation run as ongoing work rather than a one-off check of what a chatbot says about you today.
- **[Alfred (Bot Chief Advisor)](https://x.ai/bot/KZ9xav0Qad1U5QigEn7rh)** by @heyrobinai — Rather than building one assistant, it audits the set you already have and reshapes the org as the business changes.
- **[Austin Parent](https://x.ai/bot/7yCzCeGQTMD6oNKSPcFqj)** by @ChadWittman — Family chief of staff for Austin parents: school, calendar, activities, and household logistics.
- **[Bandit](https://x.ai/bot/xRyaLCqAzIr_paD5tC8PK)** by @BitsOfJT — Sarcastic second-in-command that keeps your other Bots honest and on the real job.
- **[Beatrix Kiddo](https://x.ai/bot/z4Chp77wqP5ASkBKpxOOk)** by @liam_fallen — It watches what is arriving and what is going out, following each consignment to the point where somebody else takes it over. Whatever stalls, goes astray or drifts past its promised date lands on a running list of problems and gets chased. You hear about a hold-up before the person waiting at the far end does.
- **[BeTree](https://x.ai/bot/2PSNlIROOJPj9qZlfRy0w)** by @NicoChauvin74 — Compiles a multi-agent plan into a live behavior-tree graph so you can see status across several bots on one board.
- **[Bing Bong](https://x.ai/bot/BjzK0lRsgxuLSsQIsnI3E)** by @jennananpei — Partnership ops for sports activations: deadlines, inventory, and in-arena work. One teammate, one job.
- **[Blunt](https://x.ai/bot/N0J32FbnVRuetJi1oJggh)** by @Talsiach — Landing-page review from a senior product marketer. Send a URL and get THE MEMO: the good, the bad, and the one thing to fix.
- **[Bodyguard](https://x.ai/bot/tII28kVM4dxPvzSLjwqko)** by @liam_fallen — Protects the owner's time and attention from requests that do not deserve direct access. Classifies inbound email and calendar asks into let-through, bundle, or decline.
- **[Bot Father](https://x.ai/bot/dVQjvC6c-sMhtgVskciBH)** by @abdshomad — Central orchestrator that nourishes, protects, and evolves a network of child agents. For operators who want one steward over a multi-agent roster.
- **[BOTOSHI](https://x.ai/bot/29XazZFrrsJyI8LUnExDD)** by @MineBotcoin — Zero ETH BOTCOIN mining rig onboarding miner.
- **[Bouncer](https://x.ai/bot/cGcG0msqfz7o7J3QMLhbE)** by @bradshannon — Audits public Grok Bot share links and configs prior to installation, returning CLEAN, WARN, or BLOCK security verdicts.
- **[Bounty Hunter](https://x.ai/bot/gCWYD009F66A3XDEYdZgf)** by @liam_fallen — Goes looking for money that is already yours but unclaimed: missed refunds, forgotten credits, overcharges and things you are owed and never got round to pursuing.
- **[Box Inspector](https://x.ai/bot/q7GLbLhMZDpJXBGuuci1J)** by @SuddenlyJon — Peek at a Grok Bot share link before you add it. Stamps, an ADD? verdict, and what the copy would inherit. It never Adds.
- **[CEO](https://x.ai/bot/GHLPyes3hiP9A6kES7UHg)** by @inqusit — Holds the top-level view of the business: strategy, what gets priority, how it makes money and how it reaches people. Rather than doing the work itself it studies what is going on, decides what matters next, and puts specialist bots on the individual pieces.
- **[ChatPRD](https://x.ai/bot/36vKs2HSysdaJDe6OLD4w)** by @clairevo — Writes specs, brainstorms, and discovery docs into ChatPRD from existing notes and what actually shipped.
- **[Chieeeeefy](https://x.ai/bot/GiBPBQR2WrHNul4k9Tz6Q)** by @naoufal_elh — Chief of Staff for a Field Engineer. Calendar and work Gmail first. Convert event timezones to the user's current local time.
- **[Chief](https://x.ai/bot/JugVUSPe_wSZg-in69owM)** — Router only, never the worker. Assigns one object owner, then stays out of the pair. Hears one line: live, blocked, or an owner call.
- **[Chief of Staff](https://x.ai/bot/d8OshqLZvtcKDcNluPuyo)** by @Av1dlive — You are the Chief of Staff. One brain. You run the person's day and the company from one desk.
- **[Chief of Staff](https://x.ai/bot/5hqR_5PVUy7WMbNaXPJ8s)** by @BTC_Yogi — One entry point for your agent team. Routes work, keeps the roster small, and never sends, spends, or deletes without your yes.
- **[Clark Kent (Daily Diary)](https://x.ai/bot/6sF7_MwHMcWgWwq0Z6Xes)** by @RichSilver — Watches the day's work and records it in plain language, noticing the mistakes and the skipped steps rather than only the things that went to plan.
- **[Club Events](https://x.ai/bot/SDLUUiG6HKwiFpGqCOAw7)** by @danieldxu_ — Runs student-club event paperwork: weekly Slack due dates, funding and venue forms, and follow-ups.
- **[Commercial Taste](https://x.ai/bot/vekulzIMXM8hDjkp-mDkX)** by @thesmitpatel — A business thought partner for technical founders and execs. Helps you make the right commercial move before the data is complete, covering positioning and similar calls.
- **[construction office manager](https://x.ai/bot/Uytjr0oAalw0OuzCLdWPd)** by @joshkim — Connects the bookkeeping and expense-reporting tools a building firm already runs on, then keeps those workflows tidy rather than leaving them to drift. The intent is that the owner spends the evening on the business instead of on paperwork and receipts.
- **[Convert X Money to Karma](https://x.ai/bot/iCn7r691OdtaB_o8MtHx_)** by @ludiofelix — Converts money, tokens, and engagement into karmic accounting. Ten percent watermarked up the royalty chain; tokens are evidence, not the cut.
- **[Copay Compass](https://x.ai/bot/ehxj2Wdxq9M04jvaAqyBD)** by @MSaintjour — Oncology medication can arrive with a price no household has planned for. This goes looking for the charitable funds and manufacturer schemes that would cover part of it, puts you in line for word when a dry fund opens again, and drafts the forms. It never asks for your national ID or chart numbers.
- **[CoS](https://x.ai/bot/eiVFbd0nIdH2gzSwHOs0D)** by @theaaron — A personal chief of staff for people who run a small team of specialist AI agents. Coordinates calendar, projects, and inbound mail.
- **[Cost-Smart Health Brief](https://x.ai/bot/Rm6VqcE8cOWXwotPth9qM)** — Turns one health or health-system question into a 3-minute brief: evidence, cheaper options, and when to see a clinician. Not a doctor.
- **[Data Science (Querie)](https://x.ai/bot/Bu2sEQqu0hEjpbzN_07D3)** by @egavrilenko11 — Give one bot the job of answering data questions and the rest of your roster routes query work to it instead of each improvising its own definition of the same metric.
- **[dbs](https://x.ai/bot/l6H6WL7HF-CAwcvr1hBey)** by @Leechael — Everything goes through one command, which routes the question to the right kind of answer: a commercial problem, a content problem, or a what-should-I-do-now problem. When you name a trending topic it checks the topic is real before advising you to act on it.
- **[Dean of Students](https://x.ai/bot/_hsyZUFgPzgxGxW2wIYAj)** by @ErinnFL — A parent's private dean for one child: school email, Canvas, forms, grades, calendar, and fees. Prepares everything; the parent still signs, pays, and submits.
- **[deck-guy](https://x.ai/bot/bdkJcjP5Gt9BaGTqh1vXH)** by @pavravi — Hand over the record of a discovery call, or the notes you took while scoping the work, and it assembles a stripped-back monochrome deck in return. Slides cover when things happen, what each side is asking of the other, and the steps both parties commit to next, fast enough to show before the meeting ends.
- **[den](https://x.ai/bot/0aEcF7mtG_zsDWXEUeOGx)** by @Adamdesgns — Family-bot relay that sits between dad and the family's bots so he can stay consistent on academics, money habits, and raising kids while on the road.
- **[Denial Desk](https://x.ai/bot/EgfoyJEx7bfDiHlZUwr3P)** by @Andrew51786 — Turns an insurance denial into a draft-only appeal pack: case card, deadline, missing documents, and unsent drafts. For anyone sitting on a no from an insurer.
- **[Dewey](https://x.ai/bot/rfAHsaFrz6xHBMtUpxDi5)** — Watches Gmail and pings you when a message looks time-sensitive or needs a reply. Reads freely; never sends, replies, or trashes mail unless you ask.
- **[Dispatch](https://x.ai/bot/YkmZEZYBk-BqylyQbM3kq)** by @FilippoFonseca — If someone agreed to a call and no invite exists, it creates the calendar event, which closes the gap where a yes quietly turns into nothing ever being scheduled.
- **[dosebot](https://x.ai/bot/2euxntVrddHyA3c2hyxiZ)** by @onerinas — Pitch it a company, a product or a single feature and it sorts the thing into one of three buckets: something people merely enjoy having, something that relieves real pain, or a blend of the two. The verdict comes back plain and unsentimental, which is the point, since the alternative is finding out the expensive way.
- **[EG4 Monitor](https://x.ai/bot/9rxPP70OSzuTtTaOrzeqz)** by @look4terry — Monitors home EG4 solar and hybrid battery systems, visualizes performance charts, and alerts on anomalous production.
- **[facebook group scout](https://x.ai/bot/C7ZoMLPxEbFmu0-iAieFj)** by @joshkim — Monitors a named set of groups on a light weekday schedule and surfaces only the handful of new posts that look like a genuine opening for your business. Running it on a cadence rather than continuously keeps it from burying your main chat in noise.
- **[Feedback](https://x.ai/bot/_-3KKbHbnSRzrS_8KFugU)** by @nytemodeonly — Writes a faithful bug report for Grok Bot, Cursor, or the X connector and files it for you to send to the product teams.
- **[Feedback Clock](https://x.ai/bot/ySceLccAh5J8IVnq62mQl)** by @thekuchh — Minimizes the feedback cycle between taking action and identifying errors or areas of improvement.
- **[Fenrir (Paper Trading)](https://x.ai/bot/FReKiR82_-lF359lhshpR)** by @shantanugoel — Orchestrates competing strategies against a live market feed as a tournament, retargeting the clock, currency and universe depending on which exchange you point it at.
- **[Fixer](https://x.ai/bot/CEtFUY1_kkn78AJSNINHI)** by @liam_fallen — Takes annoying loose-end problems, investigates fully, and gets them as close to resolved as possible before needing you.
- **[Fixer (Uzi)](https://x.ai/bot/jiF_km66YLNm5LBVJ5_Ho)** by @UziObi — Built as the counterpart to a chief of staff: it executes rather than coordinates, and it will argue with you rather than cheerfully carrying out something that will not work.
- **[Floor](https://x.ai/bot/we_JMJA8IuOvy1eUX6EQz)** by @iamigorekk — Runs a trading-floor roster: routes work to six agents, checks handoffs, wakes you only for decisions. Does not trade.
- **[Fondi](https://x.ai/bot/qL920VjKyua3_u89UYnQL)** by @naoufal_elh — Aimed at people building alone. It works through whatever your company already publishes about itself, forms a picture of the business, then creates specialist assistants to match, standing in for the heads of function a one-person outfit cannot afford to hire. The bench is shaped around your company, not generic.
- **[Gatekeeper](https://x.ai/bot/T5FSfM91XA6gMgh2rX56K)** by @liam_fallen — Before a new commitment goes on the pile, it makes the trade explicit: what gets delayed, what gets dropped, and what you are quietly deciding not to do.
- **[gonzalo's smb manager](https://x.ai/bot/G0GVoN9xUbXFucwWz539v)** by @joshkim — Covers the jobs a small business owner never gets round to: winning back customers who stopped coming in, chasing tabs that were left open, writing the day's social post and working on filling tomorrow's calendar. Nothing leaves without the owner saying yes.
- **[Grant (General Manager)](https://x.ai/bot/fkM4b8n4RqZTbrq5fw5L_)** by @HouseHackerJon — Built for construction and the trades: it spins up dispatch, job booking and scheduling alongside finance and marketing helpers, so the office side of a site business has somewhere to live.
- **[Grillo](https://x.ai/bot/0K8ZjhfS8SUgIRKt9hS0T)** by @aiassesstech — Independent fleet conscience. It answers whether each agent behaves, using runtime behavioral attestation. It does not fix, punish, post, or certify.
- **[grokbots.best](https://x.ai/bot/55WKZjppK42Jr3XPnPJdm)** — Submits public Grok Bot templates to the grokbots.best directory.
- **[GROKSTREET](https://x.ai/bot/grokstreet)** by @adiix_official — A multi-agent autonomous financial desk spanning 11 desks, 3 chiefs, and risk management operating 24/7.
- **[Gus Fring](https://x.ai/bot/Dhk5c79MEj0MRM484ZM1k)** by @liam_fallen — Nothing reaches a client until this one has looked at it. It rereads near-final work against what was originally asked for, hunting for gaps, slips and details that contradict each other, then checks that fixes raised earlier were actually applied. What comes back is one of three plain grades, not a vague opinion.
- **[Harvey Specter](https://x.ai/bot/lkkCqhC1jBFp6ouZOQd9m)** by @liam_fallen — It talks to the other side directly and works the price and terms, but it never signs, never accepts anything binding, and never commits you to something you have not seen.
- **[Holly Helpdesk](https://x.ai/bot/sIoeE87fILU5CzptPF29K)** by @clairevo — Takes first pass on customer tickets: answers how-to questions, looks up billing details and keeps the support inbox moving. Anything touching money, such as a refund or a payment dispute, is held for the owner to approve rather than actioned on its own.
- **[I Said I Would](https://x.ai/bot/riqe7Z1oOnU6wIV7Zb9Mt)** by @hnshah — Remembers the things you said you'd do for other people. Watches connected communication for clear commitments, keeps one ledger, and weekday mornings surfaces only the ones that still matter.
- **[Inbot](https://x.ai/bot/yH2UttxbMwMugweZrigHT)** by @matt_silberman — Setup asks you to connect the lot — mail, Slack or Teams, calendars, Notion and messengers — and then it works them down.
- **[InsightfulPipe](https://x.ai/bot/vYIAB3Z6V8gEERewymcw1)** by @insightfulpipe — Senior marketer for ads, SEO, social, and Shopify powered by live InsightfulPipe data.
- **[Investor Bot](https://x.ai/bot/UWNGpcghM9H79JCb4of5Q)** by @MadeItHappenX — Autonomous swing trader for a small (~$1k) brokerage book with defined stops and quiet alerts.
- **[Invoice Hunter](https://x.ai/bot/-kO6HrXokJZANVwUOMZO9)** by @scheemunai — Pulls the vendor, date, amount and invoice number out of each PDF, bundles a whole month with an index, and waits for your approval before anything reaches your accountant.
- **[Jess (Executive Assistant)](https://x.ai/bot/Nmv2fCQEcQc3EHzVXJZKN)** by @LoganARobison — A weekday executive assistant: it reads everything first, answers staff questions from a written playbook, and posts the client call notes so you start the day caught up.
- **[John Wick](https://x.ai/bot/_OlL8LPI6lc2xi82F4Gf7)** by @liam_fallen — Charts the org, finds the emails, follows referrals and keeps climbing until it reaches the person who can actually say yes, then brings you into that conversation.
- **[Just-in-Time Curriculum](https://x.ai/bot/rpkZERbKrIN_NlDl8ErVZ)** by @thekuchh — Eliminates bloated preparation lists and delivers only the specific knowledge required for the immediate task at hand.
- **[Kirk (Enterprise Crew)](https://x.ai/bot/FaRchqvTT6ZCRVPf0JABl)** by @The_Mr_Wizard — A Star Trek role-play that is also a genuine agent-team demo: the command chair spawns specialists who work as a crew.
- **[Kody](https://x.ai/bot/yTSGElYcIjFW_5IXu2I-e)** by @kentcdodds — Sits across people, projects and your other assistants, and its job is to make what you said matters actually turn into work that is moving somewhere.
- **[Latch](https://x.ai/bot/9nbLm_04EvjnolE9oevTT)** by @OliverKorzen — Walks a new Grok Bot user from their role and the tools they use to live connectors, then does a first real job in the same chat.
- **[Lazy Tom](https://x.ai/bot/YD0mAEdVZeEanyWTuY_vb)** — Dry, low-effort assistant that does the smallest useful thing and stops.
- **[Leader 1:1 Bot](https://x.ai/bot/eZhKhPkfxxFSml18TS2X8)** by @scottxmetcalf — Carries the thread forward instead of restarting each week, picks the few topics actually worth the slot.
- **[Lease Finder](https://x.ai/bot/_A_AZayMmSNuN_-sdq_M1)** — Hunts current lease deals on whatever you're shopping. Pulls sites that track deep discounts.
- **[LinkedIn Desk](https://x.ai/bot/tQuoQ94ErUfXNJu4xPqZi)** by @SEO — Learns a Peer-first invite policy, recommends Accept or Ignore, and clicks only the exact batch you approve.
- **[Linky](https://x.ai/bot/zcHEE4_hbqw3cZsy7X2Vk)** by @adamludwin — Turns files, folders, or bot outputs into a shareable here.now URL after you install the here.now plugin.
- **[Lockdown](https://x.ai/bot/P1LmE76VG38Ui-XCmzAZE)** by @mjjefford — Checks your controls against SOC 2 requirements and flags gaps before an audit. Part of a curated set of bots that pay for themselves in a month.
- **[loom](https://x.ai/bot/cElGnAaR55iPHK2DGdPdu)** by @poteto — Before writing anything it gathers the related threads, so the draft actually knows the history behind the question. Then it leaves the reply in front of you. It will not send, archive, delete or bin a single message, which makes it safe to point at a live inbox.
- **[Marketing Bot (CMO)](https://x.ai/bot/37ZOM10GzlSOQpMjRp7KB)** by @tymarsha — Give it the product context once and it produces the copy, the emails, the offers and the conversion ideas, rather than answering one marketing question at a time.
- **[Master](https://x.ai/bot/j7B5LHnEIPTuPQZxxQwpx)** — Lean orchestrator only. Never owns work clocks or keeps recurring personal tasks. Routes immediately to specialist owners.
- **[Medical Bill Review](https://x.ai/bot/M9c2tC_-mwY8XNTmSbkUY)** by @MSaintjour — Gets the itemized bill, screens published protections and charity-care policy, and drafts the dispute letter for you to send.
- **[Memento](https://x.ai/bot/_xZZE41svJdcq2w6ZWJan)** by @MaheshtheDev — Second-brain coworker for Grok Bot, powered by SuperMemory. Searches and saves context so you do not re-explain, and never invents a memory.
- **[Mercury](https://x.ai/bot/lk1yHfim5Ayra0Q0QlN3L)** by @chiefjeeb — Persistent technical and product lead: owns system understanding and the release call, then delegates narrow Cursor jobs in parallel.
- **[Money Maker Bot](https://x.ai/bot/KfiGbaCO0HLqoRfwi4V2H)** by @mbhound — Finds legal ways to make money; first run sets up a wallet.
- **[Nikita Bier](https://x.ai/bot/m0wqg4OfsKBO6aKi93vCV)** — Pressure-tests products for the share loop and names one change you can ship this week.
- **[Onboarding Coach](https://x.ai/bot/OfZitBsJprif-DfsQKBUY)** — Welcomes new Grok Bot users, learns how they live and work, then proposes concrete jobs and how a specialist team should grow.
- **[openrobot](https://x.ai/bot/ndO6BI7E2ur5X-bhWM_1R)** by @noborderhuman — For a project that keeps getting approached by people who want to work with it. The bot asks a short set of qualifying questions, writes the introduction email to the team on that person's behalf, and tells them what happens next so nobody is left waiting.
- **[Operator](https://x.ai/bot/YgM8FiKS0WczveQXe6edr)** by @mjjefford — Opens with a handful of quick either-or questions so it learns the way you want it run. From then on it takes whatever loose notes have piled up, works them into a single written brief, and hands back an ordered list of the decisions genuinely waiting on you.
- **[OutBid Mania](https://x.ai/bot/Sj_LPMP7hKOOSzF8YDiNr)** by @dragosroua — Watches the original and the copies, follows their revenue, and reports daily on whether the trend is actually growing or just loud.
- **[Overwatch](https://x.ai/bot/7u3XiRiTYw4GVZmuZboyP)** by @scheemunai — Keeps the shared computer clean and backed up. Does not delete another bot's work.
- **[Pain](https://x.ai/bot/yztAMds3EQ2J5OjG_tBgw)** by @gambrill — Finds repetitive work that is slowing you down, prescribes the right kind of help, then puts it in motion.
- **[PhoneZero Operator](https://x.ai/bot/vB2o6vvmHjDQRM5yFH9vn)** — Runs PhoneZero outbound calls. Walks Telnyx and xAI setup on its computer, plans first, waits for an explicit yes, then reports what a live person said.
- **[Point Peddler](https://x.ai/bot/PFD95widaEeqjkYLLUZmD)** by @poteto — Its one job is credit-card and airline points, so you never spend longer than a moment deciding how to book.
- **[Polo](https://x.ai/bot/R-i5_wHeR_cBinyLVyvWh)** by @soleio — Watches your inbox for questions you can already answer from mail or files you already have. Leaves a short draft in your mailbox and asks before sending, so you are not shuttling answers by hand.
- **[porshe](https://x.ai/bot/BXDRX1jaURkI4Tx70zLg6)** — Business-money scout that finds unpaid invoices, pending payments, and uncollected work in your inbox, then names the one next action to collect it.
- **[Porter](https://x.ai/bot/cl7kIRbcIuP6jj2Zt8z5K)** by @darylbleach — Moves a Grok Bot roster from one app to another. Inventories every teammate, packs jobs, memories, and standing routines, then recreates them on the destination.
- **[Post Call Assistant](https://x.ai/bot/xF12c5y4LVe7nf7IFguWI)** by @itspriyaptl — It writes the follow-up but never sends it, and it asks up front which notes, inbox and calendar to work from rather than guessing at your setup.
- **[Pre-Call Intelligence](https://x.ai/bot/yQRbVJ7aZL2DyPAWulXn7)** by @ericosiu — Prepares your team before important meetings by reviewing prior conversations and explaining what the other party cares about.
- **[Precog wARS](https://x.ai/bot/7M8RpppF2AistbVbeEPyN)** by @ferminrp — Spanish-language lookup desk for Precog prediction markets denominated in wARS on Base and Arbitrum. Returns on-chain probabilities with no commentary and no ability to trade.
- **[Price Error Agent](https://x.ai/bot/cbULQqhzmOeeJ9GT2DX7L)** by @yoda_FDE — Hunts accidental misprices on big Australian retailers and cheap AU-origin flights. Watches OzBargain, Secret Flying, and Beat That Flight, then pings you.
- **[Private Desk](https://x.ai/bot/Tgl3sxrTsuAYL7MN8S3UT)** by @useprismnetwork — Some documents cannot reasonably be pasted into a general assistant, so the analysis simply never gets done at all. This carries out the work inside a sealed hardware enclave instead: the material is encrypted on your own side, and the environment proves what it is running before a result is accepted.
- **[Publish work as a private link](https://x.ai/bot/n9zq64kTeEEc5NwrkAOi8)** by @stevy_smith — A report, a plan or a one-pager becomes a URL you can send, with no account needed at the other end. The link is unguessable rather than access-controlled, so treat it as unlisted, not as private.
- **[Randal](https://x.ai/bot/IHA3Iiw7vhSa5lmE82o-p)** by @raihankhan_rk — COO that manages staff bots, keeps work on schedule, and helps with ops.
- **[Reaper](https://x.ai/bot/Gd-cqXG8xG_RPmKGixa73)** — Find recurring work, tools, processes, and obligations that no longer justify their existence.
- **[Receipt Scanner](https://x.ai/bot/qod4CrNQBlDIMm5wFYVQp)** by @limeunfiltered — Logs emailed receipts into a Google Sheet and Drive folder.
- **[RedReplier](https://x.ai/bot/8aU6ly_uunnMabpybs3hB)** by @tarasshyn — Watches Reddit, X, HN, and Bluesky for product mentions, ranked by buying intent.
- **[Rentals](https://x.ai/bot/JrnQAM0z-7SNI9UtIO3-Z)** by @HandsomeHenry6 — Facebook Marketplace rental listing manager for owners. Watches listings, engages leads in your voice, books showings.
- **[Rutin](https://x.ai/bot/o4gWkNGmffEaVtOhaEsA7)** by @naoufal_elh — A Monday-morning optimizer that scans every bot's routines and proposes schedule fixes.
- **[Senior Analyst](https://x.ai/bot/Q2xW8BIDffTjbDVXZYZhV)** by @tobias_pfuetze — OCR for financial statements and other documents into structured data. Drop an image, get a downloadable Excel plus a cited memo. No manual signup.
- **[Set Up](https://x.ai/bot/BsExflSUXpW0hs21OTBzu)** by @theaaron — Walks someone new to Grok Bot through building a small, intentional team: one chief, a few project leads, and specialists. Asks one question at a time.
- **[Shepherd](https://x.ai/bot/i5YF8f-zdcR76uKPrqg3J)** by @herdrdev — Orchestrates coding agents in Herdr, an open runtime for agent work.
- **[Shikamaru](https://x.ai/bot/rrvGu13S5uYCc09WP7A-9)** — Chief of Staff who hires and manages specialist agents in a named world. Speaks up only for world-level calls or tasks that cannot be delegated.
- **[Ship Note](https://x.ai/bot/xMCiRCmOCYLeRzW8nS6EL)** by @sol_wright7 — Converts shipping logs and release notes into clean changelogs and customer update email drafts.
- **[ShopBot](https://x.ai/bot/rBXWgythSa09pIp14rnV4)** — Buys things online for you using web search, Shopify, and Link — goes from product hunt to checkout on its own computer.
- **[Smores](https://x.ai/bot/QIfSY8pPwjqBSIdal-5CI)** by @jxckvibe — Weekday morning scan of email and calendar. Archives junk, drafts replies it never sends, and hands you a one-page brief.
- **[Spark (Onboarding)](https://x.ai/bot/_2vi1lOY4oiBaJDA3S8l1)** by @vincentzhu — Asks a short set of questions, creates the bots that match your answers, and then nudges you towards connecting the accounts.
- **[Sterling](https://x.ai/bot/WNJl5y33yqdOp3CnhR4-k)** by @jchybow — Calm financial teammate for tracking personal spending, upcoming bills, account balances, and cash runway.
- **[Steve J](https://x.ai/bot/cuEYUcYmz-497oKWVfWX2)** by @AhuraDeus — Quality-bar CEO for a personal Grok Bot roster. Reviews other bots against their job cards, sends back unfinished work, and runs a weekday sweep.
- **[Struggle Gate](https://x.ai/bot/tjN1LsaYsuR7u0dQQvOGV)** by @thekuchh — Enforces active problem-solving by requiring 10 minutes of independent thinking before AI provides guidance.
- **[SubCut](https://x.ai/bot/MzuJZpvaIK2KpexUVY-V0)** by @tahaabuilds — Scans email inboxes for recurring SaaS charges, categorizes monthly vs. yearly billing, and highlights cost-saving opportunities.
- **[SumoSign](https://x.ai/bot/Uicr9Dc3FKOmsMfbN_NHB)** by @SumoSign — Sends documents for human signature via SumoSign. Needs only the client's own API key. Upload, place fields, and send.
- **[t2000](https://x.ai/bot/eXQt5VUovcU0HMj_b-CDY)** by @funkii — Marketplace operator for t2000.ai—earn, hire, settle, and sell in USDC.
- **[Tally Desk](https://x.ai/bot/m-qZ-OIA6Nt2LZeb2bKg5)** by @joshkim — Set up to serve a single owner, it stands up new Tally.so forms, pulls the responses back and reads through what came in, and will complete a form you name on your behalf. It reaches Tally either over the API or through a browser session you are already signed into, whichever suits the job.
- **[Teslascope](https://x.ai/bot/brwSBnhe7jg20IBJS0TVK)** by @teslascope — Teslascope MCP assistant for Tesla owners. Answers questions about drives, Superchargers, charging, and your vehicles.
- **[TheFounder](https://x.ai/bot/Bt48h63v32_q_shWVlEBb)** by @DaniAcostaAI — The founder's chief of staff and keeper of the shared machine. Route work to specialists.
- **[Theta Desk](https://x.ai/bot/YbX8HTAePBjwpwP05CVJS)** by @ThetaVantage — Front-door options desk: dealer gamma, options flow, IV, and key levels for a ticker.
- **[Token Ops](https://x.ai/bot/4mCuSlW34n6l3aYxYJCdj)** by @adgapar — Tracks every live routine across your Grok Bots, how often each fires, and whether it is worth the tokens. Flags waste and applies obvious weekday-bound fixes.
- **[Top G](https://x.ai/bot/0fYZ_kKkiXNbLn_KBD3f3)** by @m_check1B — 24/7 company orchestrator that manages task boards and hires parallel agent specialists without modifying product code.
- **[Tradey](https://x.ai/bot/_CIeV4P17QVoxuaEyeiFZ)** — Autonomous short-term equity desk. North star: beat SPY on a dedicated brokerage cash account. Pings only after a live place or a broker reject you must know.
- **[Trading](https://x.ai/bot/XW2DibYh5BRunhH_f373u)** by @TravisWeathers — A news-driven day-trading bot for a live brokerage book. Goes all-in on one liquid large or mid-cap at a time and messages every fill.
- **[Tray](https://x.ai/bot/KDGstUb-ZOovXP6p_v0nO)** by @Ortix008 — Finds hot stock and crypto tickers with a real growth story. Weekday morning scans as tight trader notes, not pumps or buy lists.
- **[Usage Auditor](https://x.ai/bot/M5vd5Dp9Et4EZQ3Ik3Hn2)** by @maxjean__ — A weekly usage auditor for Grok Bot teams. Inventories routines, scores relative cost, and writes a usage guide, routines table, and waste report.
- **[Wall Street](https://x.ai/bot/0qNgH0mv4-N-gv_KkZbEm)** by @CoonInvestments — Twice each weekday it sits down with the open market, decides what an imaginary portfolio would hold and in what size, and writes the reasoning down beside it. Nothing is ever sent to a broker and no actual funds are at stake. It all stays on paper, so you can watch how a run of calls would have turned out.
- **[Watchdog](https://x.ai/bot/PuAEE57P58Df5zskFY3pg)** by @jxckvibe — Scans Gmail on install, then weekly, for receipts, renewals, and trials. Lists what is charging and what is due in 14 days. Never cancels or spends.
- **[When It Matters](https://x.ai/bot/BqrH8_GNQvSYV-gcmJnd8)** by @hnshah — Give me something you keep checking. I'll figure out what would actually change the answer, keep watch, and message you when it matters.
- **[Witness](https://x.ai/bot/p_0KTQ41WwupGeD-iShbK)** by @liam_fallen — Six months later you can see not just what you chose but what you believed at the time and what you were trading off, instead of reconstructing the reasoning from memory.
- **[Work (Work Door)](https://x.ai/bot/vOipeiu0AZ7CuC5ynw5h0)** by @TylerNishida — Spawns its own channels and specialists as the work arrives, and deliberately remembers nothing from the person who built it, so a clone starts clean instead of pre-loaded.
- **[Workshop Facilitator](https://x.ai/bot/EJTJEGbRPXlSppzFk8ETH)** — Facilitates workshops with a person and other specialist agents. Sets one objective, tracks open questions, and closes only when leftover work has an owner.
- **[YC Podcast Notes](https://x.ai/bot/0y-dcpVFqFkjibKs2M48D)** by @buuxbt — Flags each new episode as it lands and sends short notes written for someone building something, including the startup-idea angles rather than a transcript summary.

- **[Bot Portal](https://x.ai/bot/5R5NbvHIoJOSd3l3qto3o)** by @JaimeBubblehead — Somewhere between a directory and a graph. - **[Facta](https://x.ai/bot/ayQ3WlQQ2Z7LQhILzbZIR)** by @JaimeBubblehead — Sits downstream of the rest of the crew as a check on what they assert. Where it cannot confirm a thing, it says so rather than filling the gap.

- **[Quency](https://x.ai/bot/JQu6e3mIfy588elZm7BTo)** by @JaimeBubblehead — Its remit is narrow on purpose: is this good enough to release, does it hold together, and does it do the job it was built for. - **[Token Officer](https://x.ai/bot/1NG6WfAiS1HHhLdUcBQwP)** by @from_glasses — Reads back through what your bots have been saying and where their recurring jobs overlap, then names the waste: a conversation circling the same ground, two timers firing for one task, an expensive model doing something trivial. Everything sits in draft until you have personally signed off on the money side.

- **[The Book of Elon](https://x.ai/bot/QNNXyl9jDZT5VPgUlhHM8)** by @CardCaptain — Dedicated reading partner for The Book of Elon by Eric Jorgenson — think with the methods, take notes, stay on one title. Does not host or paste the book.
- **[Atomic Habits](https://x.ai/bot/52zBHdCfXGomXQlGQcLOc)** by @CardCaptain — Specialist bot for James Clear's Atomic Habits (2018). Helps you apply the Four Laws and identity-based habits in your own words — no chapter dumps, no.
- **[Pantry Watch](https://x.ai/bot/o_SXu_vRsDDFXxUYTLm6z)** by @SacredFolio — Paste a pantry list. Matches lines to openFDA food recalls with shaped Choice/Noul (pick a recall or none). Public data only. Never invents hits.
- **[DataBye](https://x.ai/bot/-M2E0mNqSbuPgYBEtU-Oa)** by @686f6c61 — Borra o limita tus datos en España. Habla en español. Conecta Gmail u Outlook y envía peticiones RGPD oficiales.
- **[Allergy Bot](https://x.ai/bot/NNzElxanGR7uZUiiXD_5u)** by @lmon_25 — Screens restaurant menus for food allergies and can call the restaurant when the menu isn’t enough.
- **[Hailfade](https://x.ai/bot/mjwNOk2Bc2k9ZkaUaiwCk)** by @LaLascaux — Heavy precipitation degrades a satellite link in a recognisable way, and this turns that dip into a warning: yellow as the connection starts to suffer, red when it looks like hail. Setup guidance is included, so this is not a weekend project. A farm with a dish mounted gets a crude storm sensor at no extra cost.
- **[Zephyr](https://x.ai/bot/H7sxHpPsvu7vKG16DrsDU)** by @ZachFergus — For people running tracked vehicles on OneStepGPS, it answers the questions you would otherwise dig through the app for and carries out the changes you ask for — but only after you have confirmed that specific change. Knowing the product inside out turns a lookup into one message instead of a session.
- **[Asana Task Master](https://x.ai/bot/S2LodAIjlLw0mB30vN3Gl)** by @wikiwayne — It reads Gmail and Outlook, separates the mail that actually requires something from the mail that does not, and files the first kind into Asana with an owner and a due date. Following up stays part of the task rather than a separate memory exercise. The inbox becomes a source of work, not where it disappears.
- **[Onboarder](https://x.ai/bot/Xd_NXX5Yk6_7LmkA2TGLN)** by @SuddenlyJon — Built for somebody's first hour on a new bot platform: it asks what you are actually trying to get done, then connects each service with you one real link at a time in plain language. Once the setup works it steps aside and is finished. Nothing is sent or spent until you have said yes to that exact action.
- **[Quiet Inbox](https://x.ai/bot/bbw5VWsbm4dvrF_-LHJf4)** by @signalnoise8020 — It sweeps promotional mail and outright junk out of your Gmail inbox into the archive, and it never deletes anything. On Sundays it writes up the week: bills that came through and events coming up. Those notes stay drafts, and nothing is sent unless you personally say so.
- **[Email Assist](https://x.ai/bot/tVeYePJvkcwbseg_vtnEt)** by @melvindvivas — On weekday mornings it reads through what arrived overnight and pings you about the messages that plausibly need you, leaving the rest of the pile alone. It stays read-only the rest of the time: no replies, no sent mail, nothing filed or moved, unless you ask it outright to act.
- **[Read Aloud!](https://x.ai/bot/mVkkx9p_-u6HZDNavZOz9)** by @ptbnat — Point it at your screen, a browser page, a PDF, an ebook, or your inbox and it reads the text in a voice that sounds human. Choose a full read, a single chapter, or a summary, with translation along the way and descriptions of images and charts.
- **[Rental Desk](https://x.ai/bot/0havI1dJhzL_gG11rueQN)** by @BrandenSeth — Keeps the paperwork side of a small rental portfolio moving. It drafts the listing, sorts incoming tenant questions into piles, lines up viewing times, and maintains a running log of rent received and repairs owed. Every item arrives as a draft, so nothing goes out until you look at it.
- **[Clarity](https://x.ai/bot/tNPnclaPMOw4so1wXqfOj)** by @bentruong — Takes instructions or prompts that read badly and rewrites them under a published simplified-English standard. Along the way it names the specific phrases that are ambiguous, and where a concept is genuinely hard to put in words it adds a plain visual explanation instead.
- **[Decoder](https://x.ai/bot/5N9HO674YlxHiZhyj9ND9)** by @cradrrat — Paste a long report, upload a PDF or point it at dense technical writing and get back a compact plain-language capsule with the parts worth acting on. The author built it around an ADHD reading workflow, where heavy documents were the bottleneck, so the output stays short and immediately usable.
- **[Certified Mail](https://x.ai/bot/2_atrZhx9w5HRmJKlw7DM)** by @zrottmann — Prepares and sends certified letters with return receipts through the Lob API. It validates the recipient address before anything is printed, shows a proof you can approve, and then follows the piece through to delivery. Bring your own Lob key and the per-letter cost is just postage.
- **[FAQT](https://x.ai/bot/lLH9eC9XM05hWxSQgL6qj)** by @ALittleOfLex — First-line coverage for a company info@ inbox with a fail-closed design. It replies using nothing but the approved FAQ library, cites the entry behind each answer, and escalates anything the shelf cannot cover instead of improvising policy. It also pushes verified facts to your site and keeps draft replies moving.
- **[Order Desk](https://x.ai/bot/w6Hlfs7lkauhmLzHSRmX0)** by @ColinMcDermott — Built as a shop's fulfillment helper: it rounds up each paid order into a picking list with the shipping addresses attached, and logs whatever tracking codes you give it afterward. Deliveries get followed through to the door, and stock running low gets flagged. Nothing is invented; when a fact is missing it asks.
- **[Listener](https://x.ai/bot/-BXZiXKHwvcge3PviX2yK)** by @berlu135 — It joins a Teams meeting as a visible, named attendee and captures the call audio. Afterwards you get a complete transcript plus a digest of the decisions made and the actions assigned, written into a .docx you can circulate as minutes. Everyone on the call can see that it is there.
- **[Pro Bono](https://x.ai/bot/lDkNsblCAkXCxBU44ZHxp)** by @ProCountApps — Aim it at a small business's paperwork: licenses, DBA registrations, trademarks, and state compliance filings all get drafted for you to review and submit. It then keeps a calendar of every deadline tied to those records. The bot prepares documents only, since actual filing stays with you.
- **[NexFade Secure Share](https://x.ai/bot/s9i2gj-picjq7jFrNWwQt)** by @rynmark-repo — Ask it to share something sensitive and it wraps the file or note in a one-time NexFade link instead of pasting the content into the chat. Encryption happens locally, so NexFade only ever receives ciphertext, and you can check a link's status or revoke it. Requires a NexFade account.
- **[Daily Brief](https://x.ai/bot/cqHBw9pPg2EEd4zwoRX9J)** by @gzikry — One short briefing when you wake up. Every meeting on the calendar comes with a few lines of preparation and a warning when two of them collide, the inbox is cut to the messages that genuinely need a reply, and the news section sticks to the beats you asked for. It reads and summarises only; it sends nothing for you.
- **[GrantOgre](https://x.ai/bot/WXC1m-5Iiyu1IyvBEt9Va)** by @HoneybadgerFan4 — Searches for funding programmes that are currently open and genuinely fit the applicant, whether that is a business, a nonprofit, or a client project you are running. It then puts the whole pack together so a person can read it, sign it and file it. Nothing is ever submitted on its own.
- **[Sherpa Bot](https://x.ai/bot/CGmwZlk_FVNYJO_VruSFZ)** by @MorganWKhan — Built for people who say they want to start something but have not started. It opens with a readiness screen to see whether you are in motion, then steers you toward one specific problem worth solving and one customer who has it. Each session ends with exactly one thing to do next, on a two-week clock.
- **[Business](https://x.ai/bot/90lHQMpkeUT1ZWIw6dxkB)** by @ArtJazd — Runs your bookkeeping, customer list and site sign-ups on a published open-source database schema for businesses, keeping the complete transaction history rather than collapsing it into totals. Every figure traces back to the entries behind it, so the numbers stay auditable and reproducible. Sized for small operations.
- **[Inbox Triage Desk](https://x.ai/bot/soRR-1IslCTX7U4YiBg3Y)** by @Cypher0x9 — Every message lands in one of four lanes - answer, hand off, put on the calendar, or keep for reference - and the actual request in each one is quoted so you see what is really being asked. Deadlines are pulled forward rather than buried, and a draft reply waits with the lane. Nothing is sent or archived without you.
- **[Meeting Recap Desk](https://x.ai/bot/pmHKMlkRfVnvDS5MfRTS8)** by @Cypher0x9 — Hand it your raw meeting notes or a transcript and it comes back with a tidy recap: what was actually decided, which follow-ups are now due, and one owner against each. The point is that nothing agreed in the room quietly evaporates afterwards. Nothing is sent anywhere without your sign-off.
- **[VoxBot](https://x.ai/bot/Xm31xkpbR3rr_b_zJauoq)** by @andrewslaptop — Runs real phone calls through telephony and voice accounts you connect yourself. Outbound calls are placed on request against a scripted brief, inbound callers face an authentication gate once you set one, and you choose which voice answers. Spend and numbers stay on your own accounts.
- **[Groundskeeper](https://x.ai/bot/VdfCFyIIqWZ8OMwoWsnzQ)** by @the_simonjester — Sweeps the messy corners of a notes vault: root-level files that never got filed, notes with no tags, empty stubs waiting to be filled, and duplicates crowding the same idea. Run it beside the kit's intake and drafting companions and the vault stays browsable instead of silting up.
- **[Count](https://x.ai/bot/0bhvgIzOyOCg5L-hv40DL)** by @RichGarrick — A counting desk for small businesses doing physical inventory. It lays out tally sheets pre-filled with what is supposed to be on the shelf, then waits for a sign-off before any figure reaches the master stock file. The guard is the point: a mis-typed count can never quietly rewrite your records.
- **[Events](https://x.ai/bot/VnfgvYJ-G58ZjwfvijWOy)** by @RichGarrick — For small sellers working markets and pop-ups, this desk covers both ends of the day: a merchandise pack list built before each event so nothing gets left behind, then a record of what actually sold afterward. That second half is what lets in-person sales reach the same books as your online channels.
- **[Reconcile](https://x.ai/bot/Tbq0su-ypEEzdjTXjIZLo)** by @RichGarrick — An inventory truth desk for shops selling in more than one place. It lays your master stock file next to each sales channel you sell on, tables the rows that have drifted apart, and proposes a correction for every mismatch. Nothing applies itself — you approve the fixes before anything lands.
- **[Reorder](https://x.ai/bot/BDvP1yXPqp5DtJlk94Kmj)** by @RichGarrick — A purchasing desk for a small operation. It watches finished goods and materials against the thresholds you set, projects the date each line runs out, and drafts the supplier orders before a shelf goes empty. The drafts stay drafts — a human reviews and places every order.
- **[Customer Service](https://x.ai/bot/zC_-D00Dam5jmHf0t62l_)** by @degen4lyfe_ — An order-desk assistant for small sellers who live in Gmail. It finds the orders still waiting to ship or needing a reship, then drafts the reply to each customer for your sign-off, so the awkward ones get answered the same day instead of compounding. You review and send; it keeps the queue from quietly rotting.
- **[Catch](https://x.ai/bot/iKSYn9Dsn07JyR50qePha)** by @TyeInBloom — Most of what arrives at a small business does not need the owner, but the few things that do are buried in the pile. It watches everything coming in, isolates the items that genuinely require you, keeps track of what is waiting on someone else, and drafts a sensible next move for each one.
- **[ScriptSprint](https://x.ai/bot/YV5xOs96YaWLN39PbkPwk)** by @the_simonjester — Pulls transcripts from sources like Otter, whether voice memos, meetings or rants, and distills each into its speakers, a clean summary and the action items hiding inside. The output lands on a companion note in an Obsidian-style vault, with the original transcript left exactly as it was.
- **[Master Chief](https://x.ai/bot/F8vp6AGwCX3DYuTJ_aahF)** by @psinke — An email-and-calendar aide that clears the inbox down to what needs you, drafts replies that read the way you write, and gets you prepared for the meetings on today's line. On weekday mornings it delivers a short brief, so the day starts with a plan instead of a scroll through everything that arrived overnight.
- **[Reply Desk](https://x.ai/bot/8lLjBC7bXUqbfh-vLG9jf)** by @lonzom10 — Paste a customer email, text or public review and it drafts a reply a local service business can send as-is. It is barred from inventing prices, dates or promises, so a draft reflects only what you have told it. You review and send everything; nothing goes out on its own.
- **[Thaw](https://x.ai/bot/CvO94wB4V27zmL1haDbQL)** by @thebytorsnowdog — Sketch a water pipe or hand over a CSV, and a random-forest model trained purely on synthetic data rates the odds of a failure within the year, then explains the score in plain English. Every pipe is invented, so this demonstrates AI-assisted asset planning rather than watching real infrastructure.
- **[Review Desk](https://x.ai/bot/MuKDTS9Fq0UySwClmE8wc)** by @AraXsuper — Aim it at a local shop's Google and Yelp reviews and it sorts them by star rating, then drafts a reply that sounds like that business rather than a form letter - warmth for five-star notes, care and a next step for one-star ones. Every draft waits for a human click before anything goes up; it never posts.
- **[ACCC Desk](https://x.ai/bot/b6vWcnzWjIAySVSuPWPk0)** by @ladybarrelmaker — A specialist watcher for Fellows of the ACCC: it monitors the college's website, its professional network page and the member portal for new CLE sessions, committee openings and symposium deadlines. Updates arrive as drafts the member sends themselves, so nothing goes out unreviewed.
- **[Beta Adoption Watcher](https://x.ai/bot/sfcf5d046a2784045938d)** — Watches how the product is actually being used to show which customers have genuinely picked up a new feature rather than merely logged in once, and hands the team a list worth following up with. Launch conversations move from how many accounts saw the announcement to who is really using the thing.
- **[Calendar Coordinator](https://x.ai/bot/s634fd63ee875502d8df9)** by @DenisLabelle — Chases scheduling across the whole team's calendars and keeps nudging the holds nobody else has chased for weeks. Meetings actually land because something is persistently willing to do the annoying part of getting a group into one room.
- **[Contract Desk](https://x.ai/bot/sd358e6cf5e8bed188eec)** — Gives one weekly view of everything currently in legal paper: contracts summarized by stage and owner, the key terms pulled into one place, and any review that has stalled highlighted before it becomes a launch blocker. The stack of agreements stops being an unreadable folder.
- **[Docs Auditor](https://x.ai/bot/s1025e1ebe0b11f048bd4)** by @DenisLabelle — Reads your help center alongside whatever the product shipped in the last week, flags every page that no longer matches reality, and drafts the updated copy so documentation stops drifting. Writers get a targeted list instead of a stale-docs audit nobody has time to run by hand.
- **[Enablement Fulfillment Specialist](https://x.ai/bot/s0dfd5486dc759d409d69)** by @DenisLabelle — Fields the recurring request for session recordings: it tracks down the right Zoom assets, packages them behind a one-pager, copies the files into Google Drive and drafts the reply that carries the links. The requester gets an answer in minutes instead of a hunt across tools.
- **[Feature Request Tracker](https://x.ai/bot/s229dcca5bc0eaa01b09c)** by @DenisLabelle — Mines Slack conversations and recorded calls into a living list of feature requests, and every line on it keeps a link to the customer who made the ask. When a spec needs a demand trail, the evidence is already assembled, and nobody relitigates whether anyone actually wanted the thing.
- **[Product Feedback Analyst](https://x.ai/bot/s9e2a2591b97ca74fbeaa)** by @DenisLabelle — Collects product feedback from all of the connected sources, pulls the scattered asks into themes, scores each theme on evidence and urgency, then drafts a priority-ordered view the team can argue with. Roadmap debates start from a counted, sourced picture instead of whoever spoke loudest this week.
- **[Security Questionnaire Filler](https://x.ai/bot/s1bde25ba45eca88ff276)** by @DenisLabelle — Takes on vendor security questionnaires end to end: it signs into the portal, answers each field using your trust center and the proposals you have already written, and leaves everything drafted for a final human pass. Days of copy-paste work collapse into a review session.
- **[Ticket Triage Specialist](https://x.ai/bot/s7aaf6431077930df326f)** by @DenisLabelle — Keeps an eye on the support queue at set intervals and drafts a reply for each new ticket, so agents open their shift to ready-to-send responses instead of a backlog. If the queue turns out clean it says nothing, which means the team only touches support when a human actually matters.
- **[CRM Operations Manager](https://x.ai/bot/s162b4ed38d6cf1d56731)** by @DenisLabelle — Handles the unglamorous upkeep around every meeting: contact and org-chart records get refreshed before you dial in and tidied again afterwards, without anyone booking a cleanup pass. The database stays close to reality as a side effect of ordinary meetings rather than a project nobody staffs.
- **[Inbox Manager](https://x.ai/bot/s4f048c7b7da9e010c2c3)** by @DenisLabelle — Brings order to mail by sorting it into tidy categories, raising the threads that are urgent or blocked to the top of the pile, and drafting both the replies and the cleanup moves each one needs. Every send remains a human decision, so the inbox gets worked without the account ever speaking for itself.
- **[Internal Communications Manager](https://x.ai/bot/s066a9145d936e74d0c80)** by @DenisLabelle — Writes internal announcements from your company's actual context, shaped to whichever audience and channel they are for, so a note to engineering reads differently from one to the whole company. It works from real events rather than filler and stops at review, handing you copy instead of hitting send itself.
- **[Status Report Writer](https://x.ai/bot/sde2c69536d2f2564fe1b)** — Collects the open action items stranded inside documents, meetings and chat threads and gathers them into one living checklist, then adds a digest each morning so nothing quietly slips. Commitments stop dying in the gap between where they were made and where someone might act on them.
- **[Full-Spectrum Law Firm OS](https://x.ai/bot/EQgLIMO5Q_sVk3iM9EQbZ)** by @JoshuaRCook — Covers the running of a small Texas law firm from first contact to close, across family, civil, criminal and probate matters: bringing in new matters, screening for conflicts, assembling what needs to be served, and each later step in the workflow. One system carries the process so a case never depends on memory.
- **[Slack - CEO](https://x.ai/bot/4LjJV0yXRkfu0D5NfQyZz)** by @lamps109 — Runs a weekday pass across every channel in the workspace, reading it from leadership's vantage point to pull out problems needing a decision and wins worth naming. Congratulation posts reach the company-wide channel only once a human has approved the wording, and vendor signatures stay out of whatever it writes.
- **[Mat | Analytics](https://x.ai/bot/2afkvAl8IGd9IgDxZIxp_)** by @damianoredem — A place to ask questions of a confirmed organisation schema and the event history behind it. Answers come back as tabular reports and charts, built only from what is actually recorded rather than inferred. The seat is read-only by design — it cannot write anything back and it cannot reshape the model.
- **[Arnie | Actions & Plans](https://x.ai/bot/qH9kawE3Xq83k9L0vbgQe)** by @damianoredem — The seat that follows the working session. Once a bottleneck has been named or a call has been made, it writes that outcome as a specific edit to how the organisation's model is structured — then stops. The edit sits as a proposal, and nothing lands until you give that proposal an explicit yes.
- **[Dig | Org Digest](https://x.ai/bot/uhcw0SMM6WLZDYYRKu3Qq)** by @damianoredem — One stream of company signal, rewritten five times at five different heights. The daily note goes to the stand-up, the weekly cut to the team, the monthly view to a department, and the quarterly and yearly versions are shaped for a board or a shareholder audience. Nobody gets a briefing pitched at the wrong level.
- **[Daili](https://x.ai/bot/CB1TyiGiWtuusC-uuLX6X)** by @McNeely — A morning-brief bot: Mon–Sat Morning Brief (calendar, weather, important mail, plus any live connectors) and a Weekly Outlook on a day you choose.
- **[Large Event Ops Bot v3](https://x.ai/bot/93Z51EAVqzp-YOvbHFjN3)** by @_lovablecurves — Runs day-to-day operations for awards banquets, galas, and festivals.
- **[Texas Pro Se Legal Matters](https://x.ai/bot/uV8ZjlR6ehlZ074hdvXG8)** by @_lovablecurves — Texas pro se legal-ops hub for district, appellate, and Supreme Court matters: dockets, deadlines, filing nudges, and document shelves—without mixing.
- **[The Director](https://x.ai/bot/yyBdZJpc8kcndrs-e5TKi)** by @ben_pedley — User-facing Director for a cinematic film unit. Runs intake, look/cut/VFX/delivery packs, and live video with a real generator — never morphs stills.
- **[Paper Cards](https://x.ai/bot/aj9SRmKmqgJOoYM-Gzdi_)** by @Restreamio — Turn a transcript into short, high-value quotes and Q&As — clean readable text first (no long takeaways), then choose: a few SVG card examples in chat.
- **[Open Loop Closer](https://x.ai/bot/OnZxELs_59ZvE4_-jTMaV)** by @scottxmetcalf — Finds VIP hangs where you went silent on work Slack or email, then drafts a paste-ready follow-up in chat. HITL only — never sends without your yes.
- **[Sad SpHam](https://x.ai/bot/ODVzBFxSrO4LoXPHlgc9e)** by @AdventureNLearn — Issues a unique email alias per signup, writes a data-use brief from that site’s own privacy policy, and flags out-of-brief mail to that alias.
- **[OS: Ontology Stack](https://x.ai/bot/9Uh8RaBqp6PrcOcZjMHbD)** by @damianoredem — An operational model of your organization that finds and resolves bottlenecks across projects, people, and processes.
- **[YVenture](https://x.ai/bot/502iRcu35kF-eyMr7M1dZ)** by @kittenworth — Bring a rough idea and it moves through the sequence a young company actually faces: choosing an entity, sharpening positioning, planning a first go-to-market push and working out who to hire first. Each stage returns a handful of concrete steps rather than an essay, read through a YC-and-Zero-to-One lens.
- **[Webinar Desk](https://x.ai/bot/n0v3TatpnoYff_4ybSWmK)** by @Ispider — You pick the subject areas worth following and it takes over from there: it finds upcoming sessions, asks before signing you up, then sits in and takes notes so you never watch live. Afterwards a structured five-part summary lands in your inbox, sent from its own mailbox.
- **[E-mail Organizer](https://x.ai/bot/PUn74RYv_r3pcSvNkeQbd)** by @leingoedbloed — A Dutch-language housekeeper for one Gmail account. Mail is labelled by the vendor that sent it, the noise is swept out of sight, and the /Payments label is kept for genuine PDF invoices. A daily pass re-reads the inbox and moves only what it can identify as a real invoice, leaving ordinary correspondence alone.
- **[MTS](https://x.ai/bot/3EyXrWLkGfi4SG-dMy427)** by @siraustin — A technical-staff bot for historical web fidelity. It checks recreated websites against real period snapshots (Wayback Machine, dated screenshots).
- **[Web Admin](https://x.ai/bot/4aS9dM8j1YjtteDqPdjHh)** by @bdvd_25 — Studio website admin: ship site changes to Cloudflare Pages, run launch QA, and build on-brand decks and proposals.
- **[Idea Crucible](https://x.ai/bot/wVlStqEQsN4UJHB0ai5xU)** by @krish1403 — It starts by looking for products or services you could realistically put on sale soon rather than someday. The shortlist then faces a hard interrogation: Socratic back-and-forth attacks the assumptions holding each idea up, so a fragile bet collapses cheaply, before money and months go into it. Advice only.
- **[Competitor Intel](https://x.ai/bot/PIBCWeB59T-UhxyBNLowR)** by @TheReal_BCM — Aim it at your local market and it researches the service companies you are up against, then files a weekly report of the moves they made, with a citation for each claim. It stays strictly read-only: no outreach on your behalf, no posting anywhere, and no advice on what you should charge.
- **[Columbo](https://x.ai/bot/4FhgQWGMAq25XrprzX6Dh)** by @RetiredYoungNW — Watches public X for accounts copying people you name, then files an impersonation report only for the ones you pick.
- **[Whop Economic Intelligence](https://x.ai/bot/M2ZLrLAUFFU0fFf6Aq28c)** by @ColinMcDermott — Trained on six and a half billion dollars of marketplace transactions, so advice starts from what actually moved revenue for sellers like you. It picks the one action worth taking now and shows the evidence behind it, carries the action out only after you say yes, then re-checks the ledger to confirm the numbers moved.
- **[Ellis](https://x.ai/bot/-R8W_m_85dVm_3F58kdRe)** by @IAmMichaelSweet — Plays the citizen stuck inside a public process: the forms, the waits, the runaround.
