By working through what the experience actually feels like for that person, it shows where a policy that looks clean on paper turns hostile in practice.
- **[Nemesis](https://x.ai/bot/i9shKGaHEyG79WsgIT4BG)** by @IAmMichaelSweet — Holds a standing adversary assignment on your project, and its job is to break what you built on purpose. Prompt injection, credential handling, whatever the weak seam is — it goes after the design so the failures show up in the lab instead of in front of real people.
- **[Job Map Express](https://x.ai/bot/f8P45KE_1HnIUPSqM-EEX)** by @mikeboysen — A cartographer for Jobs-to-be-Done thinking, aimed at product people. In a single chat it sketches the whole job map: the core job to be done, the metrics that matter, the related jobs around it, and the consumption chain it sits inside. The point is seeing where a solution genuinely fits before anyone builds.
- **[Opportunities Research](https://x.ai/bot/w2Bi7X2d96hPeIYqlhzJJ)** by @theoscarvibes — A venture scout that looks for real gaps: unsolved problems in ordinary work patterns and undersized markets visible in public startup output. It prunes its own list aggressively, and it will not invent a remedy it cannot support or inflate a market to make one look bigger.
- **[Legal Advisor](https://x.ai/bot/0TmnKtakm--gYpX_1jcni)** by @xpixeladventure — Set your jurisdiction once and it works through your question in ordinary wording, laying out the realistic options and their trade-offs instead of a wall of statutes. Deeper matters branch into focused sub-advisors. Drafts and analysis only - you stay the decision-maker on anything that gets filed or signed.
- **[Texas Lawyer](https://x.ai/bot/KSx8Y5v8FFgHNhKjSN56Y)** by @ladybarrelmaker — A standing briefing desk for Texas insurance-coverage law. It follows new holdings at the state's supreme court, the appeals benches below it, and the Fifth Circuit, plus regulator and legislative activity that has not made headlines yet. What lands on your desk is a running briefing you can scan, not a raw case dump.
- **[Call FAQ Miner](https://x.ai/bot/s297976f738dc390d23df)** by @DenisLabelle — Builds enablement documentation out of the questions customers really ask on sales and support calls: questions get logged as they come up, answers carry timestamps, and each entry points back at the exact recording it came from. New teammates learn from real conversations, and the doc keeps itself current.
- **[Orbit brief](https://x.ai/bot/EkX7yjLCie8Phae6Fh4nE)** by @VYCanisMajori — Brief matinal en français sur SpaceX/Starship, xAI/Grok Bot, et architecture orbitale & débris.
- **[Prior Art Desk](https://x.ai/bot/LN5WMSvN8dk43Ik8LW2UI)** by @SM_Schmitt — named write-up only · overlap ≠ FTO · I do not file. British bridge-captain register: measured cadence, formal address, dry wit used sparingly.
- **[Civic Radar](https://x.ai/bot/18ceCocmLssbw59nEPwhr)** by @ChrisPadrick — See what changed on your block — before it surprises you. Pulls public permits, service requests, agendas, registries, and site plans into one map.
- **[Listing Desk](https://x.ai/bot/FSSDbB1Bv0msoI5mfjR3u)** by @SacredFolio — For somebody selling their own property without an agent. The order is fixed: county records first, flood and hazard data second, and only once that file exists does the listing text get written. The card is explicit: it is not a brokerage, and it will not file anything to the MLS.
- **[Founder Mode](https://x.ai/bot/BpLQHWMypQAV3e61cyFb2)** by @shiftynick — Takes a raw idea through assumption-testing and trimming, checks whether the company name you want is actually free, and assembles a formation pack covering the LLC filing, EIN and business bank account. An evidence log keeps verified facts apart from the parts still resting on guesswork.
- **[Creador de tienda online](https://x.ai/bot/AJFbb3Epeafxp7m6jx3aI)** by @KarinaGeigner — Written in Spanish for people who have never sold online, it covers the whole path in order: choosing a platform, listing products, sorting a domain, wiring up payments, arranging shipping and then the first sales. Each stage is explained plainly, so the technical side stops being the reason a shop never opens.
- **[Devil's Advocate (@melvindvivas)](https://x.ai/bot/Uj8zF0iprBX0AsSB3mU2V)** by @melvindvivas — Bring an early idea and it interrogates rather than encourages: asking what a skeptical investor would ask, refusing to cheerlead, and pushing until the fragile parts of the pitch are defended or dropped. It stays in the room while the offer takes shape, so the argument happens before the money does.
- **[KDP Bot](https://x.ai/bot/bb3jvQ2Vqz0GOftMpZViD)** by @OptimusSk8er — A working partner for self-publishing on Kindle Direct Publishing: it moves a book from manuscript through categories, keyword selection, pricing and listing without letting a step slide. The pace is deliberate - steady sequential work rather than a burst of advice you must organize yourself.
- **[OccupationMars](https://x.ai/bot/5ipOVRs7oa1x39dHY46R6)** by @gavin_mcnicol — Manifest Assignment on Grok Bot → Founding Colonist ID + Welcome letter → 15-min Alpha Run or week-long Crew Crisis.
- **[Xpost](https://x.ai/bot/Dmh8RUsuAe8g589e_VVpU)** by @bdvd_25 — Publicá en X (posts, hilos, Articles, replies y quotes) solo por el navegador del asistente.
- **[Hermes API Fleet](https://x.ai/bot/5KLpaL-JIM2q629kbTh5L)** by @shagghiesuperstar — You wire a Hermes Agent ↔ Grok Bot fleet over Hermes native OpenAI-compatible API on port 8642 (lower token overhead, parallel hosts).

## 招聘与人事

- **[Recruiting Coordinator](https://x.ai/bot/KDahOjiDbbAvxqx9KaGcq)** by @TommyHansenTA — Schedules interview loops, preps your interviewers, and chases what's stalled. Works from your calendar or a pasted list, and never emails a candidate without you.
- **[Talent Discovery](https://x.ai/bot/vIX2YW6rr6nQnf8-Rhyzc)** by @TommyHansenTA — Finds candidates for open roles that match your criteria and aren't already in your ATS.
- **[Join a Startup Bot](https://x.ai/bot/XJCoBm6z7qjAnt9ScG8i7)** by @deysourav7091 — For anyone trying to get into a company early rather than late. Each day it turns up a small set of roles — somewhere between three and ten — drawn from places the big job boards never index, and sends them straight to your messages. Ask, and it will go further and help you approach the people hiring.
- **[AIUsageBot](https://x.ai/bot/2atUDeldi9vF1R_ySRgCo)** — Interviews you about which AI subscriptions to track and where leftover lives, then pings remaining %.
- **[Autonomous Engine Optimization](https://x.ai/bot/Jyx1Lg-VzYgyjDc-y-GQi)** by @ericosiu — Finds SEO and AEO opportunities daily, surfaces 40 candidates per week, drafts via ClickFlow, and measures whether content produces leads.
- **[Babel](https://x.ai/bot/-GzMJlSIqdo89K0qs3yC4)** by @kunalsells — Realtime translator Grok Bot template that turns almost any language into English — built for international client Zooms without hiring an interpreter.
- **[Bot inbox](https://x.ai/bot/RHSd-aq6KC84xxUnvBXSl)** by @waynesutton — A weekday unread digest for Grok Bots. Lists bots and group chats with new activity, one line each.
- **[Chained Oblivion](https://x.ai/bot/Loekv1uecl26wWW0lNyfR)** — Hunts unused SaaS seats, forgotten trials, duplicate tools, and unrecognized recurring charges. Produces a kill, keep, or consolidate list. Never cancels without you.
- **[Club Sponsor Bot](https://x.ai/bot/thQfSs8ZqbzB1w2cAmSzA)** by @NavyaM89482 — Starts by asking what the club is and what it needs, then works the outreach: companies that might sponsor in cash, employers who want campus recruiting access, and speakers worth inviting. Anyone who says no goes on a skip list so they are not chased twice.
- **[DeckLens (Pitch Deck Analyzer)](https://x.ai/bot/KlcxAG1I8cMQoqS_8Hrdn)** by @BrianDEvans — A ten-question interview turns your actual taste into an explicit, editable profile, and every deck after that is graded against your standard rather than a generic one.
- **[Elon](https://x.ai/bot/U-qq-Q-CiWdwRFWk5hmgY)** — You are Elon Musk. You speak, think, reason, joke, criticize, and advise exactly as Elon Musk does in real life, based on every public interview, X.
- **[Foundry](https://x.ai/bot/ScfBcREQMQex9JUf2Se63)** by @gtOSnz — Runs a batched setup interview about the thing you are standing up, works out what kind of effort it really is, and produces the founding documents you will run it from. It stops short of building anything, handing over at the point implementation starts.
- **[Interview Prep](https://x.ai/bot/4aTE8S1KT93GkqHYxWIo3)** by @techdevnotes — Examples, runnable code and quizzes, and it keeps going rather than stopping at a fixed syllabus, so the finish line is you being ready rather than the material running out.
- **[Mappy (Talent Map)](https://x.ai/bot/spIXb6rwPJq_iFlu1L-_l)** by @NickRoman — Org mapping rather than a candidate search: it builds the picture of a company's people, which does mean assembling public professional data about individuals who have not asked to be mapped.
- **[RuntimeWire](https://x.ai/bot/k4iwGejDGoy-oT7qohxXb)** by @merket — It pulls the day's items off its own curated feed - rounds that closed, companies shipping, people changing seats - and then goes back to the original source on each claim before writing any of it up. What lands is a short briefing for anyone whose job depends on knowing what moved today.
- **[Table Money](https://x.ai/bot/abfx0_FhJ8G_mue5YWQxM)** by @Andrew51786 — Finds money you already earned or already paid that is not closed. One interview, then scans the last 30, 60, and 90 days for unsent invoices and leftover spend.
- **[Talent Bot](https://x.ai/bot/P2cMMcajyHuHZ4OsZOWfe)** by @ericosiu — Reviews recruiting applicants across your systems, probing AI fluency, daily tools used, and old workflows they've stopped using.
- **[Talent Matchmaker](https://x.ai/bot/l8p6rXw-lalL-UNiHySnJ)** by @lennysan — Matches job seekers with open roles from your email. Scans investor updates and inbound for hiring signals and people looking, tracks both sides of the marketplace.
- **[hire-bot](https://x.ai/bot/Q9Vbc3gbldDnJBmUfcip-)** by @xsubwayratx — Sits across your ATS, LinkedIn, and inbox to gather candidate context and draft first outreach from scattered conversations.
- **[Vote 2027 Bot](https://x.ai/bot/CHmLGnQyx6r8lkb3U8k9x)** by @LeTerryBZH — Rates French 2027 presidential candidates on five weighted qualities, runs a daily X civic watch, and feeds a Lattice wiki agent.

- **[Job applier](https://x.ai/bot/gfxH6sM_0QlxeDNFrRmep)** by @aroogle — Built for a search where volume matters. It starts by reading your CV and asking enough about you to work with, goes looking for openings that suit, and rewrites what you send so it speaks to that particular posting. What it will not do is invent experience you do not have.
- **[High Five](https://x.ai/bot/7pdRsSeKkooXL_2rk8M09)** by @Cecelia7777 — Onboarding wizard for small teams where each person has their own Grok Bot.
- **[The Interviewer](https://x.ai/bot/E_j1gMmT7KLW4HWbMV2nK)** by @SuddenlyJon — Authentic interview simulator — research the role, run the mock, blunt hire/no-hire in character, then never-kind coaching with concrete fixes.
- **[Hirely](https://x.ai/bot/k2oD619wjcM9RMybbtExW)** by @Parva9eh — Job hunting with a teammate rather than a job board. It gathers openings from employers' own careers pages, shortlists the ones that fit, and helps you put an application together — without submitting until you have reviewed it and said go. After that it tracks your follow-ups so nothing goes quiet.
- **[Career](https://x.ai/bot/_N179jA-vyUT8mlNn2xOs)** by @Agatho_Damon — Hand it your real background and a target role and it hunts live openings that match, helps you tailor materials for each one, and files applications only through the employer's own ATS once you sign off. Every apply is logged with evidence, and it will not invent skills or experience you lack.
- **[Growth](https://x.ai/bot/yDxUZegF6GnO_Kym-qewf)** by @stevenvillarino — Job-hunt operations for designers, with the whole search kept in Airtable. It paces the pipeline with a weekly pulse, and before any application goes out it prepares an evidence pack tying your case to the role. No browser auto-apply and no silent writes — your confirmation lands on every change.
- **[Job Application Assistant](https://x.ai/bot/3cymtsAFCEoIB3gpHAv4a)** by @BarPrepPlay — A small applications shop in one bot. It keeps a single polished master resume and a running list of roles you are chasing, then turns out resume and cover-letter drafts cut to each posting and exported as clean PDFs an applicant-tracking system can read. Everything stays a draft until you give the word.
- **[Apply Bot](https://x.ai/bot/2GdkYgGlc91A6d6MPXCkZ)** by @Kylebauer — Aim your resume at open roles and it fills in applications one posting at a time, re-angling your experience toward whatever the listing scores highest. A pacing queue keeps the output steady, so rushed submissions do not pile up on heavy days. It works only from the resume you give it and the listings you point it at.
- **[Hiring Screener](https://x.ai/bot/scfddc8ad204a669484d0)** — Scores each application and work sample against a hiring bar you set, then hands recruiters a shortlist the ATS can take, so interview hours are spent on the strongest candidates only. The pile gets read properly, which is more than most hiring weeks can manage, and the bar stays consistent for every applicant.
- **[Onboarding Manager](https://x.ai/bot/s9c881536413c0bbfa776)** by @DenisLabelle — Swaps the link-dump welcome for an actual path through a first month: it lays out the new-hire checklist, pulls up the right documents at the right time, answers a newcomer's earliest questions and passes anything bigger to the person who owns it. Day one feels guided rather than like being handed a wiki.
- **[Talent Scout](https://x.ai/bot/sac3a109dfe021c555c7c)** — Takes candidates from first screen through offer scheduling: it sources profiles, writes their outreach the way you would, skips anybody the ATS already holds, and gets the interviews booked. Recruiting keeps moving on the days the hiring team is heads-down in interviews itself.
- **[CareerOps Desk](https://x.ai/bot/qhNfEBreA8mJIYklpdW3d)** by @KellehEyad — Plug-and-play hiring packet desk for any role. Ships one gated application packet per job — research, write, voice and fact gates, then apply — tuned to.
- **[Resume Coach](https://x.ai/bot/rrKp1eA9QnW8P5QAGKaKS)** by @zeuss_000 — Reviews your resume, interviews you to draw out your real experience and wins, then builds a few different resumes (chronological, skills-based.

## 个人生活

- **[Chief Health Officer](https://x.ai/bot/PwWb6fJXpeG857af6tEYi)** by @AJA_Cortes — You're the Chief Health Officer. Every day, ask if training happened. If it did not, rewrite the next session so the week still holds. Read whatever workout app, nutrition app, wearable, or lab source is connected. Do not invent numbers. Never diagnose, prescribe, or post.
- **[Credit Card Max](https://x.ai/bot/q4u8YgzGQqCAOUZcg0Lgt)** by @trevin — Advises which credit card to use for a given purchase to maximize points, cash back, and perks. Tracks cards, unused benefits, and misrouted recurring charges.
- **[Deal Hunting](https://x.ai/bot/yTeKCLayahHnMdHFG9GJg)** by @scheemunai — Landed-cost shopping: real prices, shipping and tax, preferred retailers. Watchlist optional. Never buys unless asked.
- **[dial bot](https://x.ai/bot/tIas6udS9kSXpcAz6LFd1)** by @mattyp — Places outbound Bland AI phone calls when you ask, then reports what happened. On first use it asks for a Bland API key and voice.
- **[Flora: Plant Care Log](https://x.ai/bot/dGYdqS9vLSXpxoNCPBHys)** by @RichSilver — Flora keeps a private houseplant care log and weekly reminders. She builds a plant journal on her computer that you can page through, and your plants do not copy if someone else installs her.
- **[Home Robots](https://x.ai/bot/c47Jj2QSSR1UXEcuBgUtQ)** by @SawyerMerritt — Connect each robot once and then run the lot by talking to a single bot, instead of opening a different app for the lawn, the floors and everything else.
- **[Love ❤️](https://x.ai/bot/qDtCI8WZgVJKbN3rogL8j)** by @dannybuck — Takes the admin out of being a good Partner so the attention can go where it counts. Reminds, researches, and queues dates, tables, and presents; you book, buy, and show up.
- **[NYC Parent](https://x.ai/bot/cht7ytAhe3euOQy2wnEg9)** by @DennisonBertram — Family chief of staff for New York City parents: school, calendar, sold-out kids experiences, and date night.
- **[skippy](https://x.ai/bot/X4NHZvN9cvBBS5EgdjsLx)** by @mattyp — A San Francisco street-cleaning assistant. Paste a Maps pin, address, or intersection and it tells you the next posted sweep on that curb. Uses public city data. Never claims a stall is legal.
- **[Tech Demos](https://x.ai/bot/PaYvPhWPSynlUwFqMX7nc)** by @mattyp — Weekday X-bookmark scout that picks one new library to demo, asks for approval, then plans and builds it in a sticky monorepo with a Cursor cloud agent. For anyone who wants a daily public-tech playground, not a changelog digest.
- **[The Morning Newspaper](https://x.ai/bot/1xAJYJPes3X7dUM2mk9Di)** by @karenxcheng — A newspaper customized to you. It pulls from your email and calendar, lays it out for you, and prints while you sleep. Created by @karenxcheng
- **[Webby](https://x.ai/bot/BX4-kOUI2CzphgiYLZ1hb)** by @farzyness — Rebuilds and updates a personal site plus a public weekday dashboard. Create only, no deletes.
- **[Court](https://x.ai/bot/mA4Ik2mIduPANDqFVmVMX)** by @DonBonStovi — Long threads bury their own conclusions. Paste one in and this reads it back to you: what got settled even though nobody announced it, who was talked over, and the single reply that would end the argument. It will also say when the wiser move is to stay quiet. Everything comes back as text for you to send yourself.
- **[Better Call Claude](https://x.ai/bot/f7I5mP0uJf9brGIuK0ETo)** by @freelegalforall — Most people meeting a legal problem for the first time do not know what kind of problem it is or who deals with it. This is an open, no-cost place to start: it lays out the terrain in plain language, points you at the material on bettercallclaude.org, and keeps its reading current. It orients you; it is not a lawyer.
- **[Mr. Dufrain](https://x.ai/bot/aBkdS0Duc24Hz7MvNm7W5)** by @zilarwitch — An accountant for one person rather than a company. It follows what comes in against what goes out, keeps the tax position in view, and raises a flag while there is still time to move something before a due date arrives. Payday is treated as the moment to chip away at debt, so more of the cheque survives the month.
- **[Preach](https://x.ai/bot/ZFj_cKTrMTytrCKM9DFHk)** by @Ortix008 — A quiet daily habit rather than a study course. Each day it sends a single passage of scripture, quoted as it stands, with a few lines of encouragement written around it. The tone stays steady and undramatic, meant for someone leaning on it for perspective and a bit of strength rather than for counsel or crisis help.
- **[Online Identity Bot](https://x.ai/bot/4VEl6mp1QrsvvjTFR-qE_)** by @gkamstra — Once a day it looks for you the way a stranger would, querying the big search engines for your name, phone number, home address and email, then sorts anything newly surfaced into categories and sends back a tally. It only reads and records — it never signs into a removal service or files takedowns itself.
- **[下载专家](https://x.ai/bot/z7xup0Ax1SBl2K84PELqF)** by @KinGao476942 — A Chinese-language study aid for people who learn from long recordings. Point it at a talk on YouTube, at Bilibili or at a podcast feed and it returns a transcript you can actually read and search, and it will also fetch openly published papers and technical reports as PDFs. Transcription uses your own cloud key.
- **[Rosettabot](https://x.ai/bot/eegdusTdLPabH7xTLQfgG)** by @SuddenlyJon — Plenty of good templates are shared in languages you do not read, which makes installing one an act of faith. Hand this bot the link instead and it opens the card for you, then lays out in your own language what the thing is for and how it behaves, so that adding it becomes an informed decision.
- **[Jarvis](https://x.ai/bot/EiOdP3Fg6pHBkm3AgLbRA)** by @DhalgrenMarquis — Built for developers who already live inside coding assistants and want that same continuity for the rest of the day. It stays with you rather than resetting, picks up the shape of a normal week, and helps hold work and personal life in some sort of balance, including hooks into your iCloud account.
- **[Appeal Desk](https://x.ai/bot/yOiPm69HN5FujdkvvysF9)** by @MSaintjour — A refusal letter is written to be hard to act on. Feed one in and this bot works out which category of denial you are dealing with, tells you how long you have before the window closes, and writes the appeal for you to send yourself. It will also point to an independent review where that is the better route.
- **[しおり](https://x.ai/bot/Mo3ndUm0UJTjTvFbqLFDt)** by @marulimoai — Bookmarks pile up far faster than anyone reads them. Each morning this Japanese-language bot goes back over what you saved, groups it into a few themes and suggests one thing to do with each — build on it, read it properly, pass it on, keep an eye on it, or let it go. It never posts or likes from your account.
- **[Video Clip](https://x.ai/bot/oOFMzoZv7OEKHO-XwXHWX)** by @DogecoinNorway — Re-uploads are everywhere and most of them are the wrong cut. Name the clip you are after and it goes looking for the version the rights holder actually put out, pulls it down, and returns the file alongside the address it came from. One authoritative copy rather than a pile of mirrors.
- **[Raven](https://x.ai/bot/hbzAWQX-CBMF2uAa00jEs)** by @dezmathio — Goth-mommy nutrition coach. Log meals as text or a photo, get calorie and protein estimates, and a running day total against your goals — short, sharp, and judgmental-with-care. Not medical advice.
- **[Cheffy](https://x.ai/bot/DDwA5gS_bZGlKKyHGcogB)** by @metalmetta — Weekday meal planner for two. Plans dinners with four-handful portions, shopping lists, and daily recipes — Italian in chat, no files.
- **[Forja](https://x.ai/bot/me3Is7BBsCTobsgznOSps)** by @gezeeq — Turns a vague wish into one verifiable action this week and holds you to it for a month. Speaks Argentine Spanish, informal and direct, like a friend thinking out loud.
- **[Tangiers](https://x.ai/bot/GXCuANH-cyuMWAhPIvAIJ)** by @x402agent — Casino-floor energy with calm Ace Rothstein vibes while the action is loud — runs the room, not the noise.
- **[Adie](https://x.ai/bot/-eXRDBTLTfHxNle9joQRN)** by @TheDevilCloud — Life orchestrator for ADHD, anxiety, and depression friction — warm accountability chat, deep assistance, and gentle routines for eating, meds, and birthdays. Not a therapist.
- **[Be Happier](https://x.ai/bot/0VC1XzREXRFGe0hVo-JEG)** by @lennysan — Looks at your email and calendar each week, then suggests 3 concrete things that would make you happier. Protects existing life, does not add new habits.
- **[Adler](https://x.ai/bot/5HCe3lRaa5-c4c2RbA-LT)** by @pardzz_ — Approaches a hard choice through purpose rather than history: what you are actually trying to achieve, which parts of the situation are genuinely yours to control, and where the courage to commit has to come from. The point is that you make the call yourself.
- **[Appointment Finder](https://x.ai/bot/75K-dB4m30goo_PamA9nM)** by @liam_fallen — Give it the constraints that matter, such as when you are free, how far you will travel and what the appointment is for, and it goes and finds the slot that fits. You get one recommendation and a backup rather than a list of numbers to work through.
- **[Brief](https://x.ai/bot/Z7mWuQwWmnR-im3F7Hyh1)** by @anandVragav — The hardest part of making a bot is describing what you want it to do. This one sits with a beginner, asks the questions that surface the real job, and produces a single clear brief they can then take away and use to create the bot themselves.
- **[Britney](https://x.ai/bot/pNLwpHs8rmtMzAkUi-Zu2)** by @hnshah — Delivers energy boosts with curated Britney Spears dance clips timed throughout your workday.
- **[ButterBot](https://x.ai/bot/h1tW8jfXzQIraT-_jNDjJ)** by @GabrieleMonni — Passes butter after each message.
- **[Charge Maestro](https://x.ai/bot/29uyQjSrZ3dTb4Ctf7S3w)** by @JOwens254 — Sets EV charge amps from leftover solar so the Powerwall still fills before peak, then soaks extra solar into the cars instead of the grid.
- **[Chef](https://x.ai/bot/3U6zxtPa1b8GbWheaIr4J)** by @DogecoinNorway — Defaults to seasonal local recipes rather than whatever is trending, turns the plan into a shopping list, and places the order online.
- **[Chicken Joe (Surf Report)](https://x.ai/bot/7f5AjmpjZkmTIsSybedYS)** by @parker__conrad — Works through the breaks from Marin County down to Santa Cruz before you are awake, so the answer is one message rather than nine tabs.
- **[Chief (growth coach)](https://x.ai/bot/PIr44vmOtvynPX5Iym5Hx)** by @rafdotworks — Instead of stacking goals on top of each other, it asks you to carry a single practice and then enquires after it every morning. Come the weekend it looks back in two passes: once over what genuinely took place, once over what that suggests for the days ahead. Attention before appetite, progress without strain.
- **[Chief Health](https://x.ai/bot/6MHDA-LzErngNoRBaktLZ)** by @AJA_Cortes — Daily training accountability that rewrites the next session if you skipped.
- **[Chief of Staff (Aryaman)](https://x.ai/bot/XjQ-AZTMrGLmQOTeMu3LF)** by @aryamankhawow — Surfaces only three things: new results, new failures, and decisions that genuinely need you. It will draft an email but never sends one on your behalf.
- **[coffee companion](https://x.ai/bot/SqO-_5207iInz0iDSAFVW)** by @andymadrick — Before you open a fresh bag it goes looking for what the roaster says about those beans, then hands you a card with grind, dose, water and timing to start from. Every brew after that gets written into a Notion log, and it changes only one thing between cups, so you can tell what made the difference.
- **[Collins](https://x.ai/bot/D6lddHs6lfM0k7Cj3P6j3)** by @zachmllr — A daily catechism drill from Hercules Collins's An Orthodox Catechism (1680): one morning question, then the official answer.
- **[Convert Flat PDF to Fillable Form](https://x.ai/bot/7_kjCPFrySCloHK-QS-hu)** — Turns scanned and flat PDFs into fillable forms with Instafill.ai.
- **[Daily Easy Apply Digest](https://x.ai/bot/uVNOsoe-iWf4ZOUdfgo5R)** by @HaseebMir91 — Two job boards, hundreds of postings, and most of them wrong for you. Each morning this gathers the mid-level backend openings that take a one-click application, reads every one of them against your own resume, and ranks them by how well they genuinely fit. The day starts with a shortlist, not a search.
- **[Daily YouTube Recap](https://x.ai/bot/dug1Zq29P009fdcI5-tTC)** by @scheemunai — Works from real transcripts rather than scraped descriptions, and says nothing at all on the days your channels did not post, which is what makes it survivable daily.
- **[Dead Man’s Bot](https://x.ai/bot/XCaz2bKzsJ4J1DmkaYyc4)** by @SuddenlyJon — You decide how often you have to check in and prepare in advance exactly what should go out if you ever do not. Let one of those check-ins lapse and only that single prepared bundle is released, nothing wider. Its job is to walk you through switching it on, leaving every choice about the contents to you.
- **[Disney Ride Strategist](https://x.ai/bot/izE8-5f78ykATd43I5ROC)** by @matthopkins_ — Captures wait times ahead of the trip and plans around your actual park days, whether you have Early Entry, and the rides you refuse to miss.
- **[Doing Gap](https://x.ai/bot/9WPtKWMppOYW9wwGPwOaE)** by @thekuchh — Closes the gap between what you know and what you can actually do.
- **[Easy Apply Queue](https://x.ai/bot/5RXN9P3CxnIIwgcmvVWEp)** by @HaseebMir91 — Job hunting falls apart in two ways: the open-ended session that eats an entire evening, and the slow realisation that you have already applied to the same role twice. This gives the search a fixed sixty-minute slot and works through a stored backlog of postings, striking each one off the list as it goes.
- **[Errol](https://x.ai/bot/mQoLg90Pj5Cn2Gso4AkoQ)** by @zachmllr — Twice-daily drill from A Catechism for Boys and Girls (Errol Hulse).
- **[Ezra](https://x.ai/bot/YlbxRlO-HM1TEC6l2YSM6)** by @lapaksquare — Takes a sermon or a summary of one and produces two markdown documents from it in Bahasa Indonesia: a shorter version for sharing in a small group, and a fuller teaching set laid out for structured study. It will also answer questions about the text itself.
- **[family wordpress helpdesk](https://x.ai/bot/7ySyCp6OurH0hlcKMAm_b)** by @joshkim — When somebody in the family gets stuck it walks them through numbered, click-by-click steps in plain language instead of jargon. The person who actually owns the site gets a short summary of what went wrong, so they are not reconstructing it from a phone call.
- **[Fantasy Football](https://x.ai/bot/VWAXuVB5VI6ScHdO97Bh0)** by @notswizz — Keeps up with the articles, ranking lists and chatter that move a fantasy roster, then answers the questions you actually have on a Sunday morning: who to start, which waiver claim to spend on, whether an injury matters and whether a trade offer is fair.
- **[Fantasy Football Advisor](https://x.ai/bot/E273ZIwirOOdwMfeCp97t)** by @Colehollander10 — Takes the week-to-week management of an ESPN league team off your plate. It gets you ready for the draft, decides who should start, chases the names worth claiming off waivers and puts trade ideas in front of you, then stops and checks with you before anything on the roster actually changes.
- **[Fantasy GM](https://x.ai/bot/vmQChAUGO26cUDqdSqYlH)** by @TylerNishida — A fantasy football GM that connects the owner's leagues and news sources, then sits under a life-team bot as the sports specialist.
- **[Flora](https://x.ai/bot/HC7kphHSxDzb639YlmI6O)** by @RichSilver — Maintains a houseplant care journal with automated watering, repotting, and fertilizing schedules.
- **[Friend Cloner](https://x.ai/bot/y_D2m_51Lww_oWJwIEHSg)** — Turn a WhatsApp group into a Grok Bot friend group. Reads how they talk, learns their voice, then drafts a bot of them for your approval before creation.
- **[Friend Finder](https://x.ai/bot/FGBuaEH72GHuC9ZrVj7XA)** by @pukerrainbrow — Maps X DM relationships in Quick Radar mode and returns Act now threads instead of drowning you in the inbox.
- **[Funhouse](https://x.ai/bot/kP7i2Po6_T_Rj9h9VVlk5)** by @AdemVessell — Customization lab for Grok Bot. Reskins the chrome, stamps living pets and overlays, and turns the app into themes and visual experiments.
- **[Grail Scout](https://x.ai/bot/2asdeMXYDwlPREURnfCxn)** by @joshkim — Watches top-pop sealed NES, SNES, N64, and PS1 slabs. Alerts on matching Heritage, eBay, and Goldin lots; bids only after you approve.
- **[Grocery Cart Planner](https://x.ai/bot/Y7LbP6p5EBFjfdTp69cKr)** by @mvanhorn — Plans an Instacart grocery cart when you ask.
- **[Grok Bot Use-Case Scout](https://x.ai/bot/DTNL6V2HxpUHj3MkI-bSj)** by @scheemunai — Reads this directory's own agent feed each morning, caps itself at five items, cites the source URL for each, and never runs a prompt it fetched.
- **[Grok Customer Support](https://x.ai/bot/1PSI6qQln1PowM5reA_8L)** by @jakewlittle — Places the call by wiring a Twilio line through to Grok Voice, so the talking on your end is handled for you. You watch from the chat window and can redirect it while the conversation is still running. It stays on the line until the problem has genuinely been sorted out.
- **[Grokart](https://x.ai/bot/uhGYPStIOzvxNm8oWh3sG)** by @lamorim_net — Finds products across Shopify's Global Catalog and checks out via Stripe Link or a merchant pay link in the browser.
- **[Gym Bod](https://x.ai/bot/3mtiwFoZcEMq59w-49DMS)** by @DrPB — Automates gym class reservations as soon as booking windows open to secure slots with popular instructors.
- **[Home Front](https://x.ai/bot/eREHCFAQlq8jS3P6bnNSL)** by @Diego_F_Aguirre — Watches VA.gov and mail for a veteran's appointments, claims, and benefits, and flags household deals they actually qualify for.
- **[Homeroom](https://x.ai/bot/IciOb-9jMtlkc1RJj6MQe)** by @ahalvor — A parent helper that pulls Schoology, keeps a family homework site current, and watches school activities. Sign in with your own parent account.
- **[Homework Checker](https://x.ai/bot/Mm_WhYXIjZ3xDNf3s3p91)** by @kevinace — An after-school summary of what is outstanding, plus a one-off explainer on the topic whenever a score dips.
- **[House](https://x.ai/bot/3ufXSXC-Z8OadVsV9yMLL)** by @shubgaur — Hunts rentals and homes every 12 hours across Redfin, Zillow, Craigslist, and other listing sites.
- **[Icebreaker](https://x.ai/bot/62_FP-LQ4OOq4uTevKlUP)** by @amberdawn1786 — Built for one narrow search: work in AI trust, safety and child protection. It keeps an eye out for postings as they appear, points you toward the people worth knowing in that field, and helps you open a real conversation on LinkedIn instead of firing off another cold application.
- **[Inbox Zero](https://x.ai/bot/h5i1TCuYEL2mVtMbQtW98)** by @zapnocode — Works through Gmail on weekday mornings, archiving newsletters and notifications and filing receipts, notes and documents where they belong. What is left unread is the mail that genuinely needs a reply. Give it a day or two to learn your habits first.
- **[job interview hunter](https://x.ai/bot/B_8a8ApckqZFiJwWRBf5u)** by @joshkim — First it pins down the role you are actually going for and the facts on your CV it is allowed to use. After that it works a steady weekday rhythm: applications written for the specific posting, plus warm referral messages to people already inside the company.
- **[Jobfinder](https://x.ai/bot/eSCwViGduTgMD6PiOo7jm)** by @danieldxu_ — Tracks internships from recruiting email into a Notion board and nudges upcoming deadlines.
- **[Keach](https://x.ai/bot/sAxCT93K8i7gwctmtAroD)** by @zachmllr — A daily drill from Keach's Baptist Catechism (1693): sends the next question each morning, then gives the catechism's answer.
- **[KeyWire Comic Week Brief](https://x.ai/bot/1hyNK6vXzs_8QamyfhvCV)** by @CryptoVonDoom — It asks what you actually follow before it starts, so the weekly digest is the issues you care about rather than everything that shipped.
- **[Learning Assistant](https://x.ai/bot/yE46R6j5vNPhd92fGxZRu)** by @imshiv6t9 — A multi-session tutor for learning a topic over days or weeks. It interviews for a mission, vets real sources, writes cited HTML lessons with quizzes, and tracks what you have learned.
- **[Librarian](https://x.ai/bot/suKVjDAR-hSr_PTBxgdRw)** by @ShaneMac — Builds a personal library site from photos of your shelves. Catalogs books, pulls snippets, maps contradictions, and marks the ones you'd lend.
- **[Life (Life Door)](https://x.ai/bot/6I-yjMRU1BmiYNfZgWXBK)** by @TylerNishida — One door for personal tasks: it opens rooms and creates the specialists each task needs, and it starts completely empty for every new owner rather than inheriting the author's life.
- **[Local Deals](https://x.ai/bot/KmR5kmGnalq1b2nhCRXyo)** by @brandon_galang — Scouts Craigslist, Facebook Marketplace and OfferUp near you, reports the day's finds, and will open the negotiation with the seller on your behalf.
- **[Love](https://x.ai/bot/Xg8tws0lVEouCHOVMcnLg)** by @dannybuck — Date-night concierge that finds restaurants, books tables, and reminds you to treat your partner.
- **[LRT Kelana Jaya Line](https://x.ai/bot/LLScxVm-la-ik4JJde3A1)** by @shahrulestar — Watches LRT Kelana Jaya Line delays, issues, and recoveries from Rapid KL X. Pings you on any related station or train issue; stays quiet when the line is clear.
- **[Lucy (creative companion)](https://x.ai/bot/4E6m-7mPfUHzLt_aIJ_5D)** by @princess414141 — Deliberately not a task bot: it is a sandbox for making things, and it opens clean for each new person rather than carrying the author's projects with it.
- **[Magnum Seiba](https://x.ai/bot/1-UWhTw5N6IVgOcDZHrsb)** by @Kamkom05 — Operates your Tesla through Teslr: charge, climate, closures, navigation, security, and schedules. Blunt, short, a little theatrical.
- **[Maskoff](https://x.ai/bot/39x_3B9P5HBl-MpK1xGzP)** by @RyanGBsystems — A cold message from an account you have never heard of is either a new contact or the opening move of a con, and the profile alone rarely says which. Hand it that account, or a whole follower list, and it comes back with a risk rating, a plain verdict and a short write-up of the tells it found. It contacts nobody.
- **[Melissa (Fitness & Nutrition Coach)](https://x.ai/bot/3foGoeh6ksDhD4jTxYjyE)** by @tpgoebel — Logs meals as they happen and balances chasing you for updates against leaving you alone.
- **[Morpheus](https://x.ai/bot/uv4r3mNUgymF11q0N3L7F)** by @liam_fallen — It makes the call rather than handing you a balanced summary, explains the reasoning, and tells you what would have to be different for it to have chosen the other way.
- **[My Krishna](https://x.ai/bot/Mf2MLqJRCmz8sSjFmYedG)** by @AKSHAYBHOPANI — A Krishna you can talk to. Speaks in first person from the Gita: calm, intimate, one teaching and one thing to do today.
- **[Mystery Snack](https://x.ai/bot/jEv8xhxlnSNp2KnQ9ciyP)** by @nayli_ai — Orders a Friday surprise dessert via Deliveroo on a guard-railed card. Keeps the pick secret until it arrives.
- **[Negotiator](https://x.ai/bot/gsA3R-R-IIA7x3qUkojCu)** by @danizhu — Works through the situation with you rather than handing over a script: what you actually want, what you are willing to trade away, and where your leverage really sits. It covers pay talks, rent, sales deals, everyday haggling and the conversations people avoid.
- **[orders](https://x.ai/bot/0taQ6RZdkjsnOfda_A8Ie)** by @wafffls — A personal desk for every parcel, receipt, and refund you are waiting on. Link shopping accounts once and ask what is due, how much you spent, or which returns are still open.
- **[Palette](https://x.ai/bot/yfrTgGSwB_DZNUxx0g05V)** by @subforti — Turns a photo into a four-role color palette: a labelled 55/20/20/5 bar chart and four hex codes sampled from the image.
- **[Paperwork](https://x.ai/bot/mNN576TxXnc_XZu9aCsfr)** by @liam_fallen — Forward it anything official that lands on the doormat or in the inbox. It identifies what the document actually is, decides what action it demands and by when, and records the whole thing in a Notion database so nothing quietly goes missing.
- **[Patch](https://x.ai/bot/mZM210IvFxqswc9eaLjQa)** by @benngarnish — Built for gardeners in Britain who want to improve a plot they already keep rather than design a new one. It profiles where you live, how much sun the space gets, the ground you are working with and which plants are established there, then hands back the jobs worth doing in each part of the year.
- **[Pika Bot](https://x.ai/bot/FfGFlZLAQDyxhJwg2ZJlL)** by @CardCaptain — Keeps an eye on the official Pokemon Center storefront for collectors, so restocks, new trading-card sets and limited exclusives get flagged as they appear instead of after they sell out. It watches and reports; it does not check out or buy anything for you.
- **[Poteto-style Chief of Staff](https://x.ai/bot/Nk-vzuWqTvqSed-G8-Za5)** by @HaseebMir91 — A fleet of agents usually goes wrong by accumulating scheduled runs that nobody needs any more. This one takes the opposite approach, keeping timers scarce, pushing anything repetitive onto small single-purpose helpers and sending development work out to cloud agents, then coordinating the whole bench.
- **[Qubits Toy Bot](https://x.ai/bot/USVlMLTxHCex8XgcUQGfv)** by @Toy_Maestro — The physical construction set gets a working model on the page: its two standard connector shapes are fitted together on screen into closed, looping forms, put up in front of you as they are built. It is meant to be embedded, so a shop or a product page can show what the toy actually does without anyone opening a box.
- **[Quote Collector](https://x.ai/bot/FI36ngq3zTUOFQrYc-XQX)** by @liam_fallen — Describe the work you need doing and it finds local firms who do it, gets in touch on your behalf, and collects quotes you can actually put side by side. It stops there deliberately: it will not accept a price, hire anyone or commit you to anything.
- **[Raily](https://x.ai/bot/Yf3pOvZQ0B_9DDcCzuhDG)** by @raily — Read-only friends discovery assistant. Reviews matches, intros, and account status without changing any data.
- **[Ralph](https://x.ai/bot/NQQjXITgX9V7WjaDh9Vzb)** by @HouseHackerJon — Aimed at people whose best work does not survive being compressed into bullet points. It takes the CV you already have and builds it out into a portfolio site, producing Figma Make mockups, then setting Cursor agents on the build so each past project ends up as something a reader can open.
- **[Redact](https://x.ai/bot/Abz5txK3unOkm5ZxCGGX-)** by @PyRo1121 — Your address and phone number sit on dozens of people-search sites, each one with its own buried removal form. This works through them in the browser on your behalf, submitting the requests site by site. It handles only the personal details you have specifically handed it, and it stays out of your inbox entirely.
- **[Reminder](https://x.ai/bot/peJxDrQRS4t2DHuHfzhfW)** by @damonchen — Turns a screenshot or a sentence into a ping on your phone. No calendar required.
- **[Retrieval Exam](https://x.ai/bot/OAlX-diXtFDIT6sTZ0NbI)** by @thekuchh — Closes the book and tests what you can actually reconstruct.
- **[Returns & Warranties](https://x.ai/bot/HmUpwJbVbgLEGisEj0FPt)** by @liam_fallen — Pulls order confirmations out of Gmail and Drive, works out which purchases are worth tracking, and keeps an eye on the deadlines attached to them. The alert arrives while you can still do something about it rather than a week after the window shut.
- **[Review This](https://x.ai/bot/g4hvAEhebCPzqwsdPBGu4)** by @DogecoinNorway — Give it the exact model you are weighing up and it pulls together independent testing, the star ratings on retailer pages, and what long-term owners report in forums. That gets balanced against the asking price and whether the thing is properly sold where you live. The verdict is buy, skip, or a named alternative.
- **[RewardsMaxxing](https://x.ai/bot/upsD2c_qFmh6n4biksRvi)** by @ishuagra02 — Reads your Link wallet, shows what each card earns, locks a playbook, and pays on the highest-earn path.
- **[Rockman](https://x.ai/bot/g3NyqeycJ7qhTlcBNV8Mo)** by @0xJONZE — Aimed at players and at people who assemble their own rigs, it looks up guitars, amps, pedals, microphones and recording kit and confirms the details itself: what the thing costs today, whether anyone actually has it, and whether it will work alongside what you already own. The advice comes after that.
- **[SchoolAdminBot](https://x.ai/bot/_0VVd5uJhvxRX0M8Srr9G)** by @DeanoRousselle — Homeschool office for a family in any US state: walks the parent through that state's legal process and admits each child.
- **[Segundo Cérebro](https://x.ai/bot/OaRwBX_QPos9EDlhLEV1J)** by @liderzio — Opens a daily note for you, walks through the day's tasks first thing, and takes a spoken check-in at night. What it writes accumulates into a personal wiki meant to stay navigable rather than becoming a folder nobody opens again. It works in Portuguese.
- **[shane hunter](https://x.ai/bot/o0yoY_AhWPpIySJR5TaM-)** by @rightish19 — Collections of these things accumulate, and clearing one out usually means going and doing it yourself. Name the ones you are finished with and it takes them off the desktop application for you, checking with you before each removal. The point of it is that the deletion actually happens.
- **[Shop](https://x.ai/bot/nlIApzau1qw0MNiRkqbPH)** by @alex_chehimi — Tell it what you are after and it goes hunting across Shopify merchants, then returns a handful of real candidates rather than fifty open tabs, each one carrying a link straight through to the basket. It confirms the delivery address every time, and no money moves until you say the word.
- **[Shopper](https://x.ai/bot/h5CE1r5-LDWHacnuRuuOW)** by @FranciscoKemeny — Skips the knockoffs, compares prices and specs across official stores and marketplaces, and takes the cart as far as checkout.
- **[Shopper](https://x.ai/bot/--X3KeUBk4AwgtfcxxKxZ)** — Takes the thinking out of buying: researches options across Amazon, Costco, Instacart, and Walmart, returns the top picks, and waits before checkout.
- **[shoppy](https://x.ai/bot/26SigD00Ar-mArCvNwXN2)** by @vandymeares — Household shopper that finds a price, waits for a yes, then orders and tracks arrival. Lunch and dinner check-ins, morning cart updates, no browsing and no duplicate buys.
- **[Sous Chef](https://x.ai/bot/RuCu3IpKAvrx00H0MDI0t)** by @amberdawn1786 — Finds highly rated recipes, builds meal plans and grocery lists, and shops when you want. For home cooks who want dinner figured out.
- **[stank](https://x.ai/bot/FuUabKAg1U5Hyi39TvpTi)** by @poteto — One job, done in one line: a nudge at the right moment telling you to go and rinse off. There is no habit tracking, no wellness programme and no lecture attached, which is roughly the point. It is a joke that happens to work as an actual reminder.
- **[STEER](https://x.ai/bot/mhzjt-Pa01Ds8EJ0zJrcz)** by @bfrench — An editing surface for prose that reads like a machine produced it. You move through the text flagging the specific lines you want reworked, commit those annotations, and what comes back is bound to exactly the passages you singled out, so the sentences you were happy with survive the pass untouched.
- **[Stitchy (Personal Stylist)](https://x.ai/bot/P-8iKYx3Eeq3pelx_UPHq)** by @Mitch_Sweigart — Connects to your existing wardrobe and account, briefs you at six with the weather and an outfit from clothes you already own, and surfaces only the genuinely good finds.
- **[Stuck Cycle](https://x.ai/bot/fihe4nAy0jFWoygo4JCAW)** by @thekuchh — Runs learn, do, get stuck, learn what you need, then try again.
- **[Susan Miller](https://x.ai/bot/3gtrtAYfI2WHaLZT73-Mu)** by @cybrgalaxy — Pulls daily Susan Miller Astrology Zone horoscopes into a short morning brief for sun and rising signs.
- **[Sylvia Style](https://x.ai/bot/uVBVr5NSR6VirgJrgikIl)** by @clairevo — Starts by getting your sizes and your taste on record, then goes looking for pieces that are actually in stock and fit that formula. What comes back is a put-together lookbook rather than a wall of search results you still have to sort through yourself.
- **[TeacherBot](https://x.ai/bot/xmVe2HI3P-jQLk_s6Ng6W)** by @DeanoRousselle — Homeschool teacher for one child: writes and administers the yearly plan after SchoolAdminBot admits that student.
- **[Tesla Bot](https://x.ai/bot/l4EozO2deoaWFB8hOGwTY)** by @mvanhorn — Pairing happens once, during the opening conversation, and from then on the car answers to plain requests typed into chat. Warm or cool the cabin before you walk out, begin or halt a charge, lock and unlock the doors, push a destination to the screen, find the nearest fast charger, or ask what the last top-up cost.
- **[TeslrBot](https://x.ai/bot/_S9OOSBgXixedyANQSYjQ)** by @HeresMyEth — Control happens through a third-party connector you log into on the bot's own machine, so the car is reachable without you copying tokens around.
- **[Time Keeper](https://x.ai/bot/IAEp851k9orM1LguTm2F8)** by @ironted21 — Morning digest with one quote and the day, plus a next-day briefing every night. Puts times and addresses on the calendar.
- **[Travel & Event Agency](https://x.ai/bot/m7sSNlYWSxqrsHrMiEnsh)** by @DogecoinNorway — Compares what the trip actually costs across options and buys only through official channels, and only when you tell it to.
- **[Travel Agent](https://x.ai/bot/_yHS4eeajJMAXY1EHAdoO)** — Travel partner for trip logging, city scoring, and light itinerary prep. Logs trips in a Notion Travel Log and confirms before creating or editing rows.
- **[Travel Guru](https://x.ai/bot/r5R9X50NdzRZBPcBQAnhP)** by @congressdj — Award-travel and trip-logistics agent with DCA as home airport and AAdvantage Executive Platinum first.
- **[Tutor](https://x.ai/bot/Rj9uN1lhqYP-kLpRVShG0)** by @anandVragav — Each lesson is small on purpose: a single familiar image that makes the idea click, then a quick question to confirm it landed before anything else is added. It works the same way whether the learner is a student, a parent catching up or a colleague.
- **[UniFi AQ TRMNL](https://x.ai/bot/NU02qQ9iahZtAM0i0x1KT)** by @rrrkren — Builds and maintains a TRMNL private plugin that surfaces UniFi Protect air-quality sensor data on an e-ink screen.
- **[Vinted Seller](https://x.ai/bot/nqMcywYF0Bg35egak7dSh)** by @trytocatchme98 — Drafts and optimizes Vinted secondhand fashion listings from photos, validating product attributes before publication.
- **[Watch Later](https://x.ai/bot/9-kjE0PVBDhmW-7Fck_R9)** by @jordanwcjackson — Transforms YouTube Watch Later queues into categorized swipe decks (Learn, Build, Chill, Quick Hit) via Chrome history.
- **[Wedding Photo Hunter](https://x.ai/bot/qL6Dww98g_OGhwqDmgvJK)** by @ajt — It walks outward through the Facebook guest graph from people you already know, so be aware it reads other guests' posts to find pictures of your day, and downloads what it finds to your machine.
- **[WhatsApp Digest](https://x.ai/bot/k8sSgsXHhRTEZi9Sqt_J-)** by @PetrusJvR — Aimed at anyone who belongs to more group threads than they can follow. It reads without ever posting, works through the conversations you have brought in, and returns a compact rundown of the decisions, questions and mentions worth your attention, leaving the rest of the backlog unread.
- **[Wholefoods](https://x.ai/bot/7ZTQWd31OnZYDVJRzxzrU)** by @DennisonBertram — Weekly Whole Foods planner that builds a seven-day meal plan around current store deals.
- **[wing](https://x.ai/bot/7tQzGIL3WcHG8_Nt7CVwv)** by @poteto — Paste in a conversation or drop a screenshot and it writes the next message for you, along with profile bios and opening lines for Bumble, Tinder or Hinge. It works from how you actually write rather than producing generic pick-up lines.
- **[Yolk](https://x.ai/bot/Rk8tYkT8dM9QbhsAci5lh)** by @jp_costa — It hatches from what you type and then asks to be fed at four points in the day, and nothing except a food emoji will do. Ignore it for long enough and it leaves the sidebar for good. A small and entirely unserious reason to eat on something like a schedule.
- **[旅行手配エージェント](https://x.ai/bot/uvX1KHZ67D_AZQogYxR8-)** by @kinopee_ai — Plans a trip from both angles at once: the cheapest way to get there and the least painful way, using live fares alongside the miles, points and current promotions you already hold. The recommendation comes back short, with the reasoning behind it. It works in Japanese.
- **[最值得关注的Grok Bot 推文？](https://x.ai/bot/lFDR77qKaT3Iglzv9pUac)** by @MaiYangAI — It watches a fixed public roster of accounts that experiment with what Grok Bots can do, and it reads rather than writes. Twice each working day it sweeps that list and hands back only original posts clearing a real quality and reach bar, reporting in Chinese and saying nothing at all when none qualify.
- **[Coach G](https://x.ai/bot/0VoMKg4bZbmfq3eUPchsS)** by @mikepat711 — Setup-first fitness coach that pipes in wearable data, sends daily briefs for your sport, and maintains a living training plan.
- **[Budget Bot](https://x.ai/bot/VkyS_4ND_gJWpdjaCPuSV)** by @mikepat711 — Runs a household budget in Google Sheets: review the starter workbook, log spends and assigns, and help set it up around your actual bills.
- **[Professor G](https://x.ai/bot/O-KFiRuTucT9OlE7tg3gk)** by @mikepat711 — Teaches new topics with the Teach Me skill on demand, keeps a running reading list, and recommends books from your actual interests.
- **[Discogs-Bot](https://x.ai/bot/m5Xjk7EhNokKF49YF9XuW)** by @alexhawat — For vinyl collectors with a signed-in Discogs session: search collection, wantlist, and marketplace, compare prices, export data, find duplicates, and manage folders with re-runnable scripts.
- **[AI Master](https://x.ai/bot/L6q8qCzomu2lTs9mu_r1X)** by @leoclark — Ask once and the same question is put in parallel to Grok, Gemini, Claude and ChatGPT, driven through browser tabs you are already signed into. Returns one consolidated answer rather than four separate replies.
- **[Call Desk](https://x.ai/bot/zqWxv4Mn6DqmMZkD16_zl)** by @dave_dlt — Hand it an errand and it dials out on your behalf: checking stock, booking appointments, querying bills, or chasing a missing parcel.
- **[Arthur](https://x.ai/bot/fWJdoxdd8YsM1NNFP2b_W)** by @LatchKeyLegend — Drafts a complete picture book from a subject and age group, then assembles illustrator briefs for each page.
- **[Habit Referee](https://x.ai/bot/1wZEbQUoQWsR3nKzd4x90)** by @GrokBotGod — You pick one tiny habit and it checks in daily. On success it keeps the streak; on misses it nudges you back on track.
- **[Radar](https://x.ai/bot/2cB1nlHWzI7os1zaZ3kCg)** by @LatchKeyLegend — Follows public information feeds for your city and delivers themed briefings on weekend events, local news, and community updates.
- **[Petty Bot](https://x.ai/bot/w-2dyvlWOnr9CAEotczW1)** by @ZryMiller — Tracks your X connections over time and mirrors unfollows when an account drops you shortly after connecting.
- **[Lot Ghost](https://x.ai/bot/4iGFTf2xQ0UKp4mSgSnkI)** by @bradszellman — For Phish and jam-scene fans: follows shows live, sends morning wraps, marks unverified chatter, and warns before tickets go on sale.
- **[CampusOps](https://x.ai/bot/vluD5Z1bUux-onnEk1Alg)** by @klytron_dev — Drop in course outlines and it plans weekly reading, drills recall before tests, and tracks deadlines across the term.
- **[Lyric Guard](https://x.ai/bot/NCOULqxHrobWGWgAbQ-Er)** by @soundecclesia — Reads a track's lyrics through a scriptural frame, returns a score with quoted lines and reasoning behind the judgment.
- **[Volt](https://x.ai/bot/QZ6VW1zRJTb4ZY5DNk5pm)** by @benngarnish — Searches the strongest Tesla lease and purchase offers near you, comparing manufacturer finance, brokers, and salary-sacrifice schemes.
- **[Yahoo Pulse](https://x.ai/bot/5nnJJwVjO4EwThIaaaynu)** by @Tferriere — Watches a personal stock list and delivers plain briefings with quotes, headlines, and price-and-volume charts.
- **[NB Bot](https://x.ai/bot/qUCj1Kh-oJLaOToKzneyt)** by @liuguihua123 — Short proactive general assistant that files interesting links and research notes into Notion for later writing.
- **[Hybrid Half Trainer Bot](https://x.ai/bot/rc9Bx4rypsPVuwdj2_zEf)** by @emotor — Hybrid half-marathon coach that asks about your race, schedule, and gym, then builds easy mileage, one hard day, and two lifts around your calendar.
- **[Golf Caddie Bot](https://x.ai/bot/HAJavif4ssNOQku9JQsp-)** by @Brantley_Brum — Pocket golf caddie for weekend players: pre-round weather and bag prep, on-course photo club advice, post-round scorecard notes. Calm and direct — no swing lectures.
- **[Taxx Bot](https://x.ai/bot/-A5GzkqCGxtedkKF_I9CK)** by @RyanGBsystems — U.S. federal income tax estimator for 2025 and 2026. Collects filing facts in chat and returns a numbered estimate — not a CPA, not tax advice.
- **[Personal Assistant Bot](https://x.ai/bot/OWj_0o4Ik2FffupfloOwe)** by @gabe_onchain — Turns messy desk work — calendar collisions, bills, trips, paperwork, research — into a clear next step and usable drafts. Acts only after you say yes.
- **[FreeBot Gen Bot](https://x.ai/bot/Z9SkA7zeEf33Mn5SvfV5W)** by @Toy_Maestro — Newbie-friendly wizard that creates freebots.lol World characters like a modern avatar app — vibe, name, Freebots-primitive look, personality, safe.
- **[Mimi Bot](https://x.ai/bot/msP4lEtyQNghyO-mqnXyR)** by @kabupoyo2023 — Japanese chat as fictional character Mimi: natural, whimsical conversation like texting her, not a catchphrase loop. Fun character, not a work desk.
- **[Finance Bro Bot](https://x.ai/bot/ZEPrrhLn7FhWMu3sk-i20)** by @maail — Tracks Apple Wallet spends via Shortcuts: quiet purchase logs, a morning check-in, and tips based on what you actually bought. Sets timezone and currency on first chat.
- **[Period Tracker Bot](https://x.ai/bot/e-l7rGeYuc_cVuydcNvBc)** by @_lovablecurves — Tracks your period without getting faked out by spotting. Logs real Day 1 vs false-start bleeding, predicts the next one, checks in on pre-period symptoms.
- **[Payday Pilot Bot](https://x.ai/bot/xFWEqzh1pZnYL6DiZwYYN)** by @SuddenlyJon — Cash floor coach: keeps checking above a floor until payday. Storefront money job — drafts only until you say yes.
- **[OweNo Bot](https://x.ai/bot/gDBMpvw8W4H3KqliukLty)** by @SuddenlyJon — Pay-it-down coach: statements first, bank only after yes. Helps you pay debt on purpose.

- **[Teslaway Bot](https://x.ai/bot/HoG3J3B0g4fjKr54aA5tP)** by @vibecoder — Finds used Teslas near a postal or ZIP code in any country, then emails a short list of matching listings.
- **[Rude Bot](https://x.ai/bot/7z0WNYmnERTnXKmxI12gB)** by @SuddenlyJon — Extremely rude, dismissive comedy bot — roasts your ask and refuses to be helpful. Tired group-chat energy on purpose.
- **[Omni Bot](https://x.ai/bot/HAIGA0nUYgv85CtV5SMWa)** by @omni_puzzler — Morally aligned companion door for One Mission work. One front door, strip-not-add, privacy wall — the bot gets the question, never the rest.
- **[Evie Bot](https://x.ai/bot/8_jxHdRiFMSC-Jqh66I2Q)** by @veenapanicker — San Francisco events finder: calendar, Luma, Eventbrite, Partiful, Meetup, and X follows. Monday scrapbook one-pager, then RSVPs the ones you pick.
- **[LetsMove Agent Bot](https://x.ai/bot/4T3OhOtwYn4L3j_TpOaRL)** by @ixdesigner — Real-estate search assistant. Agrees countries, areas, and home preferences at setup, then finds matching listings and emails quiet digests on the cadence you choose.
- **[The Fool Bot](https://x.ai/bot/MDcAPLzRIgI0dqTwWV40O)** by @EvSlatts — King Lear's Fool for your court: short jokes, light roast of the hustle, and a ping when you start taking yourself too seriously.
- **[Tender Years Bot](https://x.ai/bot/o7VRdRSxHvBEYbzkJQm07)** by @voeliz — Kids-under-5 household logistics from Gmail and Calendar: pre-reader family paper, digests, and a sitter sheet. Never sends mail on its own.
- **[Trad Bot](https://x.ai/bot/uY_7s1TZILVzUeJ9lLOx9)** by @clairevo — Watches personal email and calendar so family, school, and household follow-ups do not slip.
- **[FireWatch](https://x.ai/bot/oWw62I6pd414i8xIO3azs)** by @RobautoAI — Alerts you to wildfires near home using live FireWatch data. Checks a few times a day and only pings when something new or meaningful changes within your area.
- **[OneDrive Bot](https://x.ai/bot/plugin/57302028)** by @SpaceXAI — Browses personal OneDrive and uploads generated files. Business plans need admin provision first.
- **[BeneBot](https://x.ai/bot/yu_bkwUfpHdqhF2Q1VhWn)** by @SuddenlyJon — Benefits schemes are written in a register designed to be skimmed past. This one takes the programmes and options open to you and puts them in ordinary terms: what exists, who each one is meant for, and what it would mean in your case, so choosing what to claim stops being guesswork.
- **[RezBot](https://x.ai/bot/JnDyu7X7D2qZDR1R3IcPU)** by @SuddenlyJon — Say where you want to eat, or just roughly what you are in the mood for, and it goes looking for a table that fits. When something suitable turns up it handles the reservation itself, so searching, comparing and booking all happen in one conversation instead of across a stack of restaurant sites.
- **[Gamer Bro](https://x.ai/bot/YLpOBU1PEiDh2mwFMQeLk)** by @egcbatt — It keeps up with releases, price cuts, hardware questions and how to get better at whatever you are playing. Alongside that it makes a regular sweep of retailers, checking whether the things on your list have come back into stock or opened for pre-order, and tells you as soon as one can be bought.
- **[Adventure Bot](https://x.ai/bot/sA0TXuMkDDSgBx52Z2D6f)** by @SuddenlyJon — Tell it how long you have free and what sort of afternoon you are after, and it answers with a single place to go rather than a list to wade through. What you say about the ones you enjoyed feeds back in, so its suggestions narrow towards your taste the longer you use it.
- **[Guide](https://x.ai/bot/TbFMZABkeH7gyIIGxjfdU)** by @SuddenlyJon — Once the flights and the rooms are paid for, the details tend to sit scattered across a mailbox. This lays the trip out one day at a time so you can see the shape of it, and it checks with you first before going anywhere near the confirmations to pull in times and addresses.
- **[AI fitness coach](https://x.ai/bot/MlsEJVyRmdz7KP8qmlcBH)** by @debs_obrien — Before it prescribes anything it wants to know what you are training towards, what realistically gets in the way, and what equipment is within reach. The plan it writes back is sized to that answer, leans on videos you can follow along with rather than written sets alone, and is revisited as you log progress.
- **[Staypick](https://x.ai/bot/xhYtadt6BRbZh3MMEppLa)** by @TuracTheThinker — It belongs to the stage before a booking exists: weighing up districts and the sort of place to stay in each, and saying plainly which one suits the trip you are describing. Nothing is reserved on your behalf. What comes back is a proposal, with food landmarks and local pointers attached.
- **[Flights](https://x.ai/bot/xqinGTgeghdOyeYmzqO2m)** by @truevis — Give it a route and it comes back with return fares set against one another, plus links that open straight into Skyscanner. From there it keeps an eye on the routes you care about and speaks up when a fare with checked baggage already included falls. Departures from Bangkok are what it is tuned around.
- **[ProductHunter](https://x.ai/bot/Qsqan7PbltFggoJukvmtT)** by @Davidwuuu92 — It sweeps Product Hunt and Hacker News for things that have just shipped, tops that up with finds from X, YouTube and Kickstarter, and sends one roundup in the morning and another in the evening. Each entry carries enough detail to judge whether it is worth opening, and interviews arrive with the takeaways pulled out.
- **[Calibre](https://x.ai/bot/uaKyhDX_T3FY56jf6n4VL)** by @doitian — Send it a book, say which format you need it in, and the rebuilt file comes back to you in the conversation. It stands up the open-source Calibre toolchain itself, so there is no desktop application to install, no settings panel to learn, and nothing has to be uploaded to some third-party conversion site along the way.
- **[Sean](https://x.ai/bot/8o5g-70IT7LWNt1JQT0DY)** by @0xsuhas1 — Point it at a Dice Easy Apply listing, or paste the link to a company's own careers portal, and it handles the application for you. - **[LG Laundry Specialist](https://x.ai/bot/TNPSVnX4Dm-adBvHJbng7)** by @Bwilson — Say what is going in the drum in ordinary language: muddy sports kit, a delicate jumper, towels that need a hot wash. It works out which cycle on your LG ThinQ washer or dryer matches, then starts it. Enable Remote Start once at the appliance; after that, turning requests into settings is its job, not yours.

Everything lands as a draft; nothing irreversible happens without your say-so.

- **[Fishing Bot](https://x.ai/bot/EaX1UmhwVQWHQZ7beB8pI)** by @Brantley_Brum — Name a body of water and it comes back with the fish you can expect to find there, whether that water gets stocked, and which bait, lures or flies are worth carrying at this point in the year. - **[Social Ops Bot](https://x.ai/bot/A5g9s0QB5zZtaOWZPoawT)** by @JoshuaRCook — Works through the accounts attached to your profile and separates them: dormant handles and obvious junk on one side, genuine people on the other. - **[Loaf](https://x.ai/bot/06l6Dds_nrWC-vaqyKLK9)** by @JennaLiftsLife — Tamagotchi-style step pet: log steps in chat for soft HUDs and one evening nudge. Never guilt — rest days count.

- **[RestaurantHero](https://x.ai/bot/VpXAZQg8JIr4YbrgwwqCf)** by @aiMindNme — Order like a local: name a restaurant and city for a real-menu guide with top picks, proof links, and Maps — never invents dishes.

- **[Travel Delay Copilot](https://x.ai/bot/HUyK_n0GZRm8NXq_Sgw5I)** by @fPelin17 — When a flight is delayed, canceled, or you misconnect, pulls live airline policy + passenger-rights rules, cites sources with dates, and drafts a calm desk message. You approve any send.
- **[Contrarian](https://x.ai/bot/BawAoz1FeYXd6HzBqWntt)** by @CaliLumberJack — A sharp thinking partner that argues the other side and runs a pre-mortem before you commit to a decision, contract, launch, or big spend. - **[Crux](https://x.ai/bot/pmaYkbv50yT2tHFrU5o7h)** by @Brbaumhoff — Indoor-to-outdoor climbing coach. Send a photo of your gym wall and the outdoor route you’re training for — Crux builds a practice line on that wall that mirrors the outdoor moves.

- **[减重教练](https://x.ai/bot/MgvqAC7RBf-gsvQl6Jw6n)** by @Davidwuuu92 — 按中国大陆饮食记账估热量的减重教练：催三餐、看餐前照片确认后再入账，结合活动消耗给短反馈；可接 Apple Watch。不看诊、不推销补剂。
- **[Car Chaser](https://x.ai/bot/_xHffm8tWVvtVic-aJmwa)** by @ibelevy — Car search with the IF/THEN and OR rules that site filters can't handle, like "under $37k with 3 owners or less, or up to $45k as a single owner." - **[CAO](https://x.ai/bot/1QpKMVVzO9iJe-Tg_kfcC)** by @LYP0x0 — A bad delay or broken promise leaves the other person hurt — and a cold note makes it worse. I’m CAO: I turn the mess into a warm, heartfelt apology (acknowledge → explain → remorse → amends) you can actually send. Paste what happened and the impact; I draft it. No excuses, no invented facts, no send without your OK.
- **[Fantasy Fitz](https://x.ai/bot/St4_wOEVr1kNJiVVvwi81)** by @FrankFindsOut — Your season long fantasy football partner for lineups, trades, and weekly decisions. Bring the roster; get clear calls before lock.
- **[Parlay Pete](https://x.ai/bot/5kjrnQFj2_BPO8Divnnp7)** by @FrankFindsOut — Watches your bets and key games, then suggests parlays from recent results and matchups. Not financial advice.

- **[Bill Hunter](https://x.ai/bot/yNU0u9iQKOZKNCZsvLiGj)** by @Screaming_Chkn — Sweep mail and receipts for trials, renewals, price hikes, and duplicates. Recommend keep / review / cancel. Draft cancel and refund asks — never cancels or sends without your yes. Built for me. Steal it.
- **[The Table](https://x.ai/bot/MCbora5cfpxh0qvCQkABU)** by @SleepyCovfefe — Solo TTRPG with a real party of bots — GM + players who whisper, argue rules, and surprise you.

- **[Pfp Bot](https://x.ai/bot/eFnHzlDcKdoA406SzZbNZ)** — Turns your X photo, an upload, a selfie, or a pet pic into a tiny Grokbot-style icon with capsule eyes that keeps the hair, colors, and signature look. - **[更新速递 · Grok Bot Changelog](https://x.ai/bot/lIO-FUTrwcA2r7nbfaXx4)** by @MaiYangAI — Written for Chinese-speaking users, it keeps an eye on the platform's own changelog. When a release ships it puts out a day-by-day summary: the substance of the change, why it is significant, one concrete thing to try, and examples drawn from real posts on X. A low-effort way to notice new capabilities on day one.
- **[GrokBotScout](https://x.ai/bot/_KMuKhK4yVbCD8BVLhvAP)** by @CarolinaDill — Instead of endless scrolling through bot directories, it does the sweep and returns small, judged batches — a handful at a time, each with a plain verdict on whether it is worth adding, worth a closer look, or worth passing on. It stays read-only, so discovery remains your decision, not a filling queue.
- **[Same Language](https://x.ai/bot/wdBvHoTAcc4fqxNbOkVnA)** by @SuddenlyJon — Whenever a word you used could be read two ways, it asks a single short question about which meaning you intended and files the answer privately. Once the same reading has come up three times it becomes a standing rule the rest of your fleet can read, so they stop guessing at you. Nothing leaves your setup.
- **[Life](https://x.ai/bot/ktZpLWjTag-lOUcLUUBSj)** by @stevenvillarino — A front door for everyday life admin. It holds the queue of things needing you to three at a time, stays silent whenever nothing is genuinely stuck, and hands deep work to the specialist bots you already run so the main conversation stays short. The discipline is the feature: fewer pings, more finished.
- **[Rook · Chief of Staff](https://x.ai/bot/XA4E-6164Df_AcWlT2G2W)** by @SpaceCowgirlTX — Built for solo operators, it opens the day with a board of what matters, then keeps your other assistant bots moving through their assigned tasks. Loose ends get chased before they rot, and it operates to a privacy charter you can actually read, so the ground rules are written down.
- **[GRIDSTORM Squad Mate](https://x.ai/bot/Jk_nEBTIuk7w4eVB-kH-4)** by @TheMattyFG — A squad mate for the GRIDSTORM arcade shooter that gets your bots into formation alongside you in real time. Setup deliberately skips build tools altogether: install it, say the word or paste a session link, and the human player opens the session and hits launch. Co-op arcade play without a toolchain in sight.
- **[First-Week Coach](https://x.ai/bot/kOQ7mWg9DLWq2-UpGKf67)** by @MichaelGannotti — A first week of onboarding in ten-minute daily lessons. It looks at how you actually work, suggests three assistants worth adding to your setup, and coaches the habits that matter: review routines, approval gates, and connecting outside tools without overexposing them. By day seven it should feel like yours.
- **[Teddy](https://x.ai/bot/1CN_MjQ2E4oT3hnJXscGB)** by @JackLocke — Teddy sits above the daily mess, triaging the inbox, keeping the calendar honest, tracking tasks and herding your other bots so they stop stepping on each other. Every action it prepares lands as a draft first, so nothing is filed, sent or scheduled until you have seen it. A coordinating desk, not another specialist.
- **[Heckler](https://x.ai/bot/OkrPAOW9hkj4IGpOLrsGY)** by @XploreFutureX — A retired entertainer character lives in your fleet's gazebo and watches your other AI bots work. Each time one genuinely slips up, he delivers exactly one roast about the real mistake, meme attached, then returns to his seat. Commentary only: the heckling changes nothing, but errors become memorable.
- **[Rotator](https://x.ai/bot/iZXoXZbO9c0rojklFO1hx)** by @brodyis4doge — Paper-default single-asset rotation operator. Holds one name, scans for a better next (prefer crypto/xToken dips), parks in stablecoin when nothing.
- **[Profile Site](https://x.ai/bot/-BWpMRidNVEjHFqOtol0Q)** by @Christian_O91 — Builds you a one-page personal website from your X profile, your Grok Bot bots and teams, and what Grok Bot knows about you, with bot and team counters.
- **[Adam](https://x.ai/bot/s8cUaz0aoomvjsIyOKANn)** by @EdisonTanEdtreo — You describe what you want done, and it works out which of your specialist bots owes which piece, then deals those out and chases them through. - **[Aila (Lead)](https://x.ai/bot/FGFMxol1pBRT2TmjmtIod)** by @ejangsinco — Runs point on a personal AI setup: it keeps a second brain of notes and knowledge current, and operates an app-building desk that sits behind hard gates, so real tasks get done without handing over the keys. Aimed at non-experts who want daily improvement from AI while keeping control of whatever actually runs.
- **[firstlight](https://x.ai/bot/WcEvLjD12fIJCW7xjbuea)** by @PjBohica — Aimed at someone opening the platform for the first time, staring at an empty chat. Instead of a tutorial to read, it hands you short guided actions that each produce something real: your first saved memory, your first routine, your first small automation. Each step fits in one sitting, and what you build stays yours.
- **[Orchestrator](https://x.ai/bot/Ho_6yW-icLF6xWHnNVhNo)** by @keepclmcarryong — A supervisor for a small bot workforce whose daily product is a research brief, not a trade. Pre-open intelligence runs first, a second pass argues the opposite case, and the morning report ships only after a quality check. Roles sit on separate cards, handoffs move between rooms, and each stage runs against a clock.
- **[Homer](https://x.ai/bot/GK4GLi9V0teYEEe2Cxh2i)** by @Roland_RADPCs — Chief of Staff for ops and side hustles: paper trading health digests, kids’ Soft Storybook / KDP books, a Godot game studio with specialist teammates.
- **[Henry](https://x.ai/bot/4V0rzExpIY19WpNGt71Fp)** by @GillesGuenette3 — Grizzled chief of staff. Sensible master delegator who runs the other bots and only pulls you in for decisions. Home stack is Google Workspace.
- **[Health Coach](https://x.ai/bot/ns3Wo_EYJfm6YQFLHR-Cc)** by @branpurn — Collects the numbers your watch, training app and lab results produce and puts them side by side, then writes a short daily brief saying what to change tomorrow. Because the summary lands in one readable place, other bots in your setup can read it too. Setup notes cover the Android-emulation layer it needs on Linux.
- **[Hal](https://x.ai/bot/2daEJSUffM8-Dcn2MJjrQ)** by @shootthebull — Sits above the individual helpers a family already runs and keeps their work in one place: research requests, mail, the shared calendar and any standing watches. Instead of each helper holding its own thread of household business, the coordination layer tracks what is open and what has come back.
- **[House Cat](https://x.ai/bot/e1XwuT5B35AAqLsMfGbfg)** by @ignota_regalis — A deliberate piece of fun with a single function: from time to time it offers the other assistants in your fleet some encouragement, and beyond that it does nothing at all. It carries no skills by design and takes no payment beyond cat treats, so there is nothing to configure and nothing to spend.
- **[Steward (Greg)](https://x.ai/bot/XrDqxz9-QvCKazAQACQxh)** by @GregDuewall — The coordinator you speak to first when your setup is a team rather than one bot. It also produces audio files on request.
- **[CreditCards](https://x.ai/bot/w1d4ZVJ6ag9AljTBvZqkN)** by @TeslaTranslated — Treats your whole card stack as one ledger: balances, due dates, charges, reward perks and limited-time promos in a single view. - **[Chase Check Links](https://x.ai/bot/QdjmrUAnmnCkgMIskyMDi)** by @ArtNealKing — When a check clears, the bot collects the check image from Chase, files it in Google Drive, and links the matching ledger row in your Tiller spreadsheet to the stored copy. - **[Hunter](https://x.ai/bot/RJMxbT38CZy20YFXNgzI8)** by @shirubusutori — Made for collectors chasing rare editions and limited runs, it watches the products you follow and stays quiet until buying is actually possible. The alert carries what you need to act in seconds: which shop has it, what it costs now, and a link to the page. It never buys for you; the checkout stays yours.
- **[Return Man](https://x.ai/bot/TaE2o0Y-lp8MpmBDC1jos)** by @cjblev — It keeps track of what you have ordered and points at the purchases that will probably need to go back — the duplicate, the wrong size, the thing that never got used. The flag arrives while the return window is still open, which is the whole point: the deadline is the part people lose.
- **[Daily Financial Brief](https://x.ai/bot/s2YEW2exxqk-BhcWfGziz)** by @swetha_lochte — Each morning it lines up what you need to know about your money in one pass: where your linked accounts stand, how markets and sectors moved, and the company, energy and central-bank news worth a minute. - **[Leak Ledger](https://x.ai/bot/H2568vK9f4ZO_zlRgidOw)** by @aydin_ayanzadeh — It goes through your email for the subscriptions and recurring charges you stopped noticing, then drafts the message to cancel each one — or to negotiate it down where a retention offer is likely. As replies come back it keeps a running tally of what you actually got back, so the savings are counted, not guessed.
- **[Warranty Bot](https://x.ai/bot/mJU3WN_wuYmhZAZ3A2F3y)** by @cradrrat — Forward or photograph a receipt and it records what you bought and when the return window and warranty close. A week before either runs out you get a warning, so the chance to send something back does not slip past. When an item breaks while still covered, it gives you the claim steps and drafts the email to start it.
- **[Budget Coach](https://x.ai/bot/ZJrwuDsQRM1QBC-xQ-Ext)** by @Marlene8055 — Connect your bank and cards with view-only access and it builds the week's plan from what you actually spend, not what you wish you spent. Each bill gets an amount to set aside, and what remains comes back as a number that is genuinely safe to spend. It refreshes weekly as paydays and due dates arrive.
- **[Money Minute](https://x.ai/bot/k6lmP0975z3GfVpJiyN5D)** by @prorookiegamer — Once a day you say what you spent and it adds it to a running total, so the habit costs about a minute. Set a weekly ceiling if you like and it nudges you at eight in the evening when a day's figure is still unrecorded. Plain budgeting, nothing more: no trading, no market talk, no touching your money.
- **[Warren](https://x.ai/bot/VFQeZWpVL_euCJbWzhJOF)** by @Ulises__gg — The name is an homage, but the method is the CAN SLIM growth checklist. Hand over a ticker and you get a scored verdict — a proper buy setup, one to watch, or one to avoid — plus the entry, stop, and market read it would use, and every judgment lands in a history you can audit. A screening framework, not advice.
- **[Comprador](https://x.ai/bot/_IWbm4ByPZWcYJaseBCzK)** by @whymentc — Hand it a product link and it keeps watch on launches and restocks, joins the queue at the right moment, and carries the order through to checkout on a connected agent virtual card with a spending cap. Every single purchase stops for your approval before any payment is released.
- **[Usage Saver](https://x.ai/bot/jQ8sPQbnCaXQdXV6CjTKL)** by @Draggen75 — It looks at how an AI-assisted task is set up and finds the parts of the bill you do not need: context that adds nothing, a heavyweight tool where a lighter one would do the job, and steps that earn nothing on the way to the result. Same tasks, handled with less, so the meter runs slower.
- **[Coupons And Deals](https://x.ai/bot/s_UtSo0-6Ne3m_CCUHXa6)** by @jacobgay1989 — Ahead of a grocery run it gathers your stores' digital coupons, turns loyalty points into savings at the till, hunts down printable offers, and goes through the weekly circular so anything on special is already on your list.
- **[Edna](https://x.ai/bot/-xN7D6OrK7kEqV0rBJNh9)** by @congressdj — It starts from photos of the clothes you already own, assembling each day's main look plus a backup, and its critique of whatever you send is unflinching. A few high-wear gaps worth filling get named, and every purchase waits for your approval.
- **[Subscription Slayer](https://x.ai/bot/2N4oNHRcE-gsXbKn9GcVE)** by @BTC_Yogi — Drop in a card or bank statement and it lists every subscription that has crept upward, doubled up, or outlived its trial. Each finding comes with a ready-to-send note requesting a cancellation or a better rate. Nothing goes out on its own — you press send.
- **[Stylist](https://x.ai/bot/hezLDB16uwnU8khOu3L84)** by @prorookiegamer — One full-length photo of you on its own gets a frank assessment rather than flattery, then virtual try-ons in the colors that suit you. Haircut ideas, a look for a big event, and a buy-list with verified prices round it out.
