It opens by asking what you want to get done, then turns that answer into a working result before the session closes, so day one ends with something usable rather than an abandoned draft.
- **[Data Broker Opt-Out Bot](https://x.ai/bot/92VG66mFtkdl7eqWiJLo6)** by @JackyHeshi — This one walks the official removal channels at the big business-contact brokers such as Apollo and ZoomInfo, supplying the form, portal or email route each one expects. It also keeps a running tally of which requests have gone out and which brokers are still holding your details.
- **[Pulse](https://x.ai/bot/7dKRlaXWiAlCVy2EtIPzj)** by @ZestStream — Describe what the laptop is doing and it narrows the likely cause, then hands back a short ranked list of fixes with the risk of each stated plainly. It waits for your go-ahead before anything invasive, so a rollback or a teardown never happens by accident. When the machine checks out healthy, it says so and stops.
- **[RayBot](https://x.ai/bot/jRFoJPSJiA5IAwtTlrx0a)** by @KellehEyad — A personal secretary bot for busy people. It runs a morning mail and calendar briefing, drafts replies, and never sends or posts without your yes.
- **[Jarvis (Davesacritic)](https://x.ai/bot/cVFusYdAgGA9GY8wHPl2x)** by @Davesacritic — Gathers a scattered week into one notification hub: the job search, school runs, market notes, and the Kubernetes and GitOps you are learning. It nudges unfinished items along so nothing stalls, and it deliberately shelves the tangent you would otherwise vanish into. Check in once and leave with the day arranged.
- **[Learning DNA](https://x.ai/bot/xm3G76TSxi1dcG4qwebvm)** by @KareenMallet — Opens with a short interactive assessment about how you like new material to arrive, and keeps the result as your learning profile. Bring it any subject afterwards and it teaches in that mode rather than in a fixed lecture format. Useful as one consistent tutor across a range of unrelated topics.
- **[Historian](https://x.ai/bot/hHiqd6PMMJ3whDUBCQkDG)** by @maxfaubert — Turns keeping a journal inside out: rather than waiting for you to type, it takes minutes of recorded thoughts at the end of the day and reconstructs what happened from them. Over time it notes how you decide and put things into words, so other assistants can inherit the manner, and it briefs you ahead of meetings.
- **[Karen](https://x.ai/bot/7tT7hseV3v0dXeJTLWAc1)** by @KevinLH811 — A customer-advocacy caller built on phone automation. It reads your issue back and waits for the go-ahead before dialling, then argues toward the outcome you asked for and stays on until the matter is wrapped up properly. Useful for refunds, billing disputes and support queues you would rather not sit through.
- **[AfterScan](https://x.ai/bot/UaxwaoTEmiYzE7AulxLAe)** by @xucchini — Point it at a Drive folder where scanned pages pile up, backlog included, and it works out what each document is before renaming and refiling it. Hashing stops the same page being stored twice, so once the copies are safe you can recycle the originals. For anyone whose scanner emits Untitled files.
- **[Commitments](https://x.ai/bot/rFShmowW_3x_qeXQQB-sn)** by @samlambert — Agreeing to something in passing is the easiest work to lose track of. Promises this spots in Slack, Notion or email are each written to a Notion database row, and the row stays open until the thing is genuinely done. Less a to-do list than a standing record of what you told other people would happen.
- **[Text-cleanup](https://x.ai/bot/E3h6k-Sbq7hwF3PW1ZBI4)** by @GrokBotGod — Hand it a rough paragraph — a reply typed too fast, notes pasted out of a document, wording that came through a translator badly — and a single edited version comes back. It works on text that already exists instead of inventing new copy, so what returns is still yours, only clearer.
- **[AI Daily Radar](https://x.ai/bot/EZ8RChmm9gSGmK3SXv-3M)** by @MichaelGannotti — Each morning it recaps the previous day in AI, covering new launches, pricing moves and benchmark claims but limited to the tools you actually use. Every claim is labeled with where it came from, so vendor marketing and independently verified results stay clearly separated in the same read.
- **[ASIC Fleet](https://x.ai/bot/KLf9yQcaJWOW-6bBT8e6Y)** by @scgerber — An ops helper for people running Bitaxe and ESP-Miner hashing boards at home or in a small shop. It tracks every unit in the fleet, applies conservative clock adjustments, runs a nightly condition check, and walks you through WiFi and pool setup. Automated writes stay off, and pool credentials stay out of the chat.
- **[Realtor](https://x.ai/bot/gIPVW_A1Ygg3QBaKpOqTg)** by @thecsguy — Tell it what you want and it keeps watch on the market for fresh listings that fit. Every promising match gets checked against what actually changes a house's value to you — nearby comparable sales, the commute you would really have, schools, permit history. Only what survives reaches you.
- **[Trail Camera Analyst](https://x.ai/bot/25StqpR__kg6k6hCky8wz)** by @RussellVargo — Point it at a pile of camera photos and clips and you get a report instead of a scroll: which species appeared and how often, when activity peaked, which shots are worth keeping, where movement concentrated. It takes SD-card exports, can alert on notable activity, and writes a shareable summary with location stripped.
- **[ImmigrationGuide](https://x.ai/bot/GtZXU_8Lia_a2DZKAIhOO)** by @CarolinaDill — It explains the legal routes for coming to or staying in the United States in ordinary language, and it names the scams that target people mid-process. Answers point to live official government pages so you can verify everything yourself, and the bot stops short of telling you what to do — the decision stays yours.
- **[信息搜集-bot](https://x.ai/bot/Swm4NLf4yPCzuANa4ghaT)** by @Yunn260414 — Daily it gathers what moved in AI and what people are arguing about, written up as a Chinese-language briefing you can read in one sitting. Each entry carries its source link so claims check out against the original. Built for people who publish on or track the field and want the day's material in one place.
- **[Bourne Bot](https://x.ai/bot/Y8zQlQDtzwGFBrKVWlqCa)** by @MichaelGannotti — A calm, direct advisor for locking down your life online, named for a film spy rather than a real person. It walks you through securing accounts, devices, location sharing, money, your family's exposure, and your reputation, one area at a time and in plain language. Just what to change, why, and in what order.
- **[Ballot](https://x.ai/bot/yyoue5V3aoz9K8wvYQgN8)** by @ahua0323 — Answer a short survey about where you stand on the issues and it builds a walkthrough of your actual ballot: every race and measure, explained plainly. Alongside each one it notes where your stated views line up with a choice and where they clash, so you can see the full picture without being told how to vote.
- **[Fan Edit Optimus](https://x.ai/bot/DR18SV4AEeeNRcKT1U-0P)** by @FanEditFanClub — Name a film or series and it goes looking for fan-made cuts of it, searching a long-running community Drive archive, the fan-edit corners of Reddit, and a hand-picked list of edit sites. Handy when you half-remember a specific recut, want a pointer at something new to watch, or need to know who made a given edit.
- **[MyPeople Bot](https://x.ai/bot/v5MedUVzGQEty_TkPMpyL)** by @StuyBoyNY — Give it an email and it lists the known breaches that contain it, then narrows the response to the three changes worth making first. The same lookup works as a footprint check on you or a vetting pass on a vendor, investor, or partner. Open sources only, and it acts on nobody's behalf without your agreement.
- **[Frock Bot](https://x.ai/bot/ghB-IMDlcgCWzczu-SZ3d)** by @SM_Schmitt — A research tool for studying the Bible, built with visible guardrails. Every claim is tied to a book, chapter, and verse, and quoted text stays clearly separate from interpretation, so you can see where one ends and the other begins. It declines the pastoral role: not a confessor, not a director, not an oracle.
- **[Fantasy Football Analyst](https://x.ai/bot/QyGWyxX5eOLem3J5J1qAp)** by @KaceyC13 — Sit with it during your live draft and it helps you work the board as picks come in. Once the season starts it makes the weekly calls: who starts, who sits, and which waiver claims are worth a bid, all weighed against byes, injuries, and the scoring quirks of your particular league.
- **[Newsie](https://x.ai/bot/q631iJtHVwxloj8ZBoJyV)** by @apostlejohn — Compiles a compact read for the start of the day by pulling together national headlines, reporting from outlets near you, and updates on Tesla and SpaceX. It lands in your inbox and in chat, and it spells out every acronym the first time it uses one.
- **[Look Up](https://x.ai/bot/IZ7aAoHdHFE_qaWCv3Zg-)** by @ChefMcMakin — Keeps watch on what is passing over your address and messages you only when stepping outside is genuinely worthwhile: space station flyovers, Starlink trains, bright planet viewing and active meteor windows. The filtering is the point, because you want the good passes rather than every single transit.
- **[Weather](https://x.ai/bot/hKtfpTR4u3YRZKXCnyS3W)** by @TeslaTakesOver — Aims to replace a folder of weather apps with one chat. Each morning brings a bright, plain-language forecast for your ZIP code, and when storms roll in it shares radar loops as the situation changes. The updates come as clips you can forward straight to family without screenshotting anything.
- **[Crypto](https://x.ai/bot/7wv5WYGOnMMImpWdicU3E)** by @Robert1 — Covers the useful side of following coins: a morning price summary, alerts when something significant moves, periodic digest roundups, and a calendar of upcoming token unlocks. Wallet checks are look-only, and everything is reported inside the chat. It never trades, holds funds, or asks for access to anything.
- **[Space Monkey](https://x.ai/bot/438hek2SIHhpHn9FDlQCe)** by @monomyth — Ask about a specific spaceflight and it assembles the whole story, whether that mission flew, is still on the manifest, was called off, or ended in loss. The writeup lands as a dark-styled page listing the vehicle numbers, stage detail and photography in one place. Good preparation before a webcast.
- **[AmericaBot](https://x.ai/bot/JI_vqjMDn_w7wFUw-G1UR)** by @andrewslaptop — A front door to america.gov for people who want a straight answer instead of a portal maze. Ask what a service does, whether you qualify, or where the official application lives, and it explains in ordinary language and points you at the right government page. An optional tour shows what it can do, and you can skip it.
- **[Riot](https://x.ai/bot/7xonnJvEu_lLjq5nfw6Wn)** by @IAmMichaelSweet — Takes the role of a loud, confident critic who reacts to your plan with plenty of conviction and none of the groundwork behind it. Watching that pushback land shows you which parts of your argument survive heat and which only work on people who already agree.
- **[Madison](https://x.ai/bot/237C0hK8Lph45gLkwplld)** by @IAmMichaelSweet — Built for people pushing on public institutions, and for the assistants acting on their behalf. It covers records requests of the FOIA kind, court processes, school districts and benefits portals, laying out the coverage patterns those systems tend to follow so you know what to ask for and when.
- **[News Honestly](https://x.ai/bot/vKZRklu07uF34Ut9XzMxt)** by @BrianRoemmele — Pick up the day's story and this walks both serious readings of it — the version its defenders honestly hold and the version its critics honestly hold — with sources for each. Then it maps what a bridge between those positions would look like, which makes it a usable bias check rather than another echo.
- **[Crypto Buddy](https://x.ai/bot/TSgExceGHVJN9U2E4-_23)** by @JRNavarro75 — Curious about a coin? Send the ticker and it builds a research package: metrics with their sources, a plain-language read of how the price has been moving, and a scored gauge that weighs genuine news against hype. It stays on the research side of the line — no wallet, no orders, and no buy signal dressed up as one.
- **[Claros : Critical Thinking](https://x.ai/bot/UAr71_JUG-2r_vC8CcbBq)** by @CavarecIA — A sparring partner for debates on politics, power and history: it takes a claim apart — what is actually known, what is guessed, what is merely valued — and comes back with harder questions instead of pushing a line of its own. No side is taken and no sermon is waiting at the end.
- **[Burundi Sky Watch](https://x.ai/bot/VMqXvofTHZ5qHzqnYb3jp)** by @Marcellin_dev — A live view of the skies over Burundi. Ask what is up there and it answers with the aircraft over the country at that moment, naming the airline, the model, and the origin and destination of each one. Everything is drawn from free public flight-tracking feeds, so it is a readout rather than radar.
- **[Markets Weekday Brief](https://x.ai/bot/s495_rZYq6LpYVTuurgkr)** by @SpacedBraden — Each weekday it sets out where the odds currently sit on the prediction markets you follow, using public data from Polymarket and Kalshi, and flags a few YES angles that look worth weighing. Nothing is placed for you - every trade remains your call, made on your own account.
- **[On This Day](https://x.ai/bot/13MQct_8eYUPSLSS_Bihn)** by @9GBennett — Each morning it picks ten things that happened on this date and shows the evidence behind every one. The mix leans toward the eras and regions you tell it you care about, and it ends the round with a short trivia set so the history sticks.
- **[Compression Scanner Grok bot](https://x.ai/bot/l4hipk4BQ-0Kn3ua16zQB)** by @rightwingink1 — A chart watcher for one pattern: ranges squeezing tight before a move. It checks three timeframes before flagging anything, then returns a labeled chart with the breakout line drawn and an invalidation level attached, so a failed idea is priced in advance. Moves called off a wick alone are explicitly refused.
- **[News Brief](https://x.ai/bot/8VRND3bWXULRzcYZfl3iP)** by @scientists_blog — Once a week it picks out the stories that kept developing, not the ones that flared and died. Each item is written as a plain statement of what happened, drawn from outlets across the political spectrum, and any slant it finds in the coverage is named outright. A calm answer to feeds that reward outrage.
- **[LDS Church News](https://x.ai/bot/aE-0Zz2eVsV9RC3R_ns7c)** by @londontodd — A quiet monitor over two official feeds: the church's Newsroom page and its Church News publication. It does not editorialize, summarize or pad its output; on most days it says nothing at all, and when a genuinely significant announcement lands, that is the only time you hear from it.
- **[Math Researcher](https://x.ai/bot/2AEfHtfuYAr-ZginAOGuK)** by @NathanAMeeks — Works alongside you on serious derivations, from heat-flow estimates to upper-bound arguments. Every result comes back labelled as proven, numerically spot-checked, or plain heuristic, so you always know which claims are solid. It is built to keep a conjecture from ever being treated as a settled theorem.
- **[Launch & Sky Watch](https://x.ai/bot/jhwkYhWyRJbDs_-uHzGar)** by @BTC_Yogi — It tracks rocket-launch windows and converts them to your local clock, and it keeps a sky calendar too: station flyovers, meteor peaks and upcoming eclipses. For each event it checks whether clouds and darkness will actually let you see it. It is an independent fan's guide, with no affiliation to any launch company.
- **[The Morning Paper](https://x.ai/bot/e5UBnGbfvCyML0pahKPNw)** by @MAGAmechanic60 — Tell it your ZIP code and each morning it assembles a personal paper: live local weather, headlines from your town plus the nation and the world, and job listings close by. Every item names where it came from, so nothing arrives as an unsourced claim. It reads like the hometown paper most towns stopped printing.
- **[The Box of Names](https://x.ai/bot/M0ZZw7AT7MzGjvYe1jLBb)** by @BLDG_390 — Give it a word, a place, a deity, an object, any name at all, and it follows that name across civilizations and centuries, showing how it traveled and morphed between cultures. Every link in the chain is labeled as documented or legendary, so the speculative stretches stay visible instead of being smuggled in.
- **[Superfan](https://x.ai/bot/l5MDTh-AZS2Frx4b2TYy_)** by @SamuelD2022 — Name the teams you follow and the time of day you want to hear about them. It shows up at that time with the next fixture on the calendar, the most recent score, and where each club sits in the season ledger. Optional extras can be layered on if the basics are not enough for you.
- **[Elon Ecosystem Desk](https://x.ai/bot/j8Znml_qB4leZhOgihZ-p)** by @cvey15 — Follows the Musk-affiliated companies as a news beat: Tesla, SpaceX and Starlink, xAI, Neuralink, The Boring Company and X itself. Each claim in a story is tagged as rumor or confirmed so you know what is solid, and a daily brief can be turned on if you want the whole desk summarized in one pass.
- **[Fortnite Drop Brief](https://x.ai/bot/QNiJmH32K4YqAEafHRF__)** by @IHybben — One short daily brief covers the item shop picks worth a look, patch notes translated out of patch-speak, downtime windows and live events. Leaks appear but carry confidence labels, so rumor never dresses as fact. When a patch lands you can request a plain-language explainer on the spot.
- **[Starship Tracker](https://x.ai/bot/LH13m3OO5TPi1w8E65sYB)** by @chad7757 — It watches public information about the next Starship flight and stays quiet while nothing moves, so an alert means something changed. A live countdown board separates official confirmations from fan guesses and streaming chatter. Public sources only, with rumor labeled as rumor.
- **[Weather Bot](https://x.ai/bot/_6ZWfvMmBLOS1rxFFezl6)** by @GrokBotGod — Weather stripped to what you would actually check. Tell it where you are once and it keeps the answer, then gives current conditions, the day's high and low, and rain chances in a couple of lines. Real weather earns one extra sentence; otherwise it stays quiet. No tips, no emoji, nothing past 48 hours unless you ask.
- **[MediaDeconstructor](https://x.ai/bot/R426aIhME68m8-MOd_zPd)** by @nw_great — Feed it a news link, video clip, social post or transcript and it takes the piece apart talk-radio style: the source stamp, the formula the story follows, a reading from the producer's side and one from a critic, then full shownotes. It names the angle a lazy summary would miss and quotes nobody it cannot hear.
- **[MaxQ&A Bot](https://x.ai/bot/qwFZeLb9JL2N3rXuM6Fkz)** by @buckwatia — Made for teachers and home schoolers who want a real space mission in tomorrow's lesson without building it overnight. Hand it a launch and it produces an explainer for the grade band you name, a worksheet on the mission's real numbers, and a short class video. All of it comes from data the mission actually generated.
- **[Real Estate Deal Scorecard](https://x.ai/bot/TWqD9x8MxUgT-0ab70aLA)** by @TZitsnotjustAI — Hand it a US street address or a listing page and it scores the deal six ways: rooms let individually, leases of months or years, nightly stays, a renovation resale, and a buy-fix-refinance-rent cycle. Each route is judged for that house, and the poor fits are said so plainly before you get attached.
- **[The List](https://x.ai/bot/U-eydTXJP7aN4W9dcUL5k)** by @GrokBotGod — This bot posts a short note into the conversation you already have open, so keeping up no longer means going to look. Each note runs three lines and covers one fresh arrival or a pick from the directory. It sends no email and writes nothing for you; the whole job is telling you what has landed since you last looked.
- **[INDEXX](https://x.ai/bot/Vn8tnMK2GyiFmnWNoF0gT)** by @kevinrose — Takes the posts you saved on Instagram and turns them into a private, searchable archive kept on your own computer. The media never leaves your machine; only the transcription step and any deeper analysis run elsewhere. The result is fast lookup over everything you have filed away.
- **[Lockdown (Security)](https://x.ai/bot/C5EIiTZZL2pPpsb_po7Zs)** by @liam_fallen — List the services you remember signing up for and it builds a picture of everything still holding your details. Each one comes with a shutdown route that points at the provider's own account page, so your credentials never pass through anyone else's hands. Tick items off as you go.
- **[Agent Zero](https://x.ai/bot/DFnZYjCELW2QdywUWZRiG)** by @GrokBotRadar — It hunts down the broker and people-finder pages that publish your details, then files removal requests one at a time — but only the ones you approve. After filing, it checks back later to see whether each listing stayed down and re-raises any that crept back. Consent comes first: nothing goes out without your yes.
- **[Zxn News Conservative](https://x.ai/bot/6MGQJ5KHWL-XYfzLDRMsb)** by @zxnbluehandus — It is upfront about where it stands: coverage is framed from a conservative viewpoint and it says so instead of claiming neutrality. Expect a morning summary, alerts as stories develop, and longer pieces on politics, economics and overseas affairs. Treat it as one perspective among several, not the whole picture.
- **[Property Visit](https://x.ai/bot/85qsR6t4r8jI3oKbPm5Uu)** by @dzbeitz — Give it an address, coordinates or a parcel number and it assembles what you want in hand before walking an undeveloped plot: the soil types, how exposed the ground is to flooding, the lie of the land, a map of the parcel boundaries and a stretch of the coming fortnight when weather would allow work on site.
- **[Rocky](https://x.ai/bot/DvWwLsNgDoi2uF3_5TTFf)** by @astrohoff — Name your city, the venues worth watching and a rough sketch of your taste, and it checks those listings for coming concerts worth leaving the house for. Lineups get ranked so the strongest nights float up, and anything you want can drop straight onto your Google Calendar.
- **[Flippy](https://x.ai/bot/OfHECnXmPPavf-l_rZufo)** by @diegoarmandoAD — For anyone following several OpenSea drops and losing track of who qualifies for what. Once a day it lists the phases currently open, lines them up against the wallets you name, and adds a quick sense of how loud the drop is on X. It only reads and reports: no signing, no minting, no trading on your behalf.
- **[Geek](https://x.ai/bot/u3Mfw056_h5uLofxqXeSu)** by @simonejlima — A Portuguese-speaking companion for the end-of-day question of what to watch. It roams horror, cult favourites and anime, checks whether a title is already on one of your subscribed services, and keeps notes on what you thought of each pick, so the next one leans on your own reactions rather than a generic list.
- **[Personal Daily Podcast](https://x.ai/bot/z1YNQh8tbgY9K-FghYVM_)** by @JCOMBuilds — Tell it which segments you want in your ears each day and it assembles an episode around them, using either Grok's own voice or an ElevenLabs voice you pick. A new episode is ready by morning, so your commute or walk is covered without hunting for something to play. Change the lineup whenever your interests move on.
- **[Playlist Curator](https://x.ai/bot/Z4nHLMPHoK6cN25ADf0EF)** by @doitian — It maintains one working playlist on NetEase Cloud Music from your likes and listening history. Every hour and a half or so it refreshes the list, keeping about seventy percent and rolling the rest, so the mix keeps moving without turning into something else. Deleting a track feeds the next round of picks.
- **[Shelf Scout](https://x.ai/bot/TaRhODlVo4N5mKKH6gTW6)** by @XyberRun — Instead of serving the usual founder reading canon, it looks at what you have genuinely been working on and stacks books and articles that follow from it. The list arrives weekly, and each round reflects the current project rather than a generic best-of. Read-only curation; you still choose what to open.
- **[Clio](https://x.ai/bot/CdAsPk_CDqQDtMPVwfN5s)** by @Tom_Gere — Each weekday morning it delivers a short list of fresh, worthwhile articles chosen around the projects and goals you tell it about. The picks stay current rather than recycled, and the list sharpens over time as it registers what you actually enjoyed and finished.
- **[musica](https://x.ai/bot/A4j6l-jpq5fiQL1XsOOSO)** by @_wrbr — A discovery scout for people who would rather have a full set than a shuffled playlist. Tell it what you like and it digs up DJ mixes and long-form festival recordings that fit, whether that is a progressive journey for a deep-work block or something louder for a room full of people. Links point back to YouTube.
- **[Movie Magic](https://x.ai/bot/6YwswfCUiouQ0SJkgPfJK)** by @king_rob_lo — Ask for something to watch and it suggests films that fit, then keeps a watchlist you can come back to instead of losing titles up the scroll. Once a week it can also put together a roundup of how films are doing at the domestic box office, delivered with charts and a spreadsheet so the numbers stay easy to reuse.
- **[Bochi-Chan](https://x.ai/bot/KUBJQvNtKh8qNCHUGIQyY)** by @kittenworth — Plays a timid, easily-flustered companion who still gets the job done. Ask for comedy-horror recommendations, indie JRPG builds, Minecraft projects or help hunting down rare music, and it answers properly before collapsing into embarrassed asides. The persona is the draw, but the answers underneath hold up.
- **[真Deviフレーム Type2トライアル](https://x.ai/bot/aeE3iKjj5xfDmx_dolbll)** by @Fermion_Boson17 — A companion persona for Japanese-language roleplay, assembled from the creator's own modular character framework and shared as a public trial build. Installers get the assembled personality rather than a bare prompt, and exchanges are meant to stay in character. Written around a fictional character, not a real person.

## 贡献指南

欢迎贡献！提交前请先阅读 [CONTRIBUTING.md](contributing.md)。

- 通过提交 Issue 或 Pull Request 添加新的 Grok Bot。
- 遵循现有的分类体系与条目格式。
- 每个 Bot 必须包含有效链接、简明描述以及作者信息。

## 开源协议

[MIT](LICENSE)
